  *Symptom:* Write templates, the Homework + button, and AI features all did nothing even though the code was correct.  <br>*Cause:* The deployed app was serving stale JavaScript from the service worker cache.  <br>*Fix:* Fixed the service worker so every deploy arrives instantly (with a one-time hard-refresh to clear the old bundle).

## Hallalu CRM  ·  160 fixed

- [ ] **AI, vision, TTS and translate proxy endpoints answered without any session (billable to the owner's Claude and Google keys)** `security`
  *Symptom:* curl -X POST /api/ai and /api/translate returned results anonymously; Studio TTS costs $160 per extra million characters and the 1M free allowance is per Google project, not per customer  <br>*Cause:* first-wave worker routes had no auth or quota  <br>*Fix:* per-account TTS character budget added (6k free / 20k Plus / 60k Pro / 150k Business) with an in-app meter; OPEN: confirm /api/ai, /api/vision and /api/translate are session-gated and metered (AI credits queued)
- [ ] **Business-plan /pitch gate bypassable via path tricks** `security`
  *Symptom:* The gated /pitch content could be reached with two path tricks despite the gate.  <br>*Cause:* The gate only guarded the route while the protected content still shipped in the static asset bundle.  <br>*Fix:* Removed the protected content from the asset bundle entirely so the gate actually holds.
- [ ] **Client work portal auto-included internal follow-up reminders and private message notes in the client-facing snapshot** `security`
  *Symptom:* clientTimeline() assembled the timeline from calendar events, so private CRM cadence ('Follow up Maya - Friendly nudge', 'Gentle check-in') and candid comms notes were headed to the client. After the timeline was filtered, decoding the share-link JSON still showed the internal reminder label in the 'next' KPI.  <br>*Cause:* The client-facing artefact pulled from every source by default and stripped by prefix rather than allowing known-safe kinds; nextStep() only removed the 'Follow up' prefix.  <br>*Fix:* Events are tagged internal:true / msg:true; portalSnap always drops internal items, messages sit behind an 'Include messages' send toggle, and next is populated only from real meetings or bookings. The owner view shows internal rows faded with 'Private reminder - the client never sees this'. Verified by decoding the snapshot: 7 events, 3 internal, 4 shown.
- [ ] **Cross-account data leak: shared localStorage cache not cleared on sign-out** `security`
  *Symptom:* On a shared device, the next person to sign in loaded the previous user's leads and invoices from cache  <br>*Cause:* State is cached under a single global key 'hallalu_v2' (not owner-scoped), and signOut() called save() — re-writing the departing user's data back into that key and never clearing it  <br>*Fix:* Added wipeLocal() and call it on sign-out and on the ephemeral 'don't keep me signed in' boot path; verified the cached account data is cleared
- [ ] **Demo mode could spend real AI: askAI was guarded but /api/vision callers are not (OPEN)** `security`
  *Symptom:* The demo called the real AI endpoint (cost and abuse). askAI got a demo guard with canned replies. /api/vision is called directly by app.js:1028, comms.js:175, clientcards.js:186 and import.js:178; only avaSendMedia was guarded, and no later fix appears.  <br>*Cause:* The guard sat inside askAI; vision requests bypass it. The server only applies an anonymous per-IP daily limit (about 30 a day).  <br>*Fix:* askAI: `if (S.demo) return {error:'demo'}` plus canned worked examples. Not fixed: put the same guard in the shared fetch wrapper for /api/vision.
- [ ] **Google API key sent as ?key= query string to Cloud TTS (leaks into logs and URLs)** `security`
  *Symptom:* Worker called texttospeech.googleapis.com/v1/text:synthesize?key=<secret>  <br>*Cause:* key placed in the URL instead of a header  <br>*Fix:* moved to the X-goog-api-key header
- [ ] **OPEN (verify): owner-typed deliverable and pay links are rendered into href with esc() only, no scheme allowlist** `security`
  *Symptom:* portal.html builds '<a class="deliv-btn" href="' + esc(dv.url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' and the same for payLink. esc() stops attribute breakout but not a javascript: or data: value. Hallalu users also view each other's portals as clients, so a hostile owner could ship a click-XSS or phishing 'Pay' button on the app's origin. No scheme check appears anywhere in the build logs; editor-side sanitising was not seen.  <br>*Cause:* Owner-controlled URLs are treated as trusted on a page served cross-account.  <br>*Fix:* Not fixed: allow only http(s)/mailto/tel on render and on save, and fall back to text.
- [ ] **OPEN: a published client portal link cannot be revoked, unpublished or expired** `security`
  *Symptom:* /portal.html?p=<pid> serves the stored snapshot (client name, amount owed, work titles and details, budget, optionally message notes) from portal_links.body to anyone holding the link. Nothing in the build lets the owner unpublish it, rotate the pid or set an expiry, and the fragment links are irrevocable by design.  <br>*Cause:* Capability-URL design with no revoke path. Other Hallalu features (client folder links) have a revoke control; the portal does not.  <br>*Fix:* Not fixed: add /api/portal/unpublish (delete body or set revoked), a pid-rotate action, an optional expiry, and make the page say 'this link has been withdrawn'.
- [ ] **OPEN: public write endpoints rely on a per-isolate in-memory rate limiter** `security`
  *Symptom:* The unauthenticated portal comment, beacon and booking-submit routes, plus /api/ai (60 per minute per IP), /api/vision (20) and /api/unfurl, all call tooMany(), a module-global Map (`hits`, cleared when it exceeds 5000 entries).  <br>*Cause:* Worker isolates do not share memory across colos and are recycled, so the limit is best-effort and can be bypassed by spreading requests; the Map is also wiped wholesale at 5000 keys. Inferred from the shown code (src/worker.js:176-182).  <br>*Fix:* Not fixed: use a D1 counter or a Durable Object / the Workers rate-limit binding for public writes and expensive AI routes.
- [ ] **Owner email exposed client-side in admin.js** `security`
  *Symptom:* The admin owner email was shipped in the client-side admin.js bundle.  <br>*Cause:* The owner-email/admin check lived in client code instead of on the server.  <br>*Fix:* Moved the owner email/admin check server-side.
- [ ] **Private investor deck gate was decorative: //_pitch.html and /%5Fpitch.html returned the whole deck and deck.js (34KB) was a public asset** `security`
  *Symptom:* //_pitch.html and /%5Fpitch.html returned 200 with the full deck; /deck.js and //deck.js returned the payload; only /pitch and /_pitch.html 404ed  <br>*Cause:* static assets are served before the Worker; run_worker_first patterns match the raw path while the asset server normalises // and percent-encoding, and the JS payload sat in public/ so gating the HTML protected nothing  <br>*Fix:* both files moved out of public/ into src/private/*.txt and imported as Worker Text modules (rules glob **/*.txt); /pitch normalises the path then checks PITCH_KEY; 15 attack paths all 404
- [ ] **Standalone meeting-card link carries its whole model unsigned in the URL fragment, and the logo URL falls back to the raw value when safeUrl rejects it** `security`
  *Symptom:* Anyone can hand-craft portal.html?card=1#<base64 JSON> and have the app's own origin render an official-looking 'You're booked in' card with arbitrary title, location, add-to-calendar link, logo image and email-collect form.  <br>*Cause:* Card snapshots are client-decoded base64 JSON with no signature and no server registration (unlike registered pid portals). meetCard() builds the logo as esc(safeUrl(d.logo)||d.logo), so an allow-list rejection (empty string) falls back to the unvetted URL. Text fields are escaped with esc(), so this is a phishing and tracking-pixel risk rather than script injection.  <br>*Fix:* Not fixed in this batch. Recommended: HMAC-sign the card snapshot or register it server-side as /api/portal/register already does for pid portals, and drop the ||d.logo fallback.
- [ ] **Stored XSS in the public proposal-accept page title** `security`
  *Symptom:* A proposal titled with </title><script>… executed when a client opened the public /accept link  <br>*Cause:* The public unauthenticated accept page interpolated d.title / d.from raw into the <title> even though the body escaped them  <br>*Fix:* Escaped the title/from values consistently with the body; also added nosniff / X-Frame-Options / Referrer-Policy to the public HTML responses
- [ ] **The _headers CSP never reached the app shell because the Worker builds those responses itself, and the policy had to be pasted into six response sites** `security`
  *Symptom:* After adding Content-Security-Policy and nosniff, framing and referrer headers to public/_headers, the landing page and /app (answered by the Worker through run_worker_first) kept their hand-written header sets with no CSP; six Worker HTML responses each carried their own copy of the headers  <br>*Cause:* Workers static-assets _headers applies only to files the asset server returns; a Response built in the Worker does not inherit it, and the repeated literal header objects drifted apart  <br>*Fix:* Define the policy once as a shared constant and apply it through one helper used by every Worker HTML response, then verify the live response headers on both / and /app (done for six sites; the helper is still open)
- [ ] **Unfurl SSRF guard checks hostname only and follows redirects: DNS rebinding and redirect-to-internal bypass** `security`
  *Symptom:* The paste-a-link unfurl rejects obvious private hostnames but a public hostname that resolves to a private IP, or a public URL that 302s to an internal address, passes the guard.  <br>*Cause:* The guard validates the string hostname once and then fetches with redirect:'follow', with no resolved-IP check and no re-validation per hop.  <br>*Fix:* Use redirect:'manual' and re-validate each Location, block private/loopback/link-local ranges after resolution where possible, cap size and time, and strip response headers. Documented; hardening still open.
- [ ] **Anything kept under S.ui is lost on reload because save() destructures ui away (third incident: chosen theme)** `data-loss`
  *Symptom:* A named theme chosen in Settings reverted after a reload. The same root cause had already wiped a running work timer on refresh and the selected call lead (the calls screen then showed another company's history).  <br>*Cause:* window.save() persists const { ui, account, sync, ...rest } = S, so every preference written to S.ui is memory-only; features keep putting user choices there because it is the convenient place for view state.  <br>*Fix:* bgTheme moved to S.settings (persisted); live timers are persisted under a liveTimers key and restored by resumeTimers; the call lead is persisted. House rule: S.ui is ephemeral view state only, any user-chosen setting belongs in S.settings.
- [ ] **Invoice builder wipes line items on every repaint** `data-loss`
  *Symptom:* 'Add a line' did nothing and invoices could not be saved.  <br>*Cause:* invoiceBuilder() re-initialised the line items on every repaint, wiping typed input.  <br>*Fix:* Separated initialization from repaint so line items persist, and corrected the maths.
- [ ] **Opening a picker modal from inside a form wiped the parent form** `data-loss`
  *Symptom:* Opening the currency picker from inside New-lead / Settings / Invoice destroyed the parent modal and every value already typed into it  <br>*Cause:* The app uses a single-overlay model — openModal() calls closeOverlays() first — so drawing the picker tore down the form underneath it  <br>*Fix:* Rewrote openCurPick as its own stacked overlay (z-index above the parent modal) that closes only itself, leaving the form intact
- [ ] **Portal editor deletes were one-click with no confirmation for nine releases (house rule: delete-confirm)** `data-loss`
  *Symptom:* Clicking the remove control on a manual update, step, task, phase or deliverable removed it instantly (a phase went from 1 to 0, a timeline row from 8 to 7). The user had to ask again for delete confirmations.  <br>*Cause:* The destructive data-act handlers were written without confirmDel; there was no shared helper that every delete had to go through.  <br>*Fix:* askDel() wraps confirmDel (Keep it / Yes, delete) and re-opens the editor; every portal remove now uses it.
- [ ] **Several destructive deletes ran with no confirmation (logged social action, team post, tax payment, cadence/calendar sub-events)** `data-loss`
  *Symptom:* One tap on delete removed a logged social action, a team wall post or a recorded tax payment (which also puts the 'still held' figure back) with no undo or prompt, while core deletes (lead, project, todo, note) already confirmed.  <br>*Cause:* Confirmation was added per feature, not centrally; the social, team, tax and cadence handlers were written later and called their delete directly.  <br>*Fix:* Wrap social-act-del, team-post-del and tx-del in confirmDel with a plain-English consequence line; sweep every data-act ending in -del for a confirm.
- [ ] **/api/track throws TDZ from duplicate** `crash`
  *Symptom:* The `/api/track` endpoint threw a temporal-dead-zone error.  <br>*Cause:* A leftover duplicate declaration.  <br>*Fix:* Removed the duplicate; endpoint works.
- [ ] **Admin check crashes for signed-out users** `crash`
  *Symptom:* The app would crash for signed-out visitors.  <br>*Cause:* `isAdminUser()` dereferenced a null account.  <br>*Fix:* Added a null guard.
- [ ] **Calendar view crashed on any event kind not in KINDS (new 'focus'/'personal' events, .ics import, hand-edited record)** `crash`
  *Symptom:* TypeError reading 'color' of undefined at calendar.js:176 took the whole calendar view down  <br>*Cause:* five call sites indexed KINDS[e.kind].color directly with no fallback  <br>*Fix:* single lookup with neutral fallback at all five sites; Ava's add_event constrained to valid kinds
- [ ] **Hallucinated Stripe checkout APIs** `crash`
  *Symptom:* Checkout would have failed the instant real keys were added.  <br>*Cause:* A previous session used non-existent Stripe APIs (a fake 'Dahlia' Stripe.js, `createEmbeddedCheckoutPage`, and a fabricated `API_VERSION='2026-07-29.dahlia'`).  <br>*Fix:* Rewrote to the real Embedded Checkout API and correct version header (four fixes).
- [ ] **Signup returned Cloudflare HTML 500 'Worker threw exception' — PBKDF2 at 210,000 iterations exceeds the Workers cap** `crash`
  *Symptom:* POST /api/auth/signup returned a Cloudflare error page (content-type text/html) instead of JSON; sign-up, sign-in and every auth test failed and wrangler tail showed no logged exception  <br>*Cause:* WebCrypto PBKDF2 on Workers rejects iteration counts above 100,000 (undocumented); the throw escaped the route try/catch so no JSON error was produced  <br>*Fix:* ITER lowered to 100_000 and an HMAC 'pepper' (PASS_PEPPER secret) layered over the derived bits as the compensating control; 15/15 backend checks then passed
- [ ] **AI assistant could execute Business-tier actions (milestones, timers, team invites) on a Pro plan** `auth`
  *Symptom:* Plan awareness existed only as UI gating helpers; Ava's action layer would create Business data for lower plans  <br>*Cause:* doAvaAction had no action-to-minimum-plan map; entitlement was enforced only where the UI called gate()  <br>*Fix:* ava-more.js wraps doAvaAction with a plan-requirement map; refusals return an explanation plus the free-week offer (or upgrade once the week is used); calendar still allowed on Pro
- [ ] **Custom business types and asset categories users type are sent to the server as telemetry events and listed by name in the admin panel** `privacy`
  *Symptom:* A 'What users are adding' admin panel shows each custom label a user typed, grouped with counts; the tracker's own header promises it never captures anything typed  <br>*Cause:* track('custom_added') carries the free-text label inside the action field (clamped to 60 chars) so the admin can see demand, with no allow-list, redaction or disclosure  <br>*Fix:* Open: send a hashed or bucketed label only, or let the user opt in to share a category name, and update the tracker contract and privacy copy to match
- [ ] **No privacy policy and no delete-my-data path although accounts store personal data (flagged, not fixed)** `privacy`
  *Symptom:* Users can create accounts and store contacts and call notes but nothing explains processing or lets them erase their data.  <br>*Cause:* Compliance pages and a self-serve erase flow were never built.  <br>*Fix:* Publish a privacy policy and add a delete-account control that erases D1 rows and R2 objects (GDPR Art. 17) with an export first.
- [ ] **OPEN: client-portal privacy filter keys on a title regex because internal follow-up reminders carry no marker** `privacy`
  *Symptom:* Auto-generated reminders are stored as kind:'todo' with a title 'Follow up <name> - <label>'. The portal hides them with /^follow up/i, which also hides a legitimate meeting called 'Follow-up call' and would leak a reminder if the title format ever changed or the owner renamed one.  <br>*Cause:* schedule.js creates the events with no distinguishing field, so the client-facing filter has nothing but the title to go on. The assistant kept the regex because it errs toward privacy.  <br>*Fix:* Not fixed: stamp internal:true (or src:'followup') on the event when it is created and filter on that.
- [ ] **A versioned asset URL (calm.js?v=38) returned 0 bytes right after deploy and stayed cached, leaving the feature undefined until a cache:reload** `sync`
  *Symptom:* Straight after a wrangler deploy the page loaded index.html with the calm.js tag but window.calmStart was undefined with no console error; fetching /calm.js?v=38 returned length 0.  <br>*Cause:* Behaves like a propagation-window race where an empty or old response for the new immutable ?v= URL was cached by the browser/edge. A fetch with cache:'reload' returned the full bytes and the local file was fine.  <br>*Fix:* Bump ?v= again and redeploy and re-fetch with {cache:'reload'}; add a post-deploy check that each new asset has non-zero length (curl -s URL | wc -c) before declaring the deploy verified.
- [ ] **Cloud autosave silently dropped writes made during an in-flight push, then raised a false 'changed somewhere else' conflict on a single device** `sync`
  *Symptom:* 14 rapid saves lost the last several; the conflict dialog appeared with only one device in use  <br>*Cause:* pushNow() did 'if (pushing) return' after the debounce timer had already fired (no reschedule), and a later push carried a stale rev so the server answered 409 for the client's own earlier write  <br>*Fix:* serialise pushes (dirty flag plus immediate re-push on completion), remember revs the client itself produced and adopt them on 409; genuine second-device writes still raise the conflict
- [ ] **New demo seed data never reaches existing demo sessions (user saw an 'older version' and the session blamed a stale deploy)** `sync`
  *Symptom:* The user said the demo 'shows an older version'. Seed edits (Glossier to Sephora, ecommerce types/affiliate/photos) were invisible on any browser that had opened the demo before.  <br>*Cause:* decorateDemo and `if (!st.ecom) st.ecom = {...}` run only when the demo state is created; a reload hydrates the persisted localStorage copy.  <br>*Fix:* Version the seed (S.demoSeedV) and migrate or re-seed when it changes.
- [ ] **Stale HTML shell served from cache** `sync`
  *Symptom:* The browser kept serving a stale index.html, making fixes look broken for testers and real users.  <br>*Cause:* The HTML shell was being cached even though versioned assets already handle caching.  <br>*Fix:* Made the shell never cache so deploys are picked up immediately.
- [ ] **App never signed the user in: bootAccount scheduled on setTimeout(0) before the next script was fetched** `race`
  *Symptom:* Valid session cookie and /api/auth/me returning the full user, yet S.account stayed null on every page load (silent, no console error)  <br>*Cause:* app.js ran setTimeout(()=>{ if(typeof bootAccount==='function') bootAccount() },0) but wire.js (which defines it) was still downloading; the timer fired in that gap and the typeof guard turned the failure into a silent no-op  <br>*Fix:* removed the timer from app.js; wire.js (last script, owner of the function) calls bootAccount() at the end of its own execution
- [ ] **'Do next' sorted only by due date: a new action set for 3 days sank to the bottom and 'Today' could not be chosen** `logic`
  *Symptom:* The user added a next action and it landed at the bottom of the list; the New follow-up modal offered Tomorrow as the earliest option.  <br>*Cause:* nextStream() was a plain sort on next.due with no notion of when the action was created, save-followup never stamped a created time, and the quick chips started at 1 day.  <br>*Fix:* Bucket overdue, then today, then upcoming; within today the newest action first; save-followup stamps next.setAt; a Today chip was added. Verified in the live Do-next view.
- [ ] **'New call' button did nothing: duplicate case 'new-call' in one switch, the first case shadowed the real modal** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Button click only reset the recorder; the New call modal at the bottom of the switch was unreachable dead code  <br>*Cause:* two case 'new-call' labels in the same handler switch (lines ~803 and ~1157); the first wins  <br>*Fix:* first renamed to 'reset-recorder'; grep -o "case '...':" | sort | uniq -d used to look for other duplicates
- [ ] **'Say a first hello' prompt stayed after a call was logged** `logic`
  *Symptom:* A lead that had just been called remained in 'To reach out' with the first-hello prompt.  <br>*Cause:* saveAttempt() only advanced a lead off stage new for interested or booked outcomes and never set firstTouch for other attempts.  <br>*Fix:* Any logged call attempt sets firstTouch and moves new to messaged (never downgrading a later stage). Verified on a new-stage lead: it moved to the waiting bucket and the prompt disappeared. Note this also treats a no-answer attempt as a contact attempt.
- [ ] **A contact with only a business name could not be saved (add-lead demanded a name, handle or phone)** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Entering just a Business name and pressing Add to pipeline showed the 'give them a name or handle' toast and saved nothing.  <br>*Cause:* The save-lead fallback chain was name || handle || first phone || ... and never consulted the nlBiz field.  <br>*Fix:* Add nlBiz to the fallback chain (name, business, handle, phone) and update the toast; verified a lead 'Test Biz Co' saves with an empty name.
- [ ] **Ava (LLM assistant) created reminders and company goals in the past (2024/2025) because the model did the date arithmetic** `logic`
  *Symptom:* 'Remind me next Tuesday' stored 2025-01-07 and 'by December' stored 2024-12-31 while today was 30 Jul 2026, so the to-do never appears; the first fix (pull past dates forward) produced 2027-01-07  <br>*Cause:* dayOf() accepted whatever ISO date the model returned; models are unreliable at relative dates even with today's date in the prompt  <br>*Fix:* model now passes the user's own phrase ('next tuesday', 'in 3 days'); dayOf() resolves it deterministically in code and clamps any past ISO date to the next occurrence; prompt states the current year
- [ ] **Ava voice command stopped at the first pause, and the live transcript silently broke after the composer redesign** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Dictating to Ava ended at the first breath, cutting long commands. After the composer redesign the live 'heard' text stopped updating with no error.  <br>*Cause:* SpeechRecognition was created with continuous=false (one-shot). The redesigned composer dropped the #avaOrb and #avaHeard ids that the mic code writes to, and the null writes failed silently.  <br>*Fix:* Continuous capture with accumulation and restart-on-end guarded by a stop flag; the ids were restored.
- [ ] **Billing view shows outdated Solo/Studio/£19 pricing** `logic`
  *Symptom:* The billing/pricing view still displayed the old Solo/Studio tiers with a £19 price even for the free plan (the 'free 19 pounds' bug).  <br>*Cause:* The billing view rendered a stale pricing model instead of the current flat tiers.  <br>*Fix:* Rewrote the billing view to USD flat pricing with free=$0 and stripped the pricing promises.
- [ ] **Blended valuation dragged the low end down to the asset floor, showing $19,600 to $144,000 for a $96,000 to $144,000 earnings range** `logic`
  *Symptom:* After three assets were logged the hero range fell to a low of $19,600 although the earnings lens said $96,000 to $144,000  <br>*Cause:* The blend took the minimum of the earnings low and the asset net value, so a small floor pulled the range down; a floor should only ever raise it  <br>*Fix:* Lift the low and high to the floor only when net asset value exceeds them, and test the three cases (floor below, inside and above the range, e.g. a $200,000 building lifting the range to $219,600)
- [ ] **Call recap invents bullets and ignores the transcript** `logic`
  *Symptom:* The grounded-outreach call recap showed hardcoded bullets (e.g. 'Friday'/'mockup') instead of anything from the actual call.  <br>*Cause:* extractPoints invented 4 hardcoded bullets when the transcript was short and showRecap never sent the transcript to the AI, rendering static templates.  <br>*Fix:* Grounded the recap in the real transcript with a non-inventing fallback so the key points come from the user's own words.
- [ ] **CTA keyword regex without the i flag missed a capitalised comment keyword** `logic`
  *Symptom:* A post using Comment SEATING was not recognised as a call to action although the lowercase form was.  <br>*Cause:* The detector regex had no case-insensitive flag; shipped in v218 and fixed in v219.  <br>*Fix:* Use the i flag (and normalise whitespace) for every user-facing keyword matcher and add a capitalised case to the test list.
- [ ] **Currency search fails on plurals and place names** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Typing terms like 'dollars', 'trinidad' or 'america' into the currency search returned no match.  <br>*Cause:* curSearch didn't stem trailing plurals (or map related place terms), so 'dollars' never matched 'dollar'.  <br>*Fix:* Made curSearch stem trailing plurals so plural and related terms resolve to the right currency.
- [ ] **Date handling produced the wrong year** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Goal/close dates resolved to the wrong year (e.g. 2024, and 'next Tuesday' became 2027).  <br>*Cause:* Date arithmetic was wrong and the first guard only blocked past dates, pushing them to 2027.  <br>*Fix:* Fixed date computation generally so relative dates resolve to the correct year.
- [ ] **Dead route after creating a project while viewing a client** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Creating a project while viewing a client left the user on a dead route.  <br>*Cause:* Navigation state after project creation resolved to an invalid route.  <br>*Fix:* Corrected the post-creation navigation so it lands on a valid view.
- [ ] **Delete actions silently do nothing** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Several delete controls appeared to work but deleted nothing.  <br>*Cause:* The delete handlers were not wired to actually remove the item.  <br>*Fix:* Fixed the deletes and added delete-confirmations everywhere.
- [ ] **Deliverable Approve button shows 'Approved' even when the POST fails, and stores an approval as an open request** `logic`
  *Symptom:* The assistant noted 'the approve POST will actually 404 silently, but the UI will still show the Approved feedback - that's fine'. On a link whose pid is not registered (owner signed out, never published, or a transient failure) the client sees confirmation and the owner receives nothing. When it does post, the approval arrives as kind='request' status='open' and needs 'Mark done'.  <br>*Cause:* Approve/Request-changes reuse the requests endpoint with a prefilled '✓ Approved' body and show success optimistically without checking the response.  <br>*Fix:* Not fixed: show success only on ok:true, hide Approve when no thread is registered, and give approvals their own kind. Never exercised end to end ('proven by transitivity').
- [ ] **Demo comms/leads didn't seed** `logic`
  *Symptom:* The demo's communications/leads didn't appear because they were never seeded.  <br>*Cause:* Leads live in per-project data, but the demo seeded to st.leads (the wrong data path).  <br>*Fix:* Fixed the demo seed path so demo leads/comms populate.
- [ ] **Demo seed edits never reach returning visitors: decorateDemo only fills missing fields and demo state is cached in localStorage** `logic`
  *Symptom:* After swapping the demo portraits the live demo still showed the old stock photos; only localStorage.clear() revealed the change. Seeding a new demo meeting had the same limitation.  <br>*Cause:* decorateDemo(st) sets lead, contact and user photos only when absent and the cached demo state is reloaded verbatim, so changes to seed data never reach anyone with a prior demo visit.  <br>*Fix:* Verified each change by clearing storage and reloading with a fresh query. The durable fix (a demoSeedVersion stamp compared on boot to re-decorate or re-seed) was not shipped in this session.
- [ ] **Demo showed empty Activity and Milestones, and the first milestone seed used the wrong record shape** `logic`
  *Symptom:* In the demo the Activity timeline and Milestones views were empty, so a visitor could not see two business features that exist.  <br>*Cause:* buildSeedState/decorateDemo never seeded S.activity or S.milestones; a first attempt seeded milestones as {id,title,...} while the view reads {name, emoji, cat, startedAt, achievedAt}.  <br>*Fix:* Seed activity rows and milestones in decorateDemo using the real milestone shape; verified views render them.
- [ ] **Demo shows wrong base currency** `logic`
  *Symptom:* The UK demo displayed USD instead of GBP.  <br>*Cause:* A stale demo from before currency settings existed left USD hard-coded.  <br>*Fix:* Set `st.settings.baseCurrency='GBP'` in decorateDemo.
- [ ] **Demo tier gating honoured the signed-in account's plan, so a Pro user exploring the Business demo saw locked features** `logic`
  *Symptom:* With a real Pro account signed in, the demo (meant to show every Business feature) still reported myPlan()='pro' and hasPlan('business')=false, so Business screens were locked inside the demo.  <br>*Cause:* myPlan() returned S.account.plan before S.user.plan, so the demo seed's user.plan='business' only applied to signed-out visitors.  <br>*Fix:* window.myPlan = () => S.demo ? 'business' : (S.account?.plan || S.user.plan || 'free'); verified the demo reports business for signed-in explorers too.
- [ ] **Dialer dropped the contact's role: leadNumbers read pr.role but the contact form stores pr.title** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Numbers belonging to additional people on a client showed no role label in the dialer.  <br>*Cause:* Field-name mismatch between the contact editor (title) and leadNumbers (role); nothing errored, the label was simply empty.  <br>*Fix:* leadNumbers reads pr.title with pr.role as fallback and now sorts the starred main number first (a main-number star was added to the contact block and honoured by the dialer and WhatsApp).
- [ ] **Duplicate global definitions silently shadow each other: clientMeetingsCard in two files, two case 'mark-sent' labels, two onboarding flows** `logic`
  *Symptom:* A new client-page Meetings card was written, deployed and 'verified' present in the source, yet never rendered because projects.js (loaded later) already defines window.clientMeetingsCard; a second case 'mark-sent' in the same switch was dead code; a copy edit to the sign-up name placeholder landed in signin.js while app.js renders the live onboarding.  <br>*Cause:* Dozens of script files share flat window.* globals and one large data-act switch. The later assignment (or earlier case label) wins with no warning, node --check passes, and typeof-guarded call sites (typeof X==='function' ? X(l) : '') hide which copy runs. Two sessions had also been building overlapping prompts in the same repo.  <br>*Fix:* Removed the dead duplicate and its CSS and handlers after finding that projects.js already owns the feature (Add meeting modal, attended meetings auto-log to the worklog, calendar mirror), reconciled mark-sent into one handler, and surfaced call-booked meetings inside the existing card instead. Rule: grep for window.<name> = and case '<act>' across public/*.js before adding.
- [ ] **Ecom 'By channel' breakdown labelled every channel 'Other' because channel ids did not match platform keys** `logic`
  *Symptom:* The by-channel list in the ecommerce hub showed 'Other' for every row.  <br>*Cause:* Channel ids such as 'ecEtsy' were passed straight to P()/src() which looks up platform keys ('etsy'), so every lookup missed and fell back to Other.  <br>*Fix:* Add platOf(key) that resolves a channel id to its platform (or treats the key as a platform) and use it in grouping, order rows and badges.
- [ ] **Editing a lead can't set Google/Maps source** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Google/Maps couldn't be chosen when editing a lead, and demo leads were mislabeled call/email.  <br>*Cause:* The edit form offered only 4 sources instead of the full source picker.  <br>*Fix:* Switched the edit form to the full source picker and corrected the demo leads.
- [ ] **Element chips and DM-template chips in Socials did nothing (click delegate only matched [data-act])** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Format/element chips (data-sq-el), the Wave-8 DM template chips (data-dm-tpl) and copy buttons (data-dm-copy) never toggled or loaded. It shipped for about one wave.  <br>*Cause:* The click delegate used closest('[data-act]'); the new chips carry other attributes and have no data-act. The wave was 'verified' by presence-only checks (chip count, draft loaded).  <br>*Fix:* Widened the delegate to [data-act],[data-sq-el],[data-dm-tpl],[data-dm-copy]; verified click-to-state.
- [ ] **Fixing the retired Gemini model default in code had no effect because a GEMINI_MODEL secret overrode it** `logic`
  *Symptom:* After changing the default from gemini-2.0-flash (retired, 404) to a current model and redeploying, /api/ai still returned 'model no longer available'; the deployed secret pinned the retired model.  <br>*Cause:* geminiModel(env) = secret('GEMINI_MODEL') || default, and the deployed secret was pinned to the retired model, so the code default was never used.  <br>*Fix:* Update or delete the GEMINI_MODEL secret, add an aiText fallback Gemini->Claude on any provider error, and report which provider keys exist in /api/health.
- [ ] **Free-tier call recap emailed the raw transcript back as the 'professional' follow-up** `logic`
  *Symptom:* After a recorded call the follow-up email and short-message boxes held the pasted raw transcript instead of a written recap; the user said the notes were not professional.  <br>*Cause:* On the free tier aiAllowed() is false, so summariseCall fell to the on-device localSummary(), which only repeats transcript sentences verbatim. On the server aiText() defaulted to Gemini and had no Workers AI text backup, so a quota or no-key response ended in the same raw dump.  <br>*Fix:* Added a Workers AI text path (@cf/meta/llama-3.3-70b-instruct-fp8-fast) as the universal backup plus a cheap flag so free-tier recaps use it; summariseCall always posts to /api/ai with provider claude; a RECAP_SYSTEM persona demands a kind, professional plain-text email with a Next steps list. Verified on the live app: real Claude output, readable in dark mode.
- [ ] **Google-Maps paste parser took the category line as the lead's city** `logic`
  *Symptom:* After pasting four Maps results, one lead's city read 'Roofing Supply Shop' instead of the real city.  <br>*Cause:* The loose city fallback regex matched the rating/category line because it only looked for a capitalised phrase.  <br>*Fix:* Tighten the fallback: skip rating/category lines and only accept a city from the address line or an explicit 'City, ST' pattern.
- [ ] **Hardcoded '3.1×' caption** `logic`
  *Symptom:* A '3.1×' caption was displayed as if computed but was hardcoded.  <br>*Cause:* The multiplier was a static string rather than derived from data.  <br>*Fix:* Corrected the caption for honesty rather than hardcoding it.
- [ ] **Header clock and weather chip showed the device's time, not the chosen city's, and ambiguous city names mis-geocoded** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Choosing London on a US device still showed Eastern time in the chip. Typing a bare city name such as Manchester or London could resolve to the wrong place.  <br>*Cause:* weatherChip rendered with new Date() in the device zone; the geocoder's IANA timezone was never stored. The picker was a native datalist that resolved by name only, and loadWeather re-geocoded by name.  <br>*Fix:* Store the geocoder's timezone (hit.timezone) in S.settings.weather.tz and render the chip in that zone; the picker now stores exact lat/lon/tz, and loadWeather only re-geocodes when !loc.lat || loc.name!==city().
- [ ] **Landing 'See the demo' buttons opened /app, so after the blank-start change visitors saw empty onboarding instead of the demo** `logic`
  *Symptom:* The owner clicked 'See the demo' on the public landing and did not get the populated demo she had been testing.  <br>*Cause:* When new users were switched to a blank studio, the landing CTAs still pointed at /app (and the demo only loads for /app?demo=1), so the public path never reached the seeded demo.  <br>*Fix:* Point both demo links to /app?demo=1; verified by fingerprinting the demo reached via the landing click against a direct /app?demo=1 load (IDENTICAL).
- [ ] **Landing 'See the demo' silently dropped returning visitors into their real free-plan session, and the fix was deployed before the data-safety path was tested** `logic`
  *Symptom:* The user could not see the business parts in the demo. /app?demo=1 only started the demo if (!S.onboarded), so anyone who had used the app before stayed in their real session where business views are walled. The v184 fix always enters the demo, stashing real data in hallalu_predemo with a 'Back to my data' button. It was deployed first; the first test then showed realDataBackedUp:false.  <br>*Cause:* The onboarded guard existed to avoid overwriting local-first data. After deploy the empty backup was explained away as test seeding (leads are PROJECT_KEYS and the hand-set top-level leads were dropped), and the round trip was then proven only with top-level markers (user.name, S.__proof), not with project-scoped leads or invoices, which are most of a real user's data. How cloud sync behaves for a signed-in user entering the demo was never checked.  <br>*Fix:* Stash-and-restore with a restore button and 'Start my own' clearing the stash; verified restore by clicking the real button. Project-scoped data and signed-in sync remain unproven.
- [ ] **Landing page silently redirects onboarded users to /app** `logic`
  *Symptom:* The deployed landing page at / was never visible to onboarded users because it silently bounced them to /app.  <br>*Cause:* An onboarded-user redirect fired unconditionally at the root route.  <br>*Fix:* Fixed the redirect (adding an ?app override) so the landing page is reachable.
- [ ] **Local 'pct' variable shadows global pct() function** `logic`
  *Symptom:* A percentage calculation misbehaved due to name collision.  <br>*Cause:* A local variable named pct shadowed the global pct() function.  <br>*Fix:* Renamed the shadowing local variable to remove the collision.
- [ ] **Marking an invoice paid (or Ava log_payment) never wrote the payment to the client's own story** `logic`
  *Symptom:* A paid invoice updated invoices and the money totals, but the client page, timeline and payment history showed nothing.  <br>*Cause:* Payments lived on invoices and S.money only. mark-paid and Ava's log_payment each wrote their own subset, so there were several payment write paths and none touched lead.payments or the client timeline.  <br>*Fix:* One logClientPayment() writes lead.payments + a timeline money entry + S.money; mark-paid was inlined onto it so a payment is not counted twice.
- [ ] **NaN% reply rate and non-zero outreach on empty project** `logic`
  *Symptom:* An empty project showed NaN% for reply rate and a fresh project did not start its outreach count at 0.  <br>*Cause:* Metrics divided by zero / lacked initialization for empty projects.  <br>*Fix:* Removed the NaN and made a fresh project start outreach count at 0.
- [ ] **One booked meeting rendered two or three times in Today > Meetings** `logic`
  *Symptom:* A meeting auto-booked from a call appeared up to three times with slightly different titles.  <br>*Cause:* calEvents() merges the real S.cal.events entry with a derived reminder for lead.next (id d_<leadId>, title 'Name - label'). upcomingMeetings() de-duplicated by title, so the same meeting at the same minute counted twice.  <br>*Fix:* De-duplicate on leadId plus minute (Math.round(ts/60000)) instead of title. Verified: six rows collapsed to two, and a clean demo shows one row.
- [ ] **OPEN: back-dated posts are stamped at 12:00 local, polluting the best-time heatmap** `logic`
  *Symptom:* After adding a 'When posted' date, every back-dated post lands in the noon bucket of the personal best-time (day x hour) heatmap. Before that, the heatmap used the time the action was logged as a proxy for post time.  <br>*Cause:* whenToTs builds new Date(y,m-1,d,12,0,0) for any non-today date, inventing an hour that the user never gave. Inferred from the shown code; not fixed in the session.  <br>*Fix:* Ask for a time, or exclude date-only posts from hour-of-day analysis and say so in the card.
- [ ] **OPEN: budget total on the client-facing page read 86.48 while the snapshot said $648; dismissed without investigation** `logic`
  *Symptom:* The Delivery-portal snapshot reported spent '$648'; the rendered client page showed a total of '86.48'. The assistant wrote 'possibly just a formatting or rendering quirk I shouldn't fixate on' and moved on.  <br>*Cause:* Unknown. A money total shown to a client disagrees with its source and was never traced.  <br>*Fix:* Not fixed: compare d.budget.spent with the rendered text and check the money() formatting/rounding path.
- [ ] **OPEN: owner's own preview inflates the portal 'viewed N times' counter** `logic`
  *Symptom:* The owner's full-page portal embeds the real portal.html in an iframe and reloads it on every swatch click or edit, and the page sends a view beacon on load whenever the snapshot carries a pid. The 'viewed 3x, last seen 2h ago' stat would count the owner's previews as client views. Inferred from the shown design; no preview exclusion appears anywhere in the build logs.  <br>*Cause:* The beacon has no way to tell the owner's preview from a client visit.  <br>*Fix:* Not fixed: load the preview with a preview flag (or inside the frame) and skip the beacon, or exclude events from the signed-in owner.
- [ ] **OPEN: social goals mix a derived done flag written inside render() with a day-based goals system** `logic`
  *Symptom:* After saving a social goal, done:0 stayed stale until the Overview tab rendered. The main Goals view filters day===today, so period-based social targets do not fit it. Editing a social goal in the generic goalModal would strip its metric linkage.  <br>*Cause:* Social goals live in shared S.goals with area:'social'+metric; done is derived but written back into g.done as a side effect of render().  <br>*Fix:* Not fixed: compute done on read instead of writing it in render(), and give social goals their own edit path.
- [ ] **Outcome picker forced after every recorded call although the transcript already says what happened** `logic`
  *Symptom:* After recording, typing notes and tapping submit, the app still asked what kind of call it was and whether they answered.  <br>*Cause:* endCall always routed to the manual outcome panel (S.ui.pendingOutcome) and only then ran the recap; nothing inferred the outcome from the words that were captured.  <br>*Fix:* endCall skips the picker when at least 25 characters of real speech exist and calls summariseCall with auto:true; the recap prompt returns the outcome enum, a tidied transcript and any meeting as JSON; the recap shows the inferred outcome as an editable select and a change rewrites the logged attempt. The picker remains only for calls with no words.
- [ ] **Paste importer mis-parses co.uk domains** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Pasted leads with `.co.uk` sites and business domains were parsed wrong (domain treated as email).  <br>*Cause:* TLD ordering matched `co` before `co.uk`, and business-domain-vs-email wasn't distinguished.  <br>*Fix:* Fixed TLD ordering so `co.uk` matches first and separated business domain from email.
- [ ] **Paste importer treats a whole Google Maps paste as one lead** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Pasting Google Maps results imported only 'found 1 lead' and used the first line 'Sponsored' as the lead's name.  <br>*Cause:* The importer split leads on blank lines, but Google Maps pastes have none, so the entire block parsed as a single lead.  <br>*Fix:* Reworked the importer so a run-together paste splits into individual leads (via the AI-split path) instead of one block.
- [ ] **Phone-number parser misreads across lines** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Phone parsing failed on multi-line input and numbers with a leading parenthesis.  <br>*Cause:* The phone regex crossed line boundaries and didn't allow a leading paren.  <br>*Fix:* Rewrote the phone pattern to stay on one line and allow a leading paren.
- [ ] **Plans page prefixed GBP amounts with a hard-coded dollar sign (customers saw $15/month for a £15 price)** `logic`
  *Symptom:* The pricing view showed $15 / $25 / $50 while the Stripe prices behind the buttons were GBP, so the displayed currency did not match what checkout would charge.  <br>*Cause:* views4.js defined `const money$ = n => '$' + n` and fed it the GBP plan amounts; no currency table existed for the plan prices.  <br>*Fix:* Add a per-currency price table that must match scripts/setup-stripe.mjs and drive display and checkout currency from the same selection (later switched to USD-only with Stripe localising at checkout).
- [ ] **Plus price lived in four places; the UI said $18 while the real Stripe charge path still said $15, and a changed amount could never take effect** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Asked to change Plus to $18, the assistant edited two sources, deployed, and a browser check showed the Plus card still at '$13/month' (the old 150/12). It committed 'across all 3 sources' and told the user Stripe prices follow the same numbers 'automatically'. That was false: src/stripe.js has its own PLAN_USD at [15,150]. Between the v183 deploy and the later fix, the page advertised $18 on a path that charged $15.  <br>*Cause:* Four hand-mirrored copies: views4.js PLANS, views4.js PLAN_FLAT (what the grid renders), extras.js:589 billing map, stripe.js PLAN_USD ('Amounts mirror the pricing page exactly'). Also ensurePrice minted Stripe Prices with a fixed lookup_key (hallalu_plus_m) and reused any existing price under that key, and Stripe Prices are immutable, so changing PLAN_USD alone would never change the charge. The price grep covered public/*.js and src/*.js, not the HTML pages.  <br>*Fix:* PLAN_USD to [18,180] and an amount-aware lookup_key (hallalu_plus_m_18 / hallalu_plus_y_180) so a price change mints a fresh Price; existing subscribers keep their price; entitlement falls back to subscription metadata.plan. Listed secret names only and confirmed no PRICE_PLUS_* ids are pinned (so the deployment auto-mints), then proved the 1800/18000 unit_amount payload by computing it. The four copies are still hand-mirrored.
- [ ] **Read-aloud reply and button do nothing** `logic`
  *Symptom:* The assistant's reply did not speak and the read-aloud button after the fact did nothing.  <br>*Cause:* The speak-on-reply path and the manual read-aloud handler were both broken.  <br>*Fix:* Fixed both halves so replies speak and the button works.
- [ ] **Read-aloud toggle only applied to future replies, so enabling it after a reply did nothing** `logic`
  *Symptom:* User turned on 'Ava replies out loud' after missing her answer and heard nothing; no way to replay a past reply  <br>*Cause:* toggle only set S.ui.avaSpeaks (checked on the next reply); no per-message speaker control  <br>*Fix:* each Ava reply gets its own speaker button and switching the toggle on reads her latest reply immediately
- [ ] **Rolodex cards show fields with no way to enter them** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Contact cards displayed fields (e.g. in influencers) that had no corresponding input path.  <br>*Cause:* Card display and the entry form were driven by different definitions.  <br>*Fix:* Refactored so one schema drives both the card and the entry form.
- [ ] **Sign-out always prompts a confirm, breaking auto-forget** `logic`
  *Symptom:* signOut always showed a confirmation dialog, which is wrong for the auto-forget (ephemeral 'keep me signed in') flow.  <br>*Cause:* The sign-out path unconditionally showed a confirm dialog regardless of the ephemeral/auto-forget session mode.  <br>*Fix:* Made the ephemeral auto-forget sign-out skip the confirm dialog.
- [ ] **Time zone search for 'trinidad' found nothing because place aliases were missing from the IANA metadata** `logic`
  *Symptom:* The new searchable picker returned no result for Trinidad although America/Port_of_Spain was in the list of 400-plus zones, the same 'too few' complaint it was meant to cure  <br>*Cause:* Search matched only the IANA city name, country code and offset text, with no alias for the country or island name  <br>*Fix:* Added a curated alias list (Caribbean and other islands first) so country and place names resolve; the list reached 441 searchable places
- [ ] **Trial→paid KPI shows NaN%** `logic`
  *Symptom:* The admin dashboard's 'Trial→paid' KPI rendered NaN%.  <br>*Cause:* An operator-precedence error in the KPI calculation.  <br>*Fix:* Corrected the precedence so the KPI computes a real percentage.
- [ ] **Two window.money definitions (ui.js hard-codes the pound sign, currency.js overrides it) so correctness depends on script load order** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Callers that omit a currency rely on currency.js loading after ui.js; if the order changes or one file fails to load, amounts silently render as pounds.  <br>*Cause:* The legacy `window.money = n => '£' + ...` in ui.js was never removed when the multi-currency fmtCur-based money() was added in currency.js; the override works only because of <script> order in index.html.  <br>*Fix:* Delete the legacy definition (or make ui.js's version delegate to fmtCur/baseCurrency) and add a test that money(1234) uses the base currency symbol.
- [ ] **Typing a number into the posting-rhythm field did nothing until blur (handler bound to change, not input)** `logic`
  *Symptom:* The user's original complaint 'i tried changing the number manually and cant' survived the first fix: typed values only registered after clicking away.  <br>*Cause:* The handler was on the change event. A full re-render on every keystroke would drop focus, which is why it was not on input.  <br>*Fix:* State and save update on input and only the goal sentence repaints ([data-post-goal-line]); the full render stays on change. Verified 70, 150 and the stepper (151).
- [ ] **Voice time parser silently returned 10:00 for 'half two' and 'quarter past nine'** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Spoken 'add a meeting tomorrow at half two' booked 10:00 (or failed to match) with no error shown  <br>*Cause:* time regex only understood digits; the default-hour fallback hid the failure instead of failing loudly  <br>*Fix:* parser rewritten for word numbers, o'clock, half past, quarter to, bare hours (read as working hours); 15 spoken forms tested. OPEN: British 'half nine' still resolves to 09:00 rather than 09:30
- [ ] **Voice-memo player showed the wrong time (seconds passed to a milliseconds formatter)** `logic`
  *Symptom:* The live time and duration in the voice-note player were off by a factor of 1000; the skip button also left the seek slider stale until the next tick.  <br>*Cause:* setTime() passed audio.currentTime and duration (seconds) to clock(ms).  <br>*Fix:* Multiply by 1000 before clock(); vnSkip now updates the slider immediately.
- [ ] **WhatsApp was in the master social-platform map but missing from about ten hard-coded platform lists** `logic`
  *Symptom:* A WhatsApp channel could be logged in Socials but not detected from a pasted link, not added in Rolodex/Creators/Study/Org profile, and rendered as a plain 'W' monogram with no media-kit or auto-site emoji.  <br>*Cause:* The platform list was duplicated across socials.js PLATS, socialsx.js platGuess/prettyPlat/emoji, rolodex, creators, study, org form and save payload, kit.html, site.html, views2/views3 and Ava's vision list instead of read from one source.  <br>*Fix:* Swept every hard-coded list and added WhatsApp (glyph, wa.me link builder, org field oWa, paste-link detection, icons, Ava inference), bumped ?v= 210 to 211 and verified the live files.
- [ ] **Worker chunking not byte-safe and header auth broken** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Worker request chunking and header-based auth were faulty.  <br>*Cause:* Chunking split on characters rather than bytes and auth headers were mishandled.  <br>*Fix:* Reworked the worker for byte-safe chunking and correct header auth.
- [ ] **Compliance warnings shipped with invented or overstated rules: UK DMCC 'gating is illegal', FTC rule 'covers gating', Google LSA per-job review cap, Yelp 'cannot message reviewers'** `claims`
  *Symptom:* Source registry told users the law or platform terms said things they do not; Care.com's $10k/day clause was presented as a ban on keeping contact details; Dribbble's trigger was understated (merely viewing a profile starts a 12-month non-circumvention term)  <br>*Cause:* first-pass research summaries written straight into sources.js without reading the primary terms or statute  <br>*Fix:* 6 corrections verified against primary text (DMCC bans fake and concealed-incentive reviews, FTC 16 CFR 465.4 does not cover gating, LSA caps do not exist, Yelp business page may reply, Care.com clause is anti-scraping, Dribbble broadened)
- [ ] **Customer-facing copy exposed build internals ('See RESEARCH.md in the repo', 'Demo extraction', 'backend', 'in production Hallalu')** `claims`
  *Symptom:* Public users read text that made no sense to them and revealed proprietary process (user: 'this is proprietary and confidential')  <br>*Cause:* internal notes written as UI copy and never grepped for before shipping  <br>*Fix:* purged and rewritten as verifiable customer wording ('Every figure Hallalu shows you names where it came from'); 'Reset demo data' became 'Start fresh'
- [ ] **Myths note called 'post daily or you're punished' debunked when the evidence only shows it is unsupported** `claims`
  *Symptom:* The in-app note said the myths were debunked or outdated. The audit showed Instagram has never denied a posting-frequency penalty; Mosseri's guidance is quality over quantity.  <br>*Cause:* Unsupported was written up as disproven.  <br>*Fix:* Wording softened to 'no one has shown these true'; a Posting rhythm note carries the only traceable cadence (about 2 feed posts a week, Mosseri 2021).
- [ ] **Pricing copy told users they can 'prove the ROI' of every creator while the feature's own research says attribution is directional** `claims`
  *Symptom:* The $50 Business bullet 'hire, contract, pay & prove the ROI of every creator' is an absolute, substantiation-style claim that the product itself disclaims (influence is under-counted, read it as direction).  <br>*Cause:* Marketing bullets were written from the feature list without checking the verb against the caveats shipped in the same feature (FTC s5 / UK CAP substantiation exposure).  <br>*Fix:* Pro now says 'see the payback'; the Business line still needs the same one-word change (prove to track/see the payback) on landing.html and views4.js, and the in-app IP and valuation caveats must stay.
- [ ] **Pricing page wedge and stats bank shipped two unverifiable statistics: HoneyBook '+89% price rise' (AI affiliate SEO folklore) and '21x more likely to qualify within 5 minutes' (2007 vendor deck histogram ratio)** `claims`
  *Symptom:* Pricing page and deck asserted an 89% overnight hike that HoneyBook's live pricing contradicts; the 21x figure is a ratio of histogram bars (12,700/1,190) with no denominator  <br>*Cause:* stats accepted from a research subagent whose source for 89% was two SEO affiliate sites  <br>*Fix:* both removed; replaced with Xero invoice data (28.8 days, 32,000+ businesses), IRS Data Book FY2025 and a 2025 Hennessey Digital response-time audit; speed-to-lead concept restored with HBR 2011 (1.25M leads) after the critic was verified
- [ ] **Real TikTok/Instagram effect names deleted because a research lane 'could not verify' them while its search tools were blocked** `claims`
  *Symptom:* Time Warp Scan, Inverted, Speed ramp, Bling and Superzoom were removed from the effects suggestions. The user called it out: a critique is a claim too, and do not invent a restriction.  <br>*Cause:* The lane reported a 200/200 search budget exhausted and bot-gated engines, yet its 'unverified' list was treated as 'does not exist'. The field is free text, so keeping a stale suggestion costs nothing while removing a famous one costs usability.  <br>*Fix:* All five verified real (TikTok effect page, trending pages, Effect House docs, Instagram camera) and restored with a code comment recording why; lesson saved.
- [ ] **Requested portal behaviours were dropped or reduced but reported as done** `claims`
  *Symptom:* (1) Emoji pulse: the user chose 'private by default, toggle for the client to see'; only private shipped and the report says 'private to you' with no mention of the missing toggle. (2) 'emails and see when they have viewed it like Canva': email-open tracking was skipped, and the report says 'view tracking tells you when they opened'. (3) Access 'in Today': the card renders nothing unless a client has unread replies, so the findability problem the user raised remains; the report says 'wired (shows when there are unread replies)'. (4) Widgets 'with the real icon': first built as emoji and reported as 'real icons'; real brand SVGs only arrived after the user complained again ('not the camera logo').  <br>*Cause:* Scope reductions were decided mid-build and not carried into the final report.  <br>*Fix:* Not fixed for 1-3: add the client-visible pulse toggle, an always-visible portal entry on Today, and label page views as page views. Item 4 fixed in v181.
- [ ] **Several shipped research claims were wrong by domain transfer or overreach (portal adoption, Etsy template ban, follow-up counts, filter stats)** `claims`
  *Symptom:* A counter-evidence audit caught: the '23% client-portal adoption' figure (Fraccaro et al 2017) is about healthcare patient portals, headline 52% with a 13-33% CI dropped; the Etsy 'template ban was a misreading' note was too strong (a real June 2025 Creativity Standards change removed the templated-design clause for computerised tools); the debunk of '5-7 follow-ups' overreached (the source said fifth to twelfth contact, a small 1942 sample); the '21% more views for filtered photos' stat is a Flickr study (Bakhshi et al 2015), widely miscited as Instagram.  <br>*Cause:* Statistics were carried across domains and critiques accepted without reading the primary source.  <br>*Fix:* Research file corrected (no credible freelancer-portal figure exists), memory corrected, filter stat reattributed.
- [ ] **The shipped Instagram signal claim ranked sends above saves and likes though its source named three unranked signals, and the 2026 re-check fixed only one constant** `claims`
  *Symptom:* Copy said shares/sends are Instagram's most heavily weighted signal, above saves and likes, labelled FACT; the 2026 re-verify found it is surface-specific (sends win for non-followers on Reels, likes edge sends for followers, saves for feed) and that YouTube's "70% of watch time" is a 2018 figure. Only PLAT_METRIC was corrected; the exported carousel slide and the archived Study-the-greats report still carry the old sentence  <br>*Cause:* Mosseri (Jan 2025) named watch time, likes per reach and sends per reach without ranking them; creator-blog sources supplied the ranking and it was tagged FACT. The sentence is then duplicated as literal text in several files with no single source  <br>*Fix:* Open: move every platform-signal sentence to one claims table with a verified-on date, reference it from PLAT_METRIC, the carousel and the report, and re-verify on a schedule
- [ ] **--ink-3 metadata text was 2.54:1 on white (fails WCAG AA) and --ink-2 was well below the premium band** `a11y`
  *Symptom:* Hints, timestamps and metadata used --ink-3 (#9aa3b5) at 2.54:1; --ink-2 (#525d6f) measured 6.66:1. Dark-mode --ink-3 sits at 4.12:1.  <br>*Cause:* Text tiers were chosen by eye and never computed. This is real reading text, not decoration.  <br>*Fix:* --ink-2 #3d4757 (9.39:1) and --ink-3 #6b7688 (4.59:1), verified live by computing luminance in the browser. Dark-mode tertiary left slightly under 4.5:1.
- [ ] **39 icon-only buttons rely on the title attribute alone for a name (flagged, not fixed)** `a11y`
  *Symptom:* The scan found 39 icon buttons with no aria-label, so screen readers announce nothing useful.  <br>*Cause:* title was treated as an accessible name; it is not reliably exposed.  <br>*Fix:* Add aria-label to every icon-only control and keep title as the tooltip.
- [ ] **About 100 source files and zero automated tests (flagged, not fixed)** `testing`
  *Symptom:* The scan counted 94 to 101 source files with no test files; regressions rely on manual browser checks.  <br>*Cause:* No test runner or CI was ever set up.  <br>*Fix:* Add a core-loop smoke test (create lead, log call, send reminder) and run it before each deploy.
- [ ] **Portal reports said 'verified' for paths that were never exercised** `testing`
  *Symptom:* Reported verified but not run: the quiet-week tone (the test lead had other activity so it read on-track; 'logic is sound'); the stable ?p=<pid> publish-to-view round trip (publish only fires for signed-in non-demo accounts, so no D1 row was ever created); the print layout (window.print was stubbed and .no-print elements counted); the accent-colour override ('trivially correct'); 3 of 6 first-gallery themes never viewed; the Storefront portal type (only Delivery was rendered); the 'light + dark pass across all 10 templates' (the script measured one scheme, light, and the assistant had noted non-default themes do not respond to prefers-color-scheme:dark); the owner Mark-done flip and the inbox route handlers (only SQL and a mocked fetch).  <br>*Cause:* Verification checked function existence, snapshot fields or code reading rather than rendering the state or running the route.  <br>*Fix:* Not fixed: add an isolated empty-lead fixture, a signed-in test account for publish, print-media emulation, and a dark-scheme run.
- [ ] **Two user-reported portal bugs (Preview not working, Edit not visible) were closed with asserted, undiagnosed causes** `testing`
  *Symptom:* For 'preview button not working' the report states as fact that it 'used a pop-up that browsers block'; the assistant never reproduced it and skipped the send-sheet Preview re-test. The old handler used leadOf(window.__portalLead), which is only set by the modal openers, so an unset or stale global would also make Preview silently do nothing. For 'im not seeing edit button', its own DOM probe had listed Edit among the toolbar buttons; it wrote 'I need to fix the real cause, not guess', then 'rather than fully diagnosing it I'll just make it more prominent', and the report states the cause as fact.  <br>*Cause:* Presence in the DOM was treated as visible and findable; the failure was never reproduced at the user's viewport.  <br>*Fix:* Edit made prominent and re-checked with getBoundingClientRect at 375px; Preview replaced by the full-page live preview and an Open anchor. Root causes remain unconfirmed.
- [ ] **Verification traps in the Hallalu SPA made working code look broken and broken code look fine** `testing`
  *Symptom:* (a) Replacing window.S with buildSeedState() broke the defineProperty getters for leads and todos, so calEvents threw on forEach of undefined; (b) a forced save() left onboarded:false so the next load landed on onboarding and 'the Meetings section is missing'; (c) changing only the #hash of portal.html does not re-run decode(), so the old card stayed on screen; (d) the static python preview has no worker routing so /app 404s; (e) fetching the new ?v= asset URL before the deploy finished left a stale copy in the cache so the resizer and frame X looked absent until ?v= was bumped again.  <br>*Cause:* State is a getter-backed object shared by dozens of scripts, portal.html decodes location.hash once at boot, and assets are cache-busted only by the ?v=N query.  <br>*Fix:* Mutate S in place or run localStorage.clear() then load /app?demo=1&fresh=<timestamp>; use a full reload for hash changes; use wrangler dev or the live URL (not the static server) for worker routes; never fetch a versioned asset URL before the deploy completes and bump ?v= again if you did.
- [ ] **OPEN: landing page requests self-hosted Fraunces/Inter woff2 files that 404, twice dismissed as unrelated** `perf`
  *Symptom:* Loading '/' logged several 404s; the probe listed Fraunces-600-normal.woff2, Inter-400-normal.woff2, Fraunces-700-normal.woff2, Inter-800-normal.woff2 and Inter-600-normal.woff2. Typography falls back to system fonts. The assistant said 'pre-existing, likely favicons/fonts' and again 'unrelated font 404s'.  <br>*Cause:* The @font-face rules point at font files that are not in the deployed assets.  <br>*Fix:* Not fixed: ship the files or remove the rules.
- [ ] **A4 note page overflowed its own paper: explicit min-height stopped the flex item growing with its content** `ui`
  *Symptom:* In the full-page note editor the text ran past the bottom of the 1123px sheet (scrollHeight 1370) so the page-break bands no longer matched the text.  <br>*Cause:* A flex item with overflow visible normally has min-height:auto (its content size), but the explicit min-height:1123px replaced that automatic minimum, so with flex-shrink:1 the sheet shrank to 1123px while the content stayed 1370px.  <br>*Fix:* .note-page{flex:none} so the sheet grows with its content; verified a 1478px page with no overflow and a visible page break.
- [ ] **Add-expense modal locked to 7 hardcoded categories** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The add-expense modal offered only a hardcoded list of 7 categories with no way to enter a custom one.  <br>*Cause:* The add-expense modal hardcoded a 7-item category list with no custom-entry option.  <br>*Fix:* Expanded the category list and added a type-your-own custom option.
- [ ] **Add-lead placeholders carried the developer's own locale (Port of Spain, Trinidad & Tobago) in a global product** `ui`
  *Symptom:* City, Country and Address placeholders showed Trinidad examples to every user, which read as pre-filled data and looked wrong for non-Trinidad users; the owner flagged 'remove those example places'.  <br>*Cause:* Placeholder text was written from the author's own location and never genericised.  <br>*Fix:* Use neutral placeholders ('City', 'Country', 'Street address (one per line)') and audit other forms for locale-specific example strings.
- [ ] **AI status stuck on 'Checking…' when API unreachable** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The AI buttons showed a perpetual 'Checking…' state and failed silently when the API was not reachable locally.  <br>*Cause:* The unreachable-API case was not handled with a clear state.  <br>*Fix:* Made the buttons report exactly what is missing instead of hanging or failing silently.
- [ ] **AI-generated artifact h1 wrapped to five lines in the 344px canvas iframe** `ui`
  *Symptom:* A 48px/clamp(34px,...) heading in the Ava canvas wrapped to 5 lines and pushed the content away.  <br>*Cause:* The artifact stylesheet's heading scale assumed a full-width page.  <br>*Fix:* clamp(22px,6vw,42px) in artCSS.
- [ ] **Assets cards shipped buttons with data-act=go-ecom, an action that has no handler, so they did nothing** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The 'products from Ecommerce' asset card had two buttons that did not navigate  <br>*Cause:* The click delegate only knows data-act='go' with data-to; a new action name was invented and never wired  <br>*Fix:* Used the existing data-act=go data-to=ecom; grep every new data-act against the delegate switch before shipping
- [ ] **Assets earnings-lens inputs sat at different heights because one label wrapped to two lines and its sibling label did not** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The SDE input and the Business type select in the same two-column row did not line up; the whole valuation page looked misaligned  <br>*Cause:* The grid let each field size to its own label, so a wrapped label pushed its control down  <br>*Fix:* Scoped an as-earn-grid class that gives paired labels a shared min-height and bottom-aligns the controls (reset at phone width)
- [ ] **Ava assistant invisible on mobile/dark** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The Ava assistant was white-on-white and unreadable in dark mode on phones.  <br>*Cause:* Hard-coded #fff surfaces gave zero contrast on the hero stage, reply bubbles, and chips.  <br>*Fix:* Fixed three contrast bugs and added a real mobile layout for the Ava page.
- [ ] **Ava chat bubbles rendered 35px wide with invisible white text: CSS class collision between message modifier .ava and avatar .ava** `ui`
  *Symptom:* One character per line in the conversation and white-on-white text in light mode  <br>*Cause:* message div was class="ava-msg ava" and the avatar rule .ava{width:38px;color:#fff} matched it too  <br>*Fix:* renamed modifiers to is-ava / is-me and rewrote the bubble CSS; chat is now full width (~549px bubbles, dark text)
- [ ] **Ava chat bubbles rendered about five blank lines (intro bubble 182px tall for two lines of text)** `ui`
  *Symptom:* Every Ava message bubble had large empty gaps; the intro bubble was 182px and 7 lines tall for 2 lines of text.  <br>*Cause:* The avaMsg template literal emitted newline+indent whitespace between empty conditional blocks such as ${m.proposal?...:''}. .ava-msg .bubble2 uses white-space:pre-wrap (intentional, so AI line breaks survive), so every literal newline in the template became a visible blank line. White-space:pre-wrap combined with a multi-line template literal is the general trap.  <br>*Fix:* Build the inner pieces by string concatenation with no literal newlines; the bubble dropped to 54px / 2 lines. Found by measuring childNodes and scrollHeight rather than by eye.
- [ ] **Brand-deal logos silently never loaded (Clearbit logo API is dead) and white favicons were invisible; verification passed anyway** `ui`
  *Symptom:* Deal cards showed only the monogram fallback; the user asked for actual logos. After switching source, Glossier's white-on-transparent favicon was invisible on a white tile.  <br>*Cause:* logo.clearbit.com (free Logo API) was shut down, so every img 404'd into the fallback. The verification script only asserted the img src string existed, so it passed while the visual was broken. White favicons cannot be detected programmatically because canvas getImageData is blocked by CORS on a cross-origin image. Layering the favicon over a colour monogram was rejected by the user as unprofessional.  <br>*Fix:* Moved to DuckDuckGo/unavatar icon sources tested with an Image() onload probe, shown on a clean white tile with a neutral grey-letter fallback.
- [ ] **Broken Followers/Following/Photo grid** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The Followers/Following and Photo layout was mis-arranged.  <br>*Cause:* The grid pairing was wrong.  <br>*Fix:* Made Followers/Following a proper pair with Photo on its own row.
- [ ] **Business tier badge rendered washed-out and see-through** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The BUSINESS badge next to the name showed as a pale, blurry, semi-transparent blob with unreadable white text  <br>*Cause:* .tb-biz combined backdrop-filter:blur(4px) with a pale translucent gradient, frosting whatever was behind it and killing text contrast  <br>*Fix:* Removed the backdrop-filter and deepened the gradient to saturated colours with solid white text
- [ ] **Calendar week view packed 6 simultaneous events into ~15px-wide unreadable columns** `ui`
  *Symptom:* Six same-time follow-ups at 9am rendered as slivers  <br>*Cause:* column packing gave every overlapping event its own column with no cap  <br>*Fix:* cap at 3 columns (2 shown plus a '+N more' chip that opens the day), as Finished. does
- [ ] **Client portal status pill collided with the 'Updated just now' line (two inline-flex siblings on one row)** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The ON TRACK eyebrow sat beside 'Updated just now' instead of above the headline. The user flagged it; the assistant's earlier screenshot review had called the page 'exactly the spec'.  <br>*Cause:* Both .from and .hero .eyebrow were display:inline-flex, so adjacent siblings flowed on one line.  <br>*Fix:* .from{display:flex;justify-content:center}; the eyebrow became a centred pill on its own row.
- [ ] **Clients-signed ring misaligned** `ui`
  *Symptom:* Ring numerals sat on different baselines and the 'clients signed' label was small and off-center.  <br>*Cause:* Numerator/denominator and label were not aligned or sized correctly.  <br>*Fix:* Aligned numerals on one baseline with a smaller denominator and centered/enlarged the label.
- [ ] **Confetti persists under tab throttling** `ui`
  *Symptom:* Confetti outlived its welcome, lingering after it should have cleared.  <br>*Cause:* The confetti animation was not cleaned up when the browser throttled the background tab.  <br>*Fix:* Cleared the confetti so it no longer outlives its animation.
- [ ] **Contact card not full width** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The rich-contact card rendered narrower than the container.  <br>*Cause:* A helper constrained the card width.  <br>*Fix:* Fixed the helper; card is now full width (689px).
- [ ] **Currency picker button printed the full currency name and ate the whole value row, leaving no room to type an amount** `ui`
  *Symptom:* In the add-lead 'Potential value' field the picker read 'TTD Trinidad & Tobago Dollar' and filled the row; the amount input was squeezed to nothing so the user could not enter money next to the currency sign.  <br>*Cause:* .cur-pick-btn renders <b>code</b> <span>name (max-width:150px)</span> <i>chevron</i>; in a narrow grid cell the button grows to about 230px and the flex sibling (the amount input) gets the leftover sliver.  <br>*Fix:* Scope a rule to the picker+amount row that hides the name span (code-only 'USD >') and gives the amount input flex:1 with a min-width; keep the full name only in the search sheet.
- [ ] **Dark-mode hero/aurora + a dozen surfaces stay light (invisible text)** `ui`
  *Symptom:* In dark mode the hero/aurora card and many other surfaces stayed white, making their text invisible.  <br>*Cause:* .hero had a hardcoded background:#fff and only .card/.modal/.sheet had dark overrides — .hero/.profile-hero/.pstats/.call-stage/.rolo-card/.cmdk/.drawer/.onb had none.  <br>*Fix:* Added dark-mode overrides for all the uncovered surfaces so every card is readable in dark mode.
- [ ] **Demo portraits mismatch names and gender** `ui`
  *Symptom:* Demo lead photos didn't match the leads' names or gender.  <br>*Cause:* Photos used random `i.pravatar.cc/?u=` URLs.  <br>*Fix:* Replaced with gender-inferred `randomuser.me` portraits via name-set heuristics.
- [ ] **e-business-card preview collapsed to a 2px sliver** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The e-card live preview rendered as a 2px strip with its contents out of flow  <br>*Cause:* .ecard has overflow:hidden and, as a flex item in the modal's flex column, that zeroes its auto min-height so it collapses  <br>*Fix:* Set the card to flex:none so it keeps its intrinsic height
- [ ] **Ecommerce currency pickers were plain selects while the rest of the app had a searchable one** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The user asked for every currency list to be searchable and voice-searchable; the ecommerce product and order forms still used a dropdown of codes.  <br>*Cause:* epCur/eoCur in ecom.js used a plain <select> built from curOpts instead of curPickBtn/openCurPick from currency.js.  <br>*Fix:* Swapped to curPickBtn with a hidden input; added a voice mic (SpeechRecognition) to the shared picker.
- [ ] **Fixed-position notification heart sat 10 to 12px off the topbar pills and moved when the async weather chip loaded** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The heart centred at y=50 against pills at y=60 once the weather chip had loaded; three successive hard-coded top offsets (18, 30, 40px) each measured differently because the topbar height changes after first paint and per page  <br>*Cause:* The cluster is position:fixed with a literal top value while the pills live in a topbar whose height depends on async content, so no constant is right for every state  <br>*Fix:* Open caveat: place the button inside the topbar flow (scrolls with the header) or compute its top from the topbar's measured bottom after the weather chip settles; for now it is tuned to the settled desktop layout
- [ ] **Greeting h1 collapsed to a ~120px column with the sidebar open; the first fix made it worse** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The dashboard greeting 'Good afternoon, Amara' wrapped to 3 lines in a ~120px column at desktop widths once the left sidebar was expanded. Separately the topbar pushed its buttons and the weather chip off the right edge.  <br>*Cause:* The greeting h1 was display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap, which reports its min-content width to the parent, so the title shrank to its narrowest word. The topbar used a flex:1 spacer plus wrap, so the buttons overflowed. The first attempt (flex-wrap/min-width/text-wrap:balance on .topbar) squeezed it further. Media queries cannot catch this because the sidebar narrows the content without changing the viewport width.  <br>*Fix:* Greeting became a normal block h1 with an inline badge; .topbar got flex-wrap with the title allowed to grow, buttons wrapping and max-width:100%.
- [ ] **Header buttons overflow, blocking Add to-do** `ui`
  *Symptom:* On a phone the 'Add to-do' button was clipped off the right edge, so users couldn't add a goal/to-do.  <br>*Cause:* `.topbar` had no `flex-wrap`, so the action buttons overflowed on narrow screens.  <br>*Fix:* Added flex-wrap to `.topbar`, fixing every view that uses the pattern.
- [ ] **Microphone icon not centered in inputs** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The mic sat near the top line instead of centered in goal, to-do and other fields.  <br>*Cause:* The mic was centered on the combined label+field height instead of just the field.  <br>*Fix:* Re-centered every mic in the app so each measures 0px off-center.
- [ ] **Mobile FAB overlaps the 5-tab bottom bar** `ui`
