- [ ] **wrangler d1 create failed at the account's database-count cap while a rename plan assumed an unlimited number of databases** `other`
  *Symptom:* Renaming CocoMail to Joy needed a new D1 database and the create call answered 'On the Workers Free plan? Upgrade to create more'; the session had to delete its own empty database and recreate it, and had already created the new R2 bucket and worker  <br>*Cause:* The rename was planned as create-new-then-delete-old, which needs one spare database; the account was at its cap with many apps each holding a database  <br>*Fix:* Check wrangler d1 list against the cap before any rename, create the replacement first only when there is room, and prefer reusing a database or prefixing tables over adding one per tiny app
- [ ] **wrangler d1 migrations apply replays old migrations when earlier ones were applied with d1 execute (empty d1_migrations table)** `other`
  *Symptom:* Applying migration 007 failed because 006 re-ran and its column already existed; the tracking table was empty.  <br>*Cause:* All previous migrations had been run with 'wrangler d1 execute --file', which does not record them in d1_migrations, so 'migrations apply' thought none were applied.  <br>*Fix:* Backfilled d1_migrations rows for 001-006, then applied 007; pick one mechanism per database from day one.
- [ ] **wrangler email sending returns Unauthorized 2036 on every call even after a fresh login adds the email_sending scope, until the product is activated in the dashboard** `other`
  *Symptom:* Both 'enable' and a plain 'list' failed with code 2036 after wrangler whoami listed email_sending (write); only a one-time dashboard activation (Email, Email Sending, Get started) followed by wrangler email sending enable worked  <br>*Cause:* The error reads as a scope problem but the real gate is an account-level beta activation, so repeated re-logins do nothing  <br>*Fix:* On 2036 with the scope present, send the user to the dashboard activation first; then enable the zone, check DKIM, SPF and DMARC records, and add the send_email binding
- [ ] **wrangler r2 object get writes a 0-byte file because it defaults to local simulated storage unless --remote is passed** `other`
  *Symptom:* Pulling a stored .eml to check its Authentication-Results header produced an empty file and no error, so the first check appeared to find no headers  <br>*Cause:* Newer wrangler resolves r2 object commands against the local simulator by default  <br>*Fix:* Pass --remote when reading production objects and test the byte count before parsing
- [ ] **zsh does not word-split an unquoted $VAR in a for loop: the repo loop ran once with the whole string, so cd failed and nothing was pushed** `other`
  *Symptom:* for r in $REPOS; do cd $HOME/$r ... done printed CD_FAIL after one iteration under the Bash tool's zsh  <br>*Cause:* zsh keeps $REPOS as a single word unlike bash (needs ${=REPOS} or an array)  <br>*Fix:* moved the loop into a bash script file and re-ran; use arrays or run multi-repo loops under bash
- [ ] **zsh loop variable named path clobbers PATH so every later command is not found** `other`
  *Symptom:* After a for path in ... loop in zsh, tail, python3 and other tools failed with command not found for the rest of that shell session.  <br>*Cause:* In zsh the lowercase array path is tied to the uppercase PATH, so assigning the loop variable rewrites PATH.  <br>*Fix:* Never use path (or the other tied names cdpath, fpath, manpath) as a loop or scratch variable in zsh; use p or file_path.
- [ ] **zsh unquoted --include=*.js aborts grep with 'no matches found' and reads as 'not found'** `other`
  *Symptom:* Searches like `grep -rniE 'x' --include=*.js .` printed '(eval):N: no matches found: --include=*.js', leading the agent to conclude strings did not exist when they did.  <br>*Cause:* zsh expands the unquoted glob *.js before grep runs; if no file in cwd matches, zsh aborts the whole command (nomatch).  <br>*Fix:* Quote globs (--include='*.js'), prefer rg or a node one-liner, and treat empty grep output as 'search failed' until stderr is checked.
- [ ] **zsh: using `path` as a read/loop variable overwrites $PATH, so every later command is 'command not found: git/curl'** `other`
  *Symptom:* A verification loop (`while IFS=$'\t' read -r slug path; do git show HEAD:worker.js ...`) printed 'command not found: git' on every iteration and reported all 34 images 'missing'; a second session's `path=${pair#*:}` loop then broke curl the same way. The misleading output was first dismissed as a 'PATH quirk'.  <br>*Cause:* In zsh, `path` is a special array tied to $PATH (as are fpath, cdpath, manpath, module_path). Assigning to a variable named path replaces PATH for the rest of that shell/function, so external commands stop resolving.  <br>*Fix:* Rename the variable (pth, rel, p2). When 'command not found' appears for a command that worked seconds earlier, echo $PATH before debugging anything else. Hit in two separate sessions on 2026-09-08 and 2026-10-01.

## Aprizely  ·  38 fixed

- [ ] **Account switcher returns the raw session token in login JSON and stores it in localStorage for 60 days (also in Breadcrumb)** `security`
  *Symptom:* A single XSS can read every remembered account's session token and take over those accounts.  <br>*Cause:* The switcher design needs tokens on the device and keeps them in script-readable storage, undoing the HttpOnly cookie protection; accepted as a risk during the session.  <br>*Fix:* Keep switch tokens server-side keyed by an opaque device id in an HttpOnly cookie, or store them encrypted with a per-device key and require a PIN to switch.
- [ ] **javascript:/data: URL scheme click-XSS via stored and AI-sourced links** `security`
  *Symptom:* A live_url/repo_url/pitch_url — or an AI-sourced competitor URL — set to javascript:… became a clickable href that ran script on click  <br>*Cause:* URL fields were rendered into href using esc() only, which escapes quotes but does not validate the URL scheme  <br>*Fix:* Added a safeUrl() guard (http/https only, else '#') applied across all 9 href sites, including AI competitor links
- [ ] **Public handle-availability endpoint lets anyone enumerate existing account handles** `security`
  *Symptom:* An unauthenticated signup helper reports whether a handle is taken, and a code comment accepts that handles are semi-public. Any script can probe candidate handles and learn which accounts exist, including the owner's account.  <br>*Cause:* The availability check returns an exact taken-or-free answer with no authentication or CAPTCHA, and the handle doubles as the login identifier on an app whose only secret is a 5 to 6 digit passcode.  <br>*Fix:* Keep the check but give it a low per-IP budget, check availability only at submit time where possible, and rely on the existing lockout so a known handle is not a cheap first step; consider a generic response for taken handles on the login screen.
- [ ] **Temporary account passcode echoed into chat and tool output and passed on the command line** `security`
  *Symptom:* While seeding the owner's account, the generated 6-digit temporary passcode was printed by cat of seed/.passcode.txt, passed as a process argument to seed.mjs, and repeated in the final chat message, so it now sits in the session transcript.  <br>*Cause:* Credential handling optimised for convenience: the value was generated in the shell, written in plaintext to a gitignored file and then read back and quoted to show the user.  <br>*Fix:* Generate the passcode, write it to a mode-600 file and print only the path; pass it by environment variable or stdin instead of argv; force a change on first login; never quote it in the reply.
- [ ] **Inherited 20,000-character clamp on note text would silently cut a long dictation** `data-loss`
  *Symptom:* A dictation longer than about 25 minutes of speech would be truncated on save while the UI promised the exact transcript was kept; the voice-note recorder also stopped at 5 minutes.  <br>*Cause:* The notes routes used clean(body.text, 20000) and a 5-minute recorder cap carried over from short journal entries.  <br>*Fix:* Raise note text to 200,000 characters, the summariser input to 100,000 and the recording cap to 60 minutes (done).
- [ ] **Demo mode threw 'Cannot access id before initialization' when logging a cost or payment** `crash`
  *Symptom:* In ?demo, POST /costs and POST /payments raised a ReferenceError.  <br>*Cause:* The demo api() stub declared const id after the handlers that use it (temporal dead zone).  <br>*Fix:* Hoist const id to the top of the DEMO block. Found by exercising the new payment sort in demo.
- [ ] **One malformed report blanked the entire Reports list** `crash`
  *Symptom:* The whole Reports view rendered blank when a single report had a string (not array) tasks field  <br>*Cause:* reportCard assumed well-formed data; one bad row threw and aborted rendering of the entire list  <br>*Fix:* Make reportCard defensive so a malformed report degrades gracefully and never breaks the list
- [ ] **A 22-app, 285-log bulk sync landed on the wrong account because the saved agent token belonged to the seed user** `auth`
  *Symptom:* The portfolio sync and report appeared under @rhema while the user was looking at @rhe.  <br>*Cause:* The saved agent token whoami resolved to the seed account and nothing checked it against the account the user sees before bulk writes.  <br>*Fix:* Run whoami and compare with the expected handle before any bulk write; show the handle in the CLI output of start and finish.
- [ ] **Agent token and board state shared one file (~/.aprizely.json): state writes clobbered the token** `auth`
  *Symptom:* apz.mjs reported 'no token' after a board start/step because state overwrote the saved token.  <br>*Cause:* Both token and per-session state were written to the same JSON path; a state write replaced the whole file.  <br>*Fix:* Keep token and state in separate files, merge on write, and never replace the credential file from the state path.
- [ ] **/app HTML shell edge-cached with no Cache-Control, so the plain URL kept pointing at the old /_v path after deploy** `sync`
  *Symptom:* After deploying ASSET_VER r53, plain /app still referenced /_v/r52/app.js while /app?cb=... returned r53.  <br>*Cause:* serveHtml() sets Content-Type but no cache header on the rewritten shell, so the edge cached it.  <br>*Fix:* Verify with a cache-busted fetch. The durable fix is Cache-Control: no-store on the rewritten HTML (as done on Evertrue).
- [ ] **app.js changes shipped without bumping ASSET_VER, so cached browsers kept the old build** `sync`
  *Symptom:* The new KPI tile was missing in the pane after deploy, and the previous task's 'Work & reports' panel had never reached cached browsers either.  <br>*Cause:* The shell rewrites assets to immutable /_v/<ASSET_VER>/file paths (ASSET_VER='r52' in worker.js) and the constant must be bumped on every asset change. The saved memory note wrongly said to bump ?v= in app.html, which the rewrite ignores.  <br>*Fix:* Bumped ASSET_VER to r53 and corrected the memory note. The constant could instead be derived from a content hash at deploy.
- [ ] **Worklog state keyed by cwd is shared by concurrent sessions run from the same directory** `race`
  *Symptom:* From ~, apz done N returned 'done <index> out of range' and the board tab showed 0/13. The shared ~/.aprizely.worklog.json held another session's Capital Signal board, so this session's ticks were applied to, or rejected by, the wrong board.  <br>*Cause:* State lives in <cwd>/.aprizely.worklog.json, so two sessions started in the same directory overwrite each other's active board.  <br>*Fix:* Workaround used: cd into a dedicated project dir and rebuild the state file. Proper fix: key state by session id (APZ_SESSION env or --id) or refuse start when an unfinished board exists in that cwd.
- [ ] **A payment added today did not appear at the top of the ledger and the root cause was never found** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Owner logged a payment and could not find it in the list.  <br>*Cause:* UNCONFIRMED. The ledger sliced the first 12 rows in API order and there was no way to set the paid date; the session added a belt-and-braces sort rather than reproducing the original.  <br>*Fix:* Render sorts a copy of S.payments by at descending before slice(0, 12), the payment sheet has a date field (default today, timezone-corrected) and shows a 'today' badge. Reproduce with real data to confirm.
- [ ] **Account totals computed client-side from a capped page of rows undercount** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Any sum of runs or reports in the browser could only reflect the 20 worklogs and 60 reports that /api/state returned, against 205 and 191 stored.  <br>*Cause:* /api/state paginates worklogs and reports for payload size, and the client summed what it had loaded.  <br>*Fix:* Added a server-side lifetime aggregate (runs, reports, work_ms) to /api/state as S.totals, with a client fallback for demo mode. Also raised the worklogs limit from 20 to 100 so project runs load.
- [ ] **Account work total inflated to 1148h by boards left 'active'** `logic`
  *Symptom:* The first server aggregate of work logged read 1148h across 208 runs.  <br>*Cause:* Duration was computed as updated minus started. 24 boards were left active, so updated kept moving and spans ran to days (max 292h).  <br>*Fix:* Cap each run at 4h in both the SQL aggregate and agentSessionDur, giving 233h47m. A run's duration should come from finished where present.
- [ ] **Agent API matches project names exactly, so Bug Ledger created a duplicate of the seeded BugLedger card with no way to merge** `logic`
  *Symptom:* A second Bug Ledger card appeared next to BugLedger and the agent API has no rename or delete.  <br>*Cause:* Project lookup is a case-insensitive exact match on name and the API is append-only.  <br>*Fix:* Normalise names (strip spaces and punctuation) or accept an alias list in project resolution, and add an owner-only merge in the app.
- [ ] **apz start --tasks silently splits notes on ';' and '|', creating stray tasks that shift every index** `logic`
  *Symptom:* A note such as 'Diagnose::...(HTTP 200); returning-user boot awaits /me...' became its own task. Boards came out with 7 tasks instead of 6, 8 instead of 7 and 16 instead of 6, so done <index> ticked the wrong task or went out of range.  <br>*Cause:* The task-string parser treats ';' (and the documented '|') as separators even inside the text after '::', and gives no warning or escape syntax.  <br>*Fix:* Workaround: avoid ';' and '|' in notes and inspect .aprizely.worklog.json indices after start. Proper fix: only split on '|' outside a note, support an escape, and print the parsed task list on start.
- [ ] **apz step used as a read probe re-opened a finished task, and done indices ignore #Wave headers** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Running step 0 to dump state dropped progress from 15/23 to 14/23, and done 2 ticked a different task than expected.  <br>*Cause:* The CLI has no read-only task listing, step writes, and indices count tasks while skipping wave header lines.  <br>*Fix:* Add apz tasks (read-only, prints index and status), make step on a done task a no-op, and document that indices exclude headers.
- [ ] **Board time was misleading: flat 15m/task estimate beside seconds of real work, and an idle active wave kept its real clock ticking (22m for a 5s step)** `logic`
  *Symptom:* A 5-second wave read '~30m' and the header claimed '~1h est' for seconds of work; a demo board left with an active wave showed 22m 14s of 'real' time long after nothing was happening.  <br>*Cause:* Auto-estimate used a fixed 15 minutes per task, never learning from completed tasks; the elapsed clock used Date.now() for any non-finished board, so abandoned boards inflated forever.  <br>*Fix:* Adaptive ETA from the mean duration of completed tasks (in-progress task counts down), falling back to a (~Nm) token then the default; real clock freezes when the board has been idle more than 3 minutes since the last update; done waves show actual only, active shows actual + ~left, pending shows ~estimate. Verified live on the stale board (22m collapsed to 2s).
- [ ] **Break-even card said 'covers 100%' when revenue was 152% of costs** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Finance dashboard displayed 'covers 100%' even though MRR was 152% of monthly costs  <br>*Cause:* Coverage copy capped/miscomputed the revenue-to-cost ratio instead of reporting the real percentage  <br>*Fix:* Compute and show the true coverage ratio (e.g. 152%) rather than a hardcoded/clamped 100%
- [ ] **Costs could not carry a renewal anchor: costCols never read b.started and createCost always stamped now()** `logic`
  *Symptom:* No way to say when a subscription first charged, so next-charge dates could not be computed or shown; Costs and Stack were one table mixing paid and free tools.  <br>*Cause:* The started column existed but the API ignored it on create and update.  <br>*Fix:* costCols reads started, createCost uses c.started || now(), PATCH accepts it; nextCharge/renewLabel roll the date forward by cycle (shown as 'Sep 2 in 7d', due ones highlighted); a 'first charged' field stores noon to dodge timezone day shift; Costs tab shows paid items only and Stack lists all tools with a 'free' chip.
- [ ] **DELETE /api/account confirmation body was never read because the worker parsed JSON only for POST and PATCH** `logic`
  *Symptom:* The client sends the delete-account confirmation in the body of a DELETE request, but the worker ignored it, so the confirm check could not work as designed.  <br>*Cause:* The request-body parser was gated on request.method being POST or PATCH, so DELETE requests reached the handler with an empty body object.  <br>*Fix:* Parse the JSON body for DELETE as well, inside the same size-capped block (done), or move the confirmation into the URL. Add a test that every destructive route rejects a missing confirm value.
- [ ] **Live board showed no per-wave time on any board without (~Nm) tokens because tasks carried no start/done timestamps** `logic`
  *Symptom:* After a per-wave/total ETA feature shipped, normal boards (no (~25m) token in labels) still showed no per-wave time, which looked like 'the old version is still running' even though the feature was committed and deployed.  <br>*Cause:* The display only parsed (~25m) tokens from labels. The worklog row stored only board-level started/updated/finished, so real elapsed time per wave could not be computed.  <br>*Fix:* Server now stamps startedAt/doneAt onto each task inside the existing tasks JSON as statuses change (merged by index from the previous row, no D1 migration); the board derives per-wave real elapsed; /api/agent/report pulls the same stamped timestamps so the published report groups the timeline by wave with real times. Proven live on a token-less board.
- [ ] **Live-board ETA rollup undercounted when a wave mixed a wave-level token with a few task tokens** `logic`
  *Symptom:* The header would have shown ~8m total instead of ~60m on the board that carried both kinds of token.  <br>*Cause:* etaMins was taskEta||waveEta, so a partial set of task estimates silently overrode the wave estimate. Caught before shipping.  <br>*Fix:* Wave token wins when present (waveEta||taskEta) and the total is the sum of per-wave values; per-task badges still show on each row.
- [ ] **Per-project and weekly time ignored agent run durations** `logic`
  *Symptom:* The Work Log 'This week' bars and the per-project totals were empty even with 205 logged agent runs.  <br>*Cause:* wlProjectTotals() and wlWeek() summed only manual work_sessions rows, and that table had 0 rows. Agent runs live in worklogs (finished/updated minus started), so they were never counted.  <br>*Fix:* Added agentSessionDur(worklog) to both functions and a 'Work & reports' panel (time, runs, reports with links) to the project modal.
- [ ] **Report ship card says 'Deployed: Not deployed yet' for Cloudflare deploys** `logic`
  *Symptom:* A report published after a verified wrangler deploy still showed 'Not deployed yet'.  <br>*Cause:* apz report derives the Deployed flag from git state only and cannot see a Workers deploy unless a deploy flag is passed.  <br>*Fix:* Open. Either pass the deploy flag explicitly, or have apz read the last wrangler version/timestamp when building the ship card.
- [ ] **Work Log tab was empty despite weeks of agent work: it only showed personal timers while agent boards live in a separate store** `logic`
  *Symptom:* The owner saw nothing in Work Log although boards and reports existed.  <br>*Cause:* worklogHTML rendered S.sessions (work_sessions start/stop timers) and never S.worklogs (the apz.mjs boards, shown only in Board view); two session stores were never joined.  <br>*Fix:* Work Log now lists every agent session itemised by wave (each task with its description), linked to its project, timestamped with duration and live/report links, plus a per-day work-time analysis that merges timers, agent sessions and payments, with the week graph at the top.
- [ ] **worker.js is classed as binary data, so plain grep silently returned zero matches** `observability`
  *Symptom:* grep for 'worklog' and 'live' in the 77KB, 1314-line src/worker.js returned nothing, while the file clearly serves /live.  <br>*Cause:* file(1) reports the source as 'data' (non-text bytes), so grep treats it as binary and suppresses matches.  <br>*Fix:* Use grep -a. Find and remove the stray control bytes so the source is text again.
- [ ] **Activity timeline dumped the entire status_note field** `ui`
  *Symptom:* status_note edits printed the whole note into the activity timeline, cluttering it  <br>*Cause:* Timeline rendered raw field values with no length cap  <br>*Fix:* Truncate long field values in the activity event builder so entries stay tidy
- [ ] **Bulk rename introduced a grammar slip ('a account')** `ui`
  *Symptom:* A bulk find-replace of 'studio' → 'account' produced 'a account' where the article should agree ('an account').  <br>*Cause:* Blind string replace doesn't fix the preceding article; 'a/an' depends on the following word's sound.  <br>*Fix:* Corrected 'a account' → 'an account' and verified the copy renders live; left the internal importStudio() function name (never user-visible) untouched. Lesson: after a bulk rename, grep for 'a <newword>' article mismatches.
- [ ] **Negative currency rendered as $-59.17 instead of minus-$59.17** `ui`
  *Symptom:* Negative money values showed the minus sign after the dollar sign  <br>*Cause:* Money formatter prefixed the symbol then let the numeric minus fall inside  <br>*Fix:* Format negatives with the minus before the currency symbol in the shared money formatter
- [ ] **New spend markers rendered '$ GBP23' because money() takes a currency symbol and the code passed a nonexistent S.user.currency defaulting to 'GBP'** `ui`
  *Symptom:* Demo render of the new work-time analysis showed a doubled symbol and ISO code.  <br>*Cause:* money(n, cur) expects the symbol; the user field is baseCurrency; a decorative icon also duplicated the symbol.  <br>*Fix:* Use S.user.baseCurrency || '$' and drop the icon. Caught only because the demo render was inspected.
- [ ] **New token button did nothing when the browser suppressed window.prompt()** `ui`
  *Symptom:* Clicking New token in Settings produced no prompt and no error in embedded browsers.  <br>*Cause:* The handler returned early when prompt() returned null, and suppressed prompts also return null.  <br>*Fix:* Replace prompt() with an inline form (done) and never branch on prompt() alone.
- [ ] **Unlock keypad numbers float high and misalign** `ui`
  *Symptom:* On the unlock PIN pad the numbers sat too high inside their circles and 1/0 didn't line up with the lettered keys — it still looked off after a first tidy pass.  <br>*Cause:* Each key reserved a letter row (ABC/DEF/…), which pushed the number upward instead of centering it; the digit wasn't positioned independently of the letters.  <br>*Fix:* Centered each number dead-centre in the key and absolute-positioned the letters tucked at the bottom; added a hairline border + subtle fill, tighter spacing and a smoother press state; also relabeled 'Create a studio' → 'Create account' on the unlock screen.
- [ ] **Control-character regex was saved with literal control bytes, which broke edits and risks bundler and git trouble** `other`
  *Symptom:* The clean() sanitiser line in worker.js showed as a mangled character class under cat -v (raw NUL, BS and VT bytes inside the regex), and an Edit whose old_string was copied from it failed with string not found.  <br>*Cause:* The regex was written through a file-writing tool that expanded the backslash-u escapes into the actual control characters instead of keeping the escape text, so the source contained invisible bytes.  <br>*Fix:* Rewrote the line with escaped forms (u0000-u0008, u000b, u000c, u000e-u001f written as escape sequences) using a small script and verified with grep -c u0000. Add a pre-commit or deploy check that fails on any control byte other than tab, LF and CR in source files.
- [ ] **npx --no-install wrangler deploy fails for a project pinning a wrangler version that is not installed locally, and a grep-filtered deploy hid the failure** `other`
  *Symptom:* Aprizely's SEO tag deploy printed nothing useful under grep -iE 'Uploaded|Deployed|Version ID'; re-running showed 'npx canceled due to missing packages and no YES option: wrangler@4.145.0'.  <br>*Cause:* --no-install refuses the pinned wrangler that is not in node_modules, and the output filter only matched success words, so the failure looked like an empty success.  <br>*Fix:* Use npx --yes wrangler deploy (or install the pinned version) and include 'error|✘' in the grep; confirm a Current Version ID line before saying deployed.
- [ ] **schema.sql no longer matches the live D1 database (columns and tables added by hand)** `other`
  *Symptom:* The repo schema.sql lacks columns that production relies on (base_currency and categories on users; pitch_url, category and active_users on projects) and has no CREATE TABLE for reports at all, yet the README tells you to run schema.sql once to bootstrap. A fresh deploy or a restore from the repo cannot reproduce production.  <br>*Cause:* Features were shipped by running ad hoc ALTER TABLE and CREATE TABLE statements against the remote D1 with wrangler d1 execute, and schema.sql was only sometimes updated afterwards. There is no migrations folder, no applied-migrations table and no check that compares the repo schema with sqlite_master.  <br>*Fix:* Reconcile schema.sql with sqlite_master from the live database now; from then on write every change as a numbered migrations/NNN.sql applied with d1 execute --file, and add a schema:check script that diffs PRAGMA table_info against schema.sql and runs before deploy.
- [ ] **Worker serving index.html through the ASSETS binding looped on 307 redirects, and the 307 stayed edge-cached** `other`
  *Symptom:* Right after the first deploy, / and /app returned an empty 307 instead of the page. /index.html redirected to /, the Worker handles / first (run_worker_first) and fetches /index.html again, so the pretty-URL redirect bounced. After html_handling was set to none, plain /app kept returning the cached 307 for a while while the same URL with a cache-busting query string already returned 200.  <br>*Cause:* The default assets html_handling rewrites /index.html and /app.html to their pretty URLs, which are exactly the paths listed in run_worker_first, so the Worker's own env.ASSETS.fetch call is redirected back to itself. The redirect response is cacheable at the edge, so a corrected config still serves the stale 307 until it expires.  <br>*Fix:* Set assets.html_handling to none in wrangler.json whenever the Worker serves HTML from ASSETS for run_worker_first paths, and fetch the explicit .html asset path. After deploying, check the plain URL with curl -sI and again with a cache-busting query string; if only the plain URL still shows a 307, wait or purge rather than redeploying.

## Stitchhooky  ·  38 fixed

- [ ] **Co-stitch rooms and theme leak across accounts** `security`
  *Symptom:* The next person on the device auto-joined your live co-stitch room and kept your theme.  <br>*Cause:* Co-stitch room membership and appearance were not scoped per account.  <br>*Fix:* Scoped co-stitch rooms and appearance per account so they no longer leak between users.
- [ ] **Security headers silently ignored** `security`
  *Symptom:* Security/CSP headers added in the worker did nothing.  <br>*Cause:* Cloudflare serves static assets before the worker runs, so worker-set headers never applied.  <br>*Fix:* Moved the headers to the proper `_headers` mechanism.
- [ ] **Stored-XSS vectors in user content** `security`
  *Symptom:* Multiple stored-XSS injection points, including an unescaped server value reaching an HTML attribute.  <br>*Cause:* User/server values were rendered without escaping.  <br>*Fix:* Escaped the values and closed all five stored-XSS vectors.
- [ ] **'Reset everything' resurrects data on reload** `data-loss`
  *Symptom:* Resetting everything did not stick; data came back on the next reload.  <br>*Cause:* Reset left the rolling backup behind, and the loss-protection loader (which prefers whichever copy has more projects) resurrected everything.  <br>*Fix:* Made reset also clear the rolling backup so it no longer resurrects.
- [ ] **Cross-account data leak on shared device** `data-loss`
  *Symptom:* A second person signing up on the same device saw the first user's projects and streak.  <br>*Cause:* Accounts were stored in a single storage slot with no per-account scoping.  <br>*Fix:* Scoped storage per account so a new user starts empty (proven: Bea sees zero projects).
- [ ] **Duplicate chart projects on every reload** `data-loss`
  *Symptom:* Reloading created a brand-new duplicate of the same chart project (three identically-named ones appeared).  <br>*Cause:* `savedId` was never persisted, so each reload treated the chart as new.  <br>*Fix:* Persisted savedId so a chart maps to one project.
- [ ] **Projects view wiped by a single corrupt save** `data-loss`
  *Symptom:* The projects view went blank with no warning.  <br>*Cause:* `load()` silently fell back to empty defaults on any parse error, so one corrupt/truncated save destroyed everything.  <br>*Fix:* Added backups and recovery (preserve the broken copy) and made each card render independently so one bad project can't blank the list.
- [ ] **Resize wipes the canvas artwork** `data-loss`
  *Symptom:* Resizing the grid erased the user's drawing.  <br>*Cause:* The resize path cleared the canvas instead of preserving it.  <br>*Fix:* Resize now preserves existing artwork.
- [ ] **Sign-up orphans or clobbers pre-account data** `data-loss`
  *Symptom:* Signing up could vanish pre-account projects or clobber an existing local store for a handle.  <br>*Cause:* Pre-account projects were stored behind an ownerless key and sign-up did not adopt or protect them.  <br>*Fix:* Pre-account projects are adopted by the first signer, and sign-up refuses a handle with an existing store and points to sign-in.
- [ ] **Boot crash from temporal dead zone** `crash`
  *Symptom:* The entire app died on first load with no console error.  <br>*Cause:* `termKey` read `db` while `let db = load()` was still initializing (TDZ).  <br>*Fix:* Reordered initialization so `db` exists before it is referenced.
- [ ] **HUD crash on second render** `crash`
  *Symptom:* Every count after the first would crash.  <br>*Cause:* `renderCover` referenced an element its own rebuild had already destroyed.  <br>*Fix:* Reworked renderCover to not reference the destroyed element.
- [ ] **LS_BAK temporal dead zone crash** `crash`
  *Symptom:* The app refused to boot.  <br>*Cause:* `LS_BAK` was declared after `db=load()` ran, a use-before-initialisation TDZ.  <br>*Fix:* Moved the declaration before it is used.
- [ ] **Passcode lockout with no escape hatch** `auth`
  *Symptom:* User was asked for a passcode they never set and could not get in.  <br>*Cause:* A browser test left a lock flag in storage and there was no recovery path.  <br>*Fix:* Added a forgot-passcode escape hatch (fixed three ways) and cleared the stray flag.
- [ ] **Cell-click runs past end of row** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Clicking a cell could advance past the last stitch of a row into the next one.  <br>*Cause:* No bound check on the click-to-advance path.  <br>*Fix:* Clamped cell clicks to the current row's end.
- [ ] **Chart collapses to a tiny thumbnail** `logic`
  *Symptom:* The chart shrank to a thumbnail inside a huge empty page card.  <br>*Cause:* A scale calculation ran while the container was hidden (width read 0, scale went negative).  <br>*Fix:* Removed transform-scaling and made the grid fill the page width with real cells.
- [ ] **Chart-grow message flow miscounts** `logic`
  *Symptom:* The chart stage didn't repaint per message, the prompt offered to add skips instead of stitches, and it grew the wrong row.  <br>*Cause:* Stage wasn't refreshed per message and the grow logic targeted a stale row index / wrong element type.  <br>*Fix:* Repaint per message, name real stitches, and grow the correct row (verified 30→32).
- [ ] **Community tab is a dead end** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Community refused with a toast telling the user to sign in via Settings.  <br>*Cause:* It gated on a sign-in path that led nowhere.  <br>*Fix:* Fixed the Community entry so it no longer dead-ends.
- [ ] **Competing writers miscount home tiles** `logic`
  *Symptom:* The home tile still counted 13 stitches from a stale value.  <br>*Cause:* Two ppMeta writers fought over the home tile count and the stale one won.  <br>*Fix:* Consolidated to a single ppMeta writer.
- [ ] **Dictation mic leak into counting view** `logic`
  *Symptom:* After dictating rows then hitting Save & start counting, speech kept appending rows to a stale draft instead of counting.  <br>*Cause:* The mic stayed live with the dictation handler still attached when entering the counting studio.  <br>*Fix:* Mic now hard-stops on entering the counting studio.
- [ ] **Learn-to-read-charts modal silently fails** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Opening 'Learn to read charts' did nothing visible.  <br>*Cause:* It opened a modal that silently failed.  <br>*Fix:* Fixed the modal so the guide opens correctly.
- [ ] **Magic-ring foundation stitches collapsed to one cell** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Round 1 of nearly every amigurumi ('6 sc in magic ring') counted as a single stitch, undercounting the row.  <br>*Cause:* The parser grouped 'N stitch in <target>' as one clustered cell. That is correct for a shell worked INTO one existing stitch, but wrong for a foundation ring where each stitch is individual.  <br>*Fix:* Distinguished ring/foundation targets (expand to N individual cells) from a cluster worked into a single stitch (stays grouped).
- [ ] **New project would not save and gave no reason: the validation error only appeared on the Type tab and typed-but-unadded row text was discarded** `logic`
  *Symptom:* The owner reported new projects not saving; Save did nothing visible, especially when the user was on another editor tab or had typed a row without pressing Add.  <br>*Cause:* The save handler required at least one row but rendered the error inside the rows tab only (and the free-tier PRO.may('count') paywall gave no feedback); text sitting in the row input was never committed.  <br>*Fix:* Show a visible error beside the Save button, auto-switch to the Type tab and scroll the input into view, and commitPendingRows() so uncommitted input is added before validation.
- [ ] **Notation parser undercounts stitches ('7 vs 18')** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Stitch totals came out far too low, e.g. reporting 7 when it should be 18.  <br>*Cause:* Two parser bugs — e.g. '6 sc in magic ring' collapsed to a single count, and many notation variants weren't recognized.  <br>*Fix:* Parser overhaul plus a `normalizeNotation` that folds standing/stacked/foundation/post/Ntog/magic-ring variants into keys.
- [ ] **Marketing says 'no account, no ads' but the backend has accounts, social and Stripe subscriptions** `claims`
  *Symptom:* The product markets itself as 'no account, no ads', while the backend has full email/password + TOTP accounts, social follows/posts/photos and live Stripe subscriptions ($4/mo, $24/yr).  <br>*Cause:* The privacy-first positioning copy was never updated when accounts, social features and billing were added.  <br>*Fix:* Open: rewrite the claim to match the real data flows and align the privacy policy, or gate accounts as optional. Needs a copy fix plus a source-verified claim review.
- [ ] **Shipped with ~30 high-severity accessibility gaps across the web surface** `a11y`
  *Symptom:* Scanner flagged ~30 HIGH accessibility issues: icon-only controls with no accessible name, images with no alt, inputs with no label.  <br>*Cause:* Accessibility was never a release gate; the six WebAIM-common failures recur.  <br>*Fix:* aria-label every icon button, alt every content image, label every input, set <html lang>; re-run A11Y-* detectors before ship and mirror the fixes to the native surfaces to keep parity.
- [ ] **Billing panel invisible at 0x0** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The billing panel had content but measured 0x0 and never showed.  <br>*Cause:* A sizing rule collapsed the panel (and ten similar panels) to zero dimensions.  <br>*Fix:* One layout rule fixed all eleven; billing now renders at 560x740.
- [ ] **Dead hero photo on landing page** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The landing hero image 404'd, showing a broken tile.  <br>*Cause:* It pointed at an expired Unsplash premium URL.  <br>*Fix:* Swapped to a verified-200 photo and added a fallback that hides any tile whose image fails.
- [ ] **Duplicate DOM ids after bento redesign** `ui`
  *Symptom:* Nine duplicate DOM ids made home-tile buttons behave unpredictably.  <br>*Cause:* Replacing the home markup left the old tile row in place below the new bento.  <br>*Fix:* Removed the leftover old tile row.
- [ ] **iOS text too close to phone edge** `ui`
  *Symptom:* In the iOS simulator, words sat too close to the edge of the phone.  <br>*Cause:* Horizontal padding was below Apple's 16pt margin guidance.  <br>*Fix:* Raised horizontal padding to 22pt with the title at 27pt.
- [ ] **Menu hidden behind next card** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The project menu rendered behind the following card instead of on top.  <br>*Cause:* Z-index stacking-context bug.  <br>*Fix:* Fixed the stacking so the menu layers above adjacent cards.
- [ ] **Mic control runs off screen on real devices** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The mic control ran off the edge of the screen on real devices.  <br>*Cause:* A missing/incorrect viewport meta tag.  <br>*Fix:* Corrected the viewport meta tag.
- [ ] **Mislabeled +n count bubble** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The +n increment bubble showed the wrong number.  <br>*Fix:* Corrected the bubble's label calculation.
- [ ] **Pattern grid cells shrank to a 36px max width with flex:1 and were too small to tap, and count-screen bubbles covered the chart with no way to delete or edit them** `ui`
  *Symptom:* Cells in long rows became tiny and hard to hit; on mobile the transcript bubbles and the dock's opaque gradient covered the chart behind them, and a mis-heard message could not be removed.  <br>*Cause:* The cell rule used flex:1 with a 36px max-width so cells shrank as rows grew, and the feed had no per-message controls while the dock gradient was opaque at the bottom.  <br>*Fix:* Larger tappable cells that wrap (rather than shrink) with per-cell stitch labels and a settings toggle, delete/edit controls on each bubble, a lighter dock gradient and a shorter feed on mobile.
- [ ] **Settings button opened nothing** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The Settings control was genuinely broken and didn't open the panel.  <br>*Cause:* `#settingsSheet` was the backdrop element, not the panel itself.  <br>*Fix:* Corrected the settings sheet structure so the panel opens.
- [ ] **Stale CSS rule squishes knit cells** `ui`
  *Symptom:* Knit grid cells rendered square despite correct code.  <br>*Cause:* A leftover `.gm-cell` CSS rule from the old grid maker silently overrode the new knit geometry.  <br>*Fix:* Removed the stale rule so per-craft geometry applies.
- [ ] **Tile colour clashes with theme accent** `ui`
  *Symptom:* Tile colours (e.g. purple) did not match the theme the user picked.  <br>*Cause:* Tile background wasn't bound to the chosen accent colour.  <br>*Fix:* Tiles now follow the theme's colour and each tile background is picker-controlled.
- [ ] **CSP blocks YouTube thumbnails and embeds** `other`
  *Symptom:* The new CSP would have broken YouTube thumbnails and video embeds.  <br>*Cause:* The content-security-policy was too strict for the embed resources.  <br>*Fix:* Widened the CSP and re-verified zero blocked resources.
- [ ] **Users served stale app version** `other`
  *Symptom:* Returning users silently ran old JavaScript/HTML, making just-added features look missing.  <br>*Cause:* The `?v=` cache-busters weren't regenerated and HTML wasn't revalidated at the cache layer.  <br>*Fix:* Bumped/regenerated the `?v=` stamps and made HTML always revalidate.

## Abba  ·  35 fixed

- [ ] **Private lesson and trade screenshots are served unauthenticated at /api/media/<key>, secrecy rests only on an unguessable key (low severity)** `security`
  *Symptom:* curl without an Authorization header returned 200 image/png for a lesson screenshot at /api/media/m/<32 hex>.png, the same as with a token.  <br>*Cause:* GET /api/media/ is intentionally public so <img> tags and the native app can load it, giving capability-URL security with no expiry, revocation or per-user check; a shared screen, Referer or browser history leaks the image permanently.  <br>*Fix:* Not changed (design trade-off, keys are 128-bit random). Hardening options: short-lived signed URLs or an auth-cookie check for private kinds, plus deleting the R2 object when the lesson or trade is deleted.
- [ ] **PUT /api/trades/:id rewrites every column, so a client sending a partial trade silently nulls the rest** `data-loss`
  *Symptom:* While adding trade editing to the native iPhone/Mac apps, the Swift Trade model lacked media/best/worst/confidence columns; saving an edit from it would have wiped those fields in D1.  <br>*Cause:* The trades PUT handler is full-replace, not merge-safe, unlike the new strategies and lesson PUTs.  <br>*Fix:* Extended the native Trade struct to carry every column and built a lossless Store.tradeBody for edits; the new strategies and notes.lesson PUTs were made merge-safe (partial PUT keeps all other fields, proven by curl). Rule recorded: never send a partial trade.
- [ ] **mxPop ReferenceError after a scripted patch silently no-oped** `crash`
  *Symptom:* A feature wired through a scripted String.replace patch threw 'mxPop is not defined' at runtime; the patch printed success.  <br>*Cause:* The patch anchor text did not match the file (shell quoting changed ${} / backslash / paren), so replace() returned the string unchanged and nothing was inserted, but later patches assumed it had landed.  <br>*Fix:* Scripted patches must assert the anchor exists (throw if src.includes(anchor) is false) and assert the output differs; prefer the Edit tool or a quoted heredoc ('EOF') for code containing $ ( \ characters.
- [ ] **Native app aborted right after granting speech/microphone permission (AudioUnit RPC timeout)** `crash`
  *Symptom:* Tapping Allow on the mic/speech prompt in the iOS simulator killed the app.  <br>*Cause:* The .ips crash report shows abort() from AudioToolbox _ReportRPCTimeout via AURemoteIO::Initialize / AudioUnitInitialize: the audio engine was started with no usable input path right after the permission grant.  <br>*Fix:* Speech.swift gained an inputUnavailable state and a guard around starting the recogniser/audio engine so unavailable input is reported in the UI instead of aborting the process; rebuilt for iOS and Mac.
- [ ] **StudioTTS.stop was undefined: voice 'stop' wiring threw on tts.speak/stop alias mismatch** `crash`
  *Symptom:* Pressing stop on spoken lessons threw TypeError (StudioTTS.stop is not a function) and audio kept playing.  <br>*Cause:* The studio-voices module exported speak/cancel but callers (tts.js, lessons UI) invoked stop; no alias existed, and the call sat in an event handler so the error was swallowed.  <br>*Fix:* Add stop as an alias of cancel in the module and guard callers with typeof checks; add a smoke test that every public method name used by callers exists on the module object.
- [ ] **Native trades PUT replaces the whole record: partial updates from iPhone/Mac dropped fields (not merge-safe)** `sync`
  *Symptom:* Editing one field in the native app could blank other trade fields saved by the web app.  <br>*Cause:* API.swift sends a full-object PUT from a possibly stale local copy; the Worker replaces rather than merges.  <br>*Fix:* Send the complete freshly-fetched object, or add a server-side merge/PATCH; verify round-trip of every field in a cross-client test.
- [ ] **Calendar and day views grouped trades by the UTC date of an ISO timestamp, putting evening futures trades on the wrong day** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Futures trades taken late in the local day appeared under the following calendar day, so per-day totals and per-trade minis in the month calendar were wrong.  <br>*Cause:* tradeDay() sliced the first 10 characters of the UTC ISO string instead of deriving the trader's local calendar date.  <br>*Fix:* Day grouping now uses the trader's local date; verified both test futures trades group on the 24th with a +$1,600 total and per-trade minis.
- [ ] **Candle route regex rejected ETF/dotted/dashed tickers (400 on valid symbols)** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Charts and bots returned 400 'bad symbol' for tickers such as BRK-B style or ETF symbols with punctuation.  <br>*Cause:* Symbol validation regex allowed only [A-Z]{1,5}; real instruments include '.', '-', '^', '=' (BRK-B, ^GSPC, EURUSD=X).  <br>*Fix:* Widen to a bounded allowlist class (letters, digits, . - ^ =) with a length cap, keep it anchored, and keep it SSRF-safe since the symbol is interpolated into an upstream URL (encodeURIComponent).
- [ ] **Emoji-to-icon sweep injected ${ic()} template placeholders into quoted (non-template) strings** `logic`
  *Symptom:* After a scripted emoji to SVG icon sweep, some buttons/labels rendered the literal text '${ic(...)}' instead of an icon.  <br>*Cause:* The sweep replaced emoji everywhere with a template-literal call, but a share of the emoji sat inside single/double-quoted strings, where ${} is not interpolated.  <br>*Fix:* Regex pass restricted to backtick strings, and a post-sweep grep for the literal '${ic(' inside non-template quotes; convert those strings to concatenation. Always grep for the injected token's literal form after a mass replace.
- [ ] **Live chart watch board always fetched 1d candles even while the user was sharing a 1m chart** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Watching a fast timeframe on TradingView, the board still read daily bars, so signal readouts did not match what the user saw.  <br>*Cause:* refreshBoard() called fetchCandles(symbol, '1d') with the timeframe hard-coded and there was no timeframe state on the watch object.  <br>*Fix:* Added a timeframe selector (1m to 1d) to the board, stored it on the LW state, fetched at the chosen timeframe, and made Ava suggest switching the board and the TradingView chart when the top rule needs a different interval; verified the switch does not stop the screen-share stream.
- [ ] **Live-session P&L ignored the futures contract point value** `logic`
  *Symptom:* NQ session P&L was computed with the wrong per-point value ($50 per point) instead of the contract point value, so futures trades in the live session summary were mis-valued.  <br>*Cause:* session.js buildTradeBody did its own price*qty maths instead of calling the point-value function the journal already used.  <br>*Fix:* Exposed window.pointValue from views.js and used it in the session buildTradeBody; verified NQ and equity point values (equity x1) before committing.
- [ ] **Live-session voice parser took filler words as tickers and let exit/stop chunks overwrite the entry price** `logic`
  *Symptom:* 'took profit exit at 18475' created symbol TOOK and overwrote NQ; the exit chunk's 'at 18475' replaced the entry price, so the saved trade lost its entry.  <br>*Cause:* The symbol regex accepted any capitalised token not in the keyword list, and mergeTrade() merged every field of each later transcript chunk over the existing trade.  <br>*Fix:* Added the missing words to the keyword list, a guard that stops later chunks overwriting an established symbol, and made lifecycle chunks merge only close-side fields (exit_price, stop_price, target_price, pnl, fees); NQ entry 18450 to exit 18475 preserved.
- [ ] **Native media URL hardcoded to production ignored the API base, so lesson and strategy screenshots showed grey placeholders against a local dev API** `logic`
  *Symptom:* On the simulator pointed at a local wrangler dev (ABBA_BASE), lesson screenshots stayed placeholders although curl to the same /api/media/<key> returned 200 image/png; a grep afterwards found DashboardView.swift:274 still hardcoded as well.  <br>*Cause:* API.mediaURL() built https://abba.coconvo.workers.dev/api/media/<key> instead of deriving from the configurable base, so only some call sites followed the override.  <br>*Fix:* mediaURL now returns base.absoluteString + '/media/' + key and the remaining hardcoded builder was found by grep and routed through it; verified on the simulator.
- [ ] **Naturally dictated trade phrasing ('log a trade, pinterest ticker PINS ... price right now is 23.47') logged nothing** `logic`
  *Symptom:* A real user dictation with a company name, the word 'ticker' and a spoken price produced no trade at all.  <br>*Cause:* parseTrade only recognised terse tickers/prices and trade intent was detected from a narrow phrase list; there was no 'ticker X' form, company-name lookup or 'price right now is N' form.  <br>*Fix:* Added a ticker-phrase regex, a company-name to ticker map, a price-phrase regex and broader trade-intent detection; the exact failing utterance now yields {symbol: PINS, entry_price: 23.47}.
- [ ] **SQLite datetime('now') strings failed both ISO-8601 parsers on native, so events showed as 56 years old** `logic`
  *Symptom:* The native notifications sheet showed '56 yrs' ago and mis-sorted signals and fills.  <br>*Cause:* SQLite datetime('now') returns 'YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS' (space, no T, no zone); both ISO8601DateFormatter variants rejected it and the fallback was epoch 0.  <br>*Fix:* Added an explicit UTC parser for the space-separated format; signals and fills now sort correctly and show '3 days, 20 hr'.
- [ ] **Swift URL.appendingPathComponent percent-encoded '?', so native candle requests failed and the backtest silently did nothing** `logic`
  *Symptom:* On iPhone/Mac, Backtest returned to the same screen with no result and no error.  <br>*Cause:* botCandles built '/bot/candles?symbol=...&interval=...' with appendingPathComponent, which encodes '?' as %3F so the server saw a bad path.  <br>*Fix:* Build the URL from a string/URLComponents so the query survives; backtest then ran on real Kraken candles (commit b026e6a).
- [ ] **Vision watchlist hallucinated tickers: model-extracted symbols were added without validation** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Uploading a chart/screenshot to build a watchlist added symbols that were never in the image (and some that do not exist).  <br>*Cause:* Vision model output was trusted as a ticker list; models complete plausible-looking tickers.  <br>*Fix:* Validate every extracted symbol against the real price feed (candle/quote lookup) before it is offered, and make the watchlist add confirm-first (user ticks the symbols to keep). Treat model-extracted identifiers as untrusted until resolved against a source of truth.
- [ ] **Worker router skipped JSON body parsing for /api/media/*, so new JSON multipart-upload routes received an empty body** `logic`
  *Symptom:* The new create/part/complete multipart endpoints returned errors because body was empty even though the client sent JSON.  <br>*Cause:* The router deliberately skipped JSON parsing for everything under /api/media to protect binary uploads, which also swallowed the three JSON control routes added under that prefix.  <br>*Fix:* Parse the JSON body inside those three handlers; proven with served multipart test calls and then the Claude Fathom pipeline on the uploaded memo.
- [ ] **'24/7 bots' claim was false: bots only ran while the browser tab was open** `claims`
  *Symptom:* UI and copy promised bots that trade around the clock, but strategy evaluation lived entirely in the client; closing the tab stopped them.  <br>*Cause:* Evaluator ran in bot.js on a client interval; the Worker had no scheduled evaluator, so there was no server-side execution path.  <br>*Fix:* Add a Cloudflare cron trigger that evaluates enabled bots server-side against the same candle feed and records signals; until it exists, copy must say 'runs while Abba is open'. Do not ship an availability claim the architecture cannot meet.
- [ ] **Trader-journal name 'Abba' collides with Polar Music's ABBA registered marks** `claims`
  *Symptom:* The 'Abba' name overlaps the band's registered marks: UK IR UK00801494598 (classes 9/35/38/42 and more, to 2029), EUTM 000620732 (classes 9/41), US class 9 filings, and a s.10(3) reputation claim.  <br>*Cause:* The name was picked without clearance, and software (class 9/42) sits inside the registered classes.  <br>*Fix:* Open: rename, or re-brand under a house mark with a distinctive composite, before marketing spend.
- [ ] **pkill -x Abba also kills the iOS Simulator's Abba process (same executable name), which looked exactly like a Save crash** `testing`
  *Symptom:* After relaunching the Mac build the iPhone simulator app dropped to the home screen right after Save; crash reports and log show found nothing, and the identical Save path then worked three times with a log stream attached.  <br>*Cause:* pkill -x matches the process name system-wide, and the simulator app process is also named Abba, so the Mac relaunch step killed the simulator app mid-test.  <br>*Fix:* Terminate with xcrun simctl terminate <udid> <bundle-id> for the simulator and pkill -f '<build path>/Abba.app/Contents/MacOS/Abba' for the Mac binary; before chasing a 'crash' with no crash report, check whether your own cleanup command killed the app.
- [ ] **Ask Ava chat history was lost on every re-render** `ui`
  *Symptom:* Opening another tab/theme change or any re-render cleared the Ask Ava conversation.  <br>*Cause:* The conversation lived in a DOM-local variable recreated by the view render function, not in app state.  <br>*Fix:* Hold messages in the app store (and persist to the docs/sync layer) and rebuild the DOM from it on each render.
- [ ] **Fixed top-right heart/notification button covered page titles and header content** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The owner reported the heart overlapping the information on pages; it sat over title rows and the first row of content on desktop and mobile.  <br>*Cause:* The heart was position:fixed top-right independent of the page flow, so any title row that ran to the right edge was under it; attempts to dock it into the sidebar/bottom nav were rejected ('leave the heart').  <br>*Fix:* Anchored the heart in the page header flow top-right (the Finished pattern) and made every title row reserve a gutter for it; an overlap sweep on dashboard and badges found zero elements under it on desktop and mobile.
- [ ] **Mobile bottom bar stays dark on light themes (open)** `ui`
  *Symptom:* On light-background themes the mobile bottom navigation remains a dark bar instead of following the theme.  <br>*Cause:* The bottom-bar background is a fixed dark value rather than a theme token (noticed during the heart/More-tab pass and left untouched).  <br>*Fix:* Not fixed - route the bar background and icon colours through the theme tokens like the sidebar.
- [ ] **Mobile bottom-nav 'More' tab rendered with an empty icon slot so it did not align with its siblings** `ui`
  *Symptom:* On phones the More tab showed a blank space where every other tab had an icon.  <br>*Cause:* icons.js had no 'more' glyph, so ic('more') returned nothing for the nav item.  <br>*Fix:* Added a rounded 3x3 app-grid icon in the same 1.8px stroke set and bumped icons.js to ?v=4.
- [ ] **Muted/faint text was nearly invisible on light-tinted themes such as babypink** `ui`
  *Symptom:* On pastel themes the secondary grey text and placeholders washed out against the tinted background; the user asked for the grey to follow each theme's colour and stay readable.  <br>*Cause:* One global muted tone was reused across all 14+ imported themes, and on pale themes it landed too close to the background.  <br>*Fix:* Per-theme muted/dim tokens (e.g. mauve on babypink) and theme-tinted inputs with readable placeholders; verified on the babypink theme.
- [ ] **Native BotView.whenText printed the literal source (Int(r.stopPct)) instead of the number** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The iPhone and Mac strategy-bot rule text showed raw interpolation code in place of the stop percentage.  <br>*Cause:* The Swift string used (Int(r.stopPct)) without the leading backslash, so it is plain text; it compiles and passes the build.  <br>*Fix:* Added the missing backslash; the native build is green; found while porting the plain-English explainer to native.
- [ ] **Native dark/glass pass: large currency number wrapped ('$20146 8'), pushed views lost the theme background and white tiles glared** `ui`
  *Symptom:* On iPhone the big account-size figure wrapped mid-number, pushed screens showed black behind light-theme tiles, tiles were blinding white in dark mode and pager dots overlapped card content.  <br>*Cause:* Themed background and glass card styling were not applied to pushed views, and numeric text had no single-line/scale limit, so layout broke on long figures.  <br>*Fix:* Single-line scaled numbers, theme background on pushed views, a glass CardStyle for dark mode and dot spacing; verified in light and dark on the exact screen from the user's screenshot.
- [ ] **Saved themes and sidebar colour sets could not be renamed after saving** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The owner said they were not letting her edit the name of a theme after saving it; the name was fixed at save time.  <br>*Cause:* The saved-themes list had no rename control and the Theme control lived in the sidebar instead of Settings.  <br>*Fix:* Moved themes into Settings as 'Themes & appearance' with a pencil rename on every saved theme and saved sidebar colour; theme names persisted in the settings key the Worker accepts.
- [ ] **1,433 Xcode build products under native/build were committed to git** `other`
  *Symptom:* git status listed 1,433 tracked deletions under native/build after a clean build directory.  <br>*Cause:* No .gitignore entry for native/build, so an earlier git add -A captured derived data.  <br>*Fix:* Removed the files from the index and added native/build/ to .gitignore (history still holds the blobs; the repo is local only).
- [ ] **App's own TimelineView type shadows SwiftUI.TimelineView, giving opaque compile errors ('extra trailing closure', 'no member periodic')** `other`
  *Symptom:* TradesView.swift:20 failed with 'extra trailing closure passed in call', 'type EnvironmentObject<Store> has no member periodic' and 'cannot infer contextual base .now' for a plain TimelineView(.periodic(from: .now, by: 1)).  <br>*Cause:* The app defines its own TimelineView screen, which shadows SwiftUI's in module scope, so the unqualified name resolves to the app type.  <br>*Fix:* Qualify as SwiftUI.TimelineView(.periodic(from: .now, by: 1)). Avoid naming app views after SwiftUI types (TimelineView, Section, Label, List).
- [ ] **iPhone target failed to build on .onChange(of:){ _, v in } (iOS 17-only) while the Mac target compiled, after a commit had already claimed both builds green** `other`
  *Symptom:* xcodebuild -sdk iphonesimulator: 'onChange(of:initial:_:) is only available in iOS 17.0 or newer' at LessonsView.swift:118 and :148 (and StrategiesView); the macOS build of the same code succeeded, so the earlier commit message 'both targets build' was wrong.  <br>*Cause:* IPHONEOS_DEPLOYMENT_TARGET is 16.0 but MACOSX_DEPLOYMENT_TARGET is 14.0; the two-parameter onChange closure exists only on iOS 17+/macOS 14+, and only one target was built before committing.  <br>*Fix:* Switched to the single-parameter .onChange(of:) { v in } form; rebuilt BOTH targets (iphonesimulator and macosx) and corrected the claim in a follow-up commit. Build every target before saying 'builds green'.
- [ ] **Native app targeted iOS 17.0 so it could not install on the owner's iOS 16.7.16 iPhone** `other`
  *Symptom:* The iPhone build would have been refused on the owner's phone even after Developer Mode was enabled.  <br>*Cause:* The Xcode project deployment target was left at iOS 17.0 although every API used (SwiftUI Charts, NavigationStack, vertical TextField) is available from iOS 16.  <br>*Fix:* Lowered the deployment target to iOS 16.0, rebuilt with zero availability warnings and re-ran the app on the simulator.
- [ ] **Repo history carried 388 MB of native build artifacts (20 MB binaries) despite native/build/ being gitignored, discovered only at the first GitHub push** `other`
  *Symptom:* Creating the private remote and pushing was very slow; git count-objects showed size-pack 388 MiB and the five largest blobs were Abba.app executables under native/build/.  <br>*Cause:* Build products were committed before the .gitignore rule existed, and the ignore rule never removed them from history.  <br>*Fix:* Push still worked (no file near 100 MB) and the history rewrite was left as the owner's call; recommended git-filter-repo --path native/build --invert-paths. Before a first push run: git rev-list --objects --all | git cat-file --batch-check | sort by size.
- [ ] **Swift 'unable to type-check this expression in reasonable time' from a long string concatenation inside Text(...)** `other`
  *Symptom:* StrategiesView.swift:292 failed the Mac build with 'the compiler is unable to type-check this expression in reasonable time' on a Text() built from optional map + string concatenations + String(format:).  <br>*Cause:* One expression mixing several optional .map closures, string concatenation and String(format:) overwhelms Swift's constraint solver.  <br>*Fix:* Extracted the pieces into a small helper statsLine(...) that builds the string step by step and passed the result to Text; build succeeded.

## Pixelbake  ·  25 fixed

- [ ] **Admin status is granted to anyone who signs up with an unclaimed handle in ADMIN_HANDLES (rhema and rhemajoy are free on the live app)** `security`
  *Symptom:* isAdmin() is true for any user whose handle string is in the ADMIN_HANDLES var (seven,rhema,rhemajoy,rhe). Signup is open and only checks that a handle is free and not in the small RESERVED set; a live POST /api/auth/check returned status free for 'rhema' and 'rhemajoy' (taken for 'rhe' and 'seven'). Whoever registers an unclaimed admin handle gets /api/admin/grant (up to 1,000,000 credits and any plan to any handle), showcase curation and every admin credit exemption.  <br>*Cause:* Authorisation keyed on a mutable, self-chosen string instead of a pre-registered account id; the admin handles were never reserved or pre-created.  <br>*Fix:* Open - remove unclaimed handles from ADMIN_HANDLES (or add them to RESERVED and pre-create them), key admin on user ids set by migration, and require a second factor or the owner's agent token for /api/admin/grant.
- [ ] **pxb login writes the API token file ~/.pixelbake.json with default 0644 permissions (world-readable)** `security`
  *Symptom:* ls -la showed ~/.pixelbake.json as -rw-r--r-- while the copied ~/.pixelbake-rhe.json was -rw-------; any local user or process could read the bearer token that files and bills images.  <br>*Cause:* pxb.mjs saves the token with fs.writeFileSync(cfgPath, ...) and no mode, so it inherits the umask (0644).  <br>*Fix:* Write with {mode:0o600} and chmod existing files on login; audit the other CLIs that save tokens (apz.mjs, worklog.mjs, bugledger) for the same default.
- [ ] **Sign-in has no brute-force lockout on 4-8 digit PINs and reveals which handles exist** `security`
  *Symptom:* POST /api/auth/signin checks the PIN with no attempt counter, delay or lockout, PINs may be as short as 4 digits, and the failure message is 'No account with that handle.' for an unknown handle versus 'Wrong PIN.' for a known one, so handles can be enumerated and any account (including the admin and credit-holding ones) can be guessed online.  <br>*Cause:* The Evertrue-style PBKDF2 recipe was copied without its rate limiting; the signin route only compares hash !== u.pin_hash (non-constant-time) and returns distinct errors.  <br>*Fix:* Open - add a per-handle and per-IP failed-attempt counter in D1 with a lockout window, a single generic 'Wrong handle or PIN' error, constant-time hash comparison, and a minimum PIN length of 6.
- [ ] **Copying images between accounts via upload flattens generated records to kind:upload; model, seed, prompt and cost survive only as notes text** `data-loss`
  *Symptom:* After migrating 173 images from @seven to @rhe the library showed 'Uploads 232' and 0 credits for them; model/seed/prompt are no longer queryable fields.  <br>*Cause:* The only way in is POST /api/upload, which stores pixels plus free-text notes; there is no import/clone endpoint that preserves the structured generation record or true cost.  <br>*Fix:* Add an owner-authorised import (or account merge) that copies kind/model/seed/prompt/neurons/parent ids and raises the cap for lossless masters; until then write provenance into notes and keep the master in the source account.
- [ ] **A solid-black failed generation was filed in the library like a normal image** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Library item 'Amara hyperreal test' (dreamshaper-8-lcm, 768x768) was a 1.8 KB all-black PNG. It showed as a successful bake and was enlarged by the upscaler.  <br>*Cause:* No blank or low-entropy output check after generation. Likely a safety-checker or failed-sampler output that was stored and billed as a success.  <br>*Fix:* Open. Detect near-uniform output by byte-size or histogram check, then fail the bake and refund credits, or auto-retry.
- [ ] **Brand brief drafted from a JS-rendered site saw only the empty HTML shell and invented a grey, corporate brand** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Marketing-from-URL for a client-rendered site drafted a brief with guessed grey palette, corporate audience and generic style instead of the real brand.  <br>*Cause:* The brief drafter reads server HTML only; the target page was a roughly 1.8 KB shell whose content is rendered by JavaScript.  <br>*Fix:* Corrected palette, audience, style and the six concepts through the briefs API (PATCH /api/briefs) before spending any credits. Recommended: detect a near-empty page, render it with a browser fetch, or warn 'I only saw N characters, check the brief' before the first bake.
- [ ] **Character auto-describe silently saved an empty identity brief** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Creating a character from a photo succeeded but the brief and traits were empty (refs:1, traits keys: none), so no identity anchor reached later bakes.  <br>*Cause:* describeCharacter made one vision call and parsed it as JSON, but the vision model returns prose. The parse failed inside a try/catch and nothing surfaced.  <br>*Fix:* Two-step pipeline, vision to prose then a text model to structured JSON, as the existing describe endpoint does. The resulting brief is now enumerated (eyes, nose, jaw, hair, skin, distinctive marks).
- [ ] **Free model dreamshaper-8-lcm returned an all-black PNG, reported success and charged a credit** `logic`
  *Symptom:* pxb gen --model dreamshaper-8-lcm printed a success line (0 Neurons, 1 credit) and wrote a 768x768 file that was a black frame.  <br>*Cause:* Not isolated. The bake path returns whatever bytes the model produced and has no blank-image check, so an empty frame takes the normal success and billing path.  <br>*Fix:* Worked around by using flux-2-dev once credits allowed. Recommended: reject near-uniform output server-side (mean luminance and variance), refund the credit and return an error.
- [ ] **GET /api/images and pxb list silently cap limit at 200, so 'list everything' reconciliations undercount a 372-image library** `logic`
  *Symptom:* `pxb list --limit 1000` and /api/images?limit=1000 both returned exactly 200 records; the library looked like 200 while per-project queries summed to 372.  <br>*Cause:* The list endpoint clamps limit to 200 and returns no total, cursor or truncated flag, so a client cannot tell the page is partial.  <br>*Fix:* Return total plus a next cursor (or honour larger limits) and have pxb list warn when count equals the cap.
- [ ] **Prompt improver returned 502 when the LLM wrapped its answer in prose instead of clean JSON** `logic`
  *Symptom:* POST /api/improve returned 'Could not improve that one' 502 on a short idea although the model had produced a good prompt; CLI printed pixelbake: /api/improve -> 502.  <br>*Cause:* The handler required a parsed {prompt} object; when Llama emitted text with the JSON embedded or broken, d.prompt was empty and the whole call failed.  <br>*Fix:* If JSON parsing yields no prompt but the raw text is >80 chars, regex-extract the 'prompt' field and strip the trailing negative key as a fallback before returning 502.
- [ ] **Your-days per-day route read the wrong path segment (404) and the headline printed raw DB kind words** `logic`
  *Symptom:* The new per-day summary page 404'd for valid dates and its headline said things like 'generated, upload, fix' straight from the DB enum instead of readable kind names.  <br>*Cause:* The per-day route read the wrong path segment (valid dates returned 404 and the markdown/html formats were unknown endpoints) and the summary headline interpolated raw DB kind values instead of labelled kind names.  <br>*Fix:* Read the final path segment as the date and map kinds to human labels before composing the headline; verified on all four surfaces (web, CLI, MCP, share).
- [ ] **Hero headline uses the Claude Code name and the run recommended clearing it before paid use** `claims`
  *Symptom:* The website hero and the developer hooks say the studio is for Claude Code; the run flagged that this is another company's product name inside an advertisement and left it unresolved  <br>*Cause:* The site copy already used the name and the creative system inherited it without a trademark or nominative-use check  <br>*Fix:* Open: check the vendor's brand rules, prefer a descriptive phrase (for coding agents) in paid placements, and keep the product name only as plain factual compatibility text
- [ ] **Pain ad headline in quotation marks reads as a customer review but is a composed line** `claims`
  *Symptom:* The lead static and carousel slide 1 print 'It failed. It still took my credits.' in quotes as the customer's own words; the complaint exists in 2026 Trustpilot reviews of two rivals but the sentence is invented and was read through a summarising tool  <br>*Cause:* The skill tells the composer to use the customer's words in quotes, and with no verbatim review in hand a composed line was set in the same style  <br>*Fix:* Open: either quote a real review verbatim with its source and date, or remove the quotation marks and label the line as a paraphrase; keep a quote-provenance field in the creative spec
- [ ] **Product name identical to an existing UK registered word mark in class 42** `claims`
  *Symptom:* A live paid product (Stripe plans $9/$29/$99) trades as 'Pixelbake', identical to UK word mark UK00003894245 (class 42 SaaS/graphic-design platforms, Pxlbake Ltd, registered 23/06/2023, plus PXLBAKE UK00003888678).  <br>*Cause:* The name was chosen and launched without a trademark-register search. An identical mark for identical services is infringement under TMA 1994 s.10(1) with no confusion analysis needed.  <br>*Fix:* Open: rename before any paid marketing. Registration is not required to launch, but launching under a registered name is infringement from day one.
- [ ] **Site and pitch deck quote FLUX.2 dev at 42, 51 and 40 credits in three places while billing charged 42 and 63** `claims`
  *Symptom:* The marketing run read the live site and the pitch and found three different credit figures for the same model, none matching the 42 (square) and 63 (portrait) actually debited  <br>*Cause:* Flat per-model figures were written into copy before the tile-based pricing change; the copy was not derived from the PLANS and cost functions  <br>*Fix:* Open: render every credit figure from the same cost function (or a generated table) and add a check that greps the site and deck for the model's credit numbers
- [ ] **Credit balance fell two to four times more than the listed cost of the bakes with no record of the difference** `observability`
  *Symptom:* Two sessions saw 1,029 credits leave against 483 listed (v1) and 1,728 against about 441 (v2); the rest could not be attributed.  <br>*Cause:* Uploads, retries or concurrent sessions are charged without a ledger line per charge.  <br>*Fix:* Record every credit movement (bake, upload, refund) as a transaction with a reason and surface the running total in the CLI.
- [ ] **Finished bakes silently swap the placeholder for the image, so users cannot tell when a long job is done** `ui`
  *Symptom:* User feedback: 'I don't know when it finishes'. A studio bake replaced the loading tile with the image with no signal, and modal results (upscale, fix, paint) just opened the lightbox.  <br>*Cause:* There was no completion state. The placeholder was removed and the tile prepended with no reveal, flag or ribbon.  <br>*Fix:* markFresh() pop-in with a fading 'Just baked' flag and scroll-into-view. openLightbox(id, fresh) shows a labelled success ribbon such as 'Upscaled 2x - Clear'.
- [ ] **Mobile horizontal overflow on Bulk grid (108px), Connect (532px) and pitch deck (102px) at 390px** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The 390px QA pass reported pageX overflow on the Bulk contenteditable grid, the Connect page (code/CLI tables) and the pitch deck while every other view was 0.  <br>*Cause:* Wide contenteditable table cells (min-width 120px, max-width 360px), CLI/code blocks and the pitch layout had no max-width:100% containment and .main did not clip overflow, so the page itself scrolled sideways on phones.  <br>*Fix:* Added a max-width:900px safety block: .main{overflow-x:hidden}, .card/.table-wrap/.chips/.estimate max-width:100%, narrower .grid-edit .cell; re-measured overflowPx 0 on every view.
- [ ] **Mobile nav drawer stayed open after hash navigation and covered the page content** `ui`
  *Symptom:* At 390px, tapping a sidebar link changed the hash route but the drawer stayed open over the tiles; a Playwright click on .tile timed out because the sidebar intercepted pointer events.  <br>*Cause:* The route() handler re-rendered the view but nothing removed the .open class from #side on hashchange.  <br>*Fix:* hashchange listener now does #side.classList.remove('open') before route(); mobile pass re-run with menuClosedAfterNav true.
- [ ] **Open modal survives a hash-route change** `ui`
  *Symptom:* Navigating to #library while the Upscale/lightbox modal was open left the modal on screen until a hard reload.  <br>*Cause:* Modals are appended to the body and are not closed by route(), which only swaps the view.  <br>*Fix:* Open. Close open modals in route() or on hashchange.
- [ ] **402 'Top up in Settings' names no account, so a CLI on the @seven test account looked like the topped-up @rhe account was empty** `other`
  *Symptom:* Three parallel flux-2-dev bakes failed 'You need 42 credits for this bake and have 4' right after the user said they had topped up; project images were baked and filed on @seven; pxb balance is an unknown command.  <br>*Cause:* ~/.pixelbake.json held the test account token (the house rule says @rhe). The 402 text and CLI output never show the handle, and the only identity check is a separate whoami.  <br>*Fix:* Found the mismatch with whoami and stats (@seven, 4 credits) and told the user which account held the credit. Recommended: 402 body and CLI error include {handle, balance, needed}, a pxb balance alias, and a whoami-must-be-@rhe check at the start of any task that makes images.
- [ ] **Llama 3.2 vision describe fails with error 5016 until the model licence is accepted once per account** `other`
  *Symptom:* First POST /api/images/:id/describe returned 'Thank you for agreeing to this...' (5016) instead of a description; the Re-engineer, Prompt lab and brand-from-URL describe paths all failed on a fresh account.  <br>*Cause:* @cf/meta/llama-3.2-11b-vision-instruct requires a one-time licence acceptance via env.AI.run(model,{prompt:'agree'}) before it will describe an image; the Worker called it directly and surfaced the raw 5016 error.  <br>*Fix:* Catch /agree|licen|terms/ errors, call env.AI.run(VISION_MODEL,{prompt:'agree'}) once (swallow its own error), retry the describe, and only then surface a friendly error.
- [ ] **Premium third-party Workers AI model (Pruna p-image-upscale) shipped as an engine but fails with 'Insufficient AI Gateway credits'** `other`
  *Symptom:* The Max-detail upscale engine returned '2021: Insufficient AI Gateway credits' on the first live call, so the advertised best-in-class engine could not run.  <br>*Cause:* Third-party models on Workers AI bill through AI Gateway, which is not provisioned on the account, and billing was not verified before the engine was built and advertised.  <br>*Fix:* Failure maps to a friendly 'needs AI Gateway billing - nothing charged' error. Default engine switched to the first-party Images-binding 'Clear' path. Max-detail stays gated and the pitch deck labels it 'ready, needs AI Gateway'.
- [ ] **pxb gen --ref with a reference id owned by another account returns a bare 404 'Reference image not found in your library'** `other`
  *Symptom:* Six FLUX.2 dev re-bakes failed with '/api/generate -> 404 Reference image not found in your library' even though the reference existed.  <br>*Cause:* The reference was uploaded under the rhe account but the bakes ran with the seven account token; the error does not say the id belongs to a different account.  <br>*Fix:* Re-ran with the token that owns the reference (PIXELBAKE_TOKEN per call) and uploaded the ref to the second account for later runs; the CLI should name the account mismatch in the 404 message.
- [ ] **Upscale via the Images binding is filed as 0 Neurons / $0.00000 although Cloudflare bills per unique transformation** `other`
  *Symptom:* Clear upscale results show '0 Neurons - $0.00000 - 2 cr' in the library, which contradicts the studio's 'true cost per image' promise.  <br>*Cause:* The hidden 'upscale' model has cost:()=>0. Images-binding transformations are metered per unique transform after the free allowance, and that spend is never recorded.  <br>*Fix:* Open. Record an estimated per-transformation USD cost for the Images binding, and a measured or flat figure for the Pruna engine, so the cost ledger and margins stay honest.

## Breadcrumb  ·  22 fixed
