- [ ] **+feature truncation swallows text and skips redaction** `security`
  *Symptom:* The `+feature` command read a fixed 60 characters, swallowing the rest of the sentence, and the swallowed text bypassed redaction (secret-leak risk).  <br>*Cause:* A fixed 60-char read plus the swallowed remainder never passing through the redactor.  <br>*Fix:* Read the full text and route it through redaction, using exact-match so tokens like `#bug` can't be swallowed.
- [ ] **Device-connect reopen link was a dead end: approve code lost when the app was already signed in elsewhere** `undefined`
  *Symptom:* Opening the device-connect link on a second device led to a page with no way to complete approval.  <br>*Cause:* deviceLinkPage rendered a static page that did not carry the one-time code through sign-in, so after login the code was gone.  <br>*Fix:* Support /?approve=<code> auto-approve after sign-in and a Settings 'paste code' fallback; keep the code in sessionStorage across the auth step.
- [ ] **No brute-force lockout on 5-digit passcode sign-in/recovery** `security`
  *Symptom:* A 5-digit passcode could be guessed with unlimited attempts on sign-in and forgot-passcode  <br>*Cause:* Auth had no attempt throttling; PBKDF2 is capped at 100k iterations on Workers so an unlimited-guess passcode was the real hole  <br>*Fix:* Added an attempts table with gateCheck/gateFail/gateClear keyed separately by email and device IP, wired into sign-in and recovery; escalating lockouts 5->5min, 8->30min, 12->6h; cleared on success. Verified live: 6th miss returns HTTP 429
- [ ] **Upsert ON CONFLICT omitted meta, dropping an edited prompt's asks ledger on sync** `data-loss`
  *Symptom:* Editing a prompt lost its asks/convo/estimated data after cloud sync  <br>*Cause:* The docs/prompts ON CONFLICT update clause did not include the meta column, so edits to meta never persisted on re-sync  <br>*Fix:* Add meta to the ON CONFLICT update set (and fix the size cap) so edited prompt metadata round-trips
- [ ] **Recovery codes hashed inconsistently** `auth`
  *Symptom:* Valid recovery codes could fail because hashing was inconsistent.  <br>*Cause:* Recovery codes were hashed inconsistently between generation and verification.  <br>*Fix:* Hash recovery codes consistently.
- [ ] **Docs not synced** `sync`
  *Symptom:* Docs weren't reliably persisted/synced.  <br>*Cause:* There was no synced docs store.  <br>*Fix:* Added a proper synced `docs` table and fixed the size cap.
- [ ] **Landing-demo 'Sam' data bled into newly created accounts** `sync`
  *Symptom:* Exploring the landing-page demo, then signing up, produced an account pre-filled with demo projects/prompts and greeted the user as 'Sam'  <br>*Cause:* Signup adopted the in-memory demo state via cloudPush instead of resetting to a blank trail first  <br>*Fix:* Reset state to blank on signup before adopting the account; added a cloudPull auto-heal that resets accounts matching the demo fingerprint
- [ ] **Sync blob over 2 MB silently 413'd: large journals stopped backing up without notice** `sync`
  *Symptom:* Cloud backup silently stopped for heavy users; local data diverged from the server.  <br>*Cause:* A single JSON blob was pushed per collection with a 2 MB request cap, and the client ignored the 413.  <br>*Fix:* Move large content to the generic docs table (per-document rows, size cap per doc) and surface sync failures in the UI with retry.
- [ ] **`>cursor` builder-switch token silently discarded** `logic`
  *Symptom:* A `>cursor` builder switch inside a captured prompt was silently dropped.  <br>*Cause:* An undefined template-literal segment (rendering as `undefined}`) discarded the `>cursor` token.  <br>*Fix:* Fixed the parsing so the builder-switch token is preserved.
- [ ] **Capture-bar quick chips don't update the prompt placeholder** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Clicking a quick builder chip in the capture bar didn't update the 'What did you just ask X for?' message or switch the builder; only opening 'More' did.  <br>*Cause:* The quick chips lacked the handler that updates the placeholder and sets the active builder.  <br>*Fix:* Added selectCaptureBuilder() so a chip updates the placeholder in place (preserving typed text) and sets the builder.
- [ ] **Inconsistent text normalization** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Normalization behaved inconsistently across paths.  <br>*Cause:* Normalization logic differed between code paths.  <br>*Fix:* Made normalization consistent.
- [ ] **Settings card can't read subscription periodEnd** `logic`
  *Symptom:* The settings card read periodEnd but received nothing.  <br>*Cause:* publicUser didn't expose periodEnd.  <br>*Fix:* Exposed periodEnd on publicUser so the settings card can show the subscription period.
- [ ] **Trail outcome filter is a dead control** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Clicking the trail's outcome filter (All/worked/almost/didn't-work) did nothing.  <br>*Cause:* `trail-filter` had no handler at all.  <br>*Fix:* Wired the outcome filter so it actually filters the trail.
- [ ] **Invented 2-project free-tier cap presented as policy with no basis** `claims`
  *Symptom:* UI and pricing copy enforced a 2-project free limit that no decision or evidence supported.  <br>*Cause:* A limit was assumed during build and then rendered as fact in copy and code.  <br>*Fix:* Remove the cap (or mark it as an explicit owner decision); never invent a restriction.
- [ ] **Inverted-citation restriction shipped: a study cited for the opposite of its finding (Kao, CHI 2024)** `claims`
  *Symptom:* A product rule restricting a feature cited a paper whose result actually supported the feature.  <br>*Cause:* The claim was taken from a secondary summary and never checked against the primary source; the critique was itself treated as a verified fact.  <br>*Fix:* Re-read the primary paper, reverse the restriction, add the source line and verification date next to every research-derived rule. A critique is a claim too.
- [ ] **Forget-device button lacked a confirmation** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The forget-this-device shortcut (a trash/X button) executed with no confirmation, against the every-destructive-action-confirms rule.  <br>*Cause:* The button had no confirm dialog.  <br>*Fix:* Added a confirmation before forgetting the device.
- [ ] **Insights dashboard grid misrendered** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The Insights dashboard's 4-column stat-tile row (plus donuts, bars, heatmap and comparison) didn't lay out correctly.  <br>*Cause:* An Insights grid class bug broke the layout.  <br>*Fix:* Fixed the grid class (v24) so the stat-tile row and charts render properly.
- [ ] **Plans feature row wrapped its text below the tick** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The long 'Automatic capture' feature line wrapped underneath its checkmark instead of beside it  <br>*Cause:* .row used flex-wrap:wrap, letting the label drop to a new line  <br>*Fix:* Prevent the row from wrapping so the label stays inline with its tick
- [ ] **srcdoc iframe thumbnails render blank** `ui`
  *Symptom:* Prompt thumbnails showed blank.  <br>*Cause:* `loading="lazy"` on srcdoc iframes plus a `height:200%` that never resolved against an aspect-ratio height.  <br>*Fix:* Removed lazy loading, set srcdoc as a property, and deferred one frame so thumbnails render.
- [ ] **i18n script not included in index.html** `other`
  *Symptom:* The i18n script wasn't loaded, so localization wouldn't run.  <br>*Cause:* The i18n script tag was never added to index.html (only security.js had a bumped version).  <br>*Fix:* Added the i18n script to index.html.
- [ ] **NUL bytes in worker.js source make grep/file treat it as binary** `other`
  *Symptom:* A plain grep silently matched nothing in a 1568-line source file, so an agent wrongly concluded the /api/log, /api/worklog and /worklog.mjs endpoints didn't exist and was about to recreate the client; `file` reported the source as data, not text.  <br>*Cause:* translateRoute's cache key used a literal NUL (0x00) as a delimiter (tgt + '\0' + s), embedding raw NUL bytes at lines 525/542 — which makes grep/file classify the whole file as binary and match nothing.  <br>*Fix:* Replaced the literal NULs with the \u0000 escape (byte-identical runtime string, cache unchanged) so the source stays plain UTF-8 text and grep/file work; use grep -a as a fallback. (Same trap hit BugLedger's own scan.mjs earlier.)
- [ ] **Worklog CLI prompt assumed ~/.breadcrumb/worklog.mjs was already installed** `other`
  *Symptom:* The agent couldn't find ~/.breadcrumb/worklog.mjs because the one-time install had never been run — the prompt assumed the CLI already existed, so the flow failed at the first step.  <br>*Cause:* No install step; the CLI wasn't served anywhere and the key depended on a shell-profile variable.  <br>*Fix:* Serve the CLI from a public /worklog.mjs route so it installs with a single curl (self-healing in the prompt), and read the key from a small key file (~/.breadcrumb/key) instead of a shell-profile dependency.

## Hopefil  ·  22 fixed

- [ ] **Proprietary pricing/cost information leaking in the app** `security`
  *Symptom:* The app displayed confidential proprietary information such as per-credit cost and margins.  <br>*Cause:* Internal-economics copy and figures were exposed in the shipped bundle.  <br>*Fix:* Audited and fixed all five leaks (e.g. margin language replaced) and verified a clean leak-scan.
- [ ] **Preview freezes at an outdated state** `sync`
  *Symptom:* The visual preview froze at an outdated state (preview sync bug).  <br>*Cause:* Generators ran client-side without a guard against stale async responses.  <br>*Fix:* Moved generators to the worker with a cached, stale-response-guarded async shim and a 350ms hue-slider debounce.
- [ ] **Stale-closure loses rapid goal/checklist clicks** `race`
  *Symptom:* Rapid goal clicks used stale state (Income got overwritten by Community) and rapid checklist clicks registered only 1 of 3.  <br>*Cause:* Handlers captured stale state in a closure instead of using the latest value.  <br>*Fix:* Switched to a functional updater/toggle so each rapid click registers.
- [ ] **Dead landing CTA buttons** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Landing page CTA buttons did nothing (user-blocking).  <br>*Cause:* The 'Start building' nav pill was a plain #start anchor with no action wired.  <br>*Fix:* Made the nav pill a real action, made the empty-input nudge unmissable, and un-crowded mobile nav.
- [ ] **Generated files stamped with year 1996** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Generated files were timestamped 1996 instead of 2026.  <br>*Cause:* A wrong timestamp base in the file generator.  <br>*Fix:* Corrected the timestamp so files stamp the current year.
- [ ] **Greeting rotation regression** `logic`
  *Symptom:* The per-visit greeting rotation regressed during a change.  <br>*Cause:* A regression introduced while editing the greeting logic.  <br>*Fix:* Caught and fixed the regression so greetings rotate per visit from the pool.
- [ ] **Kotlin extension properties fail through fully-qualified chains** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Every exported Kotlin app would fail to compile on certain property access.  <br>*Cause:* The generator template emitted Kotlin extension properties resolved through fully-qualified chains, which Kotlin cannot resolve.  <br>*Fix:* Fixed the generator template (and live generator) so extension properties resolve correctly.
- [ ] **Android FAB not to Material 3 spec** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The Android floating action button had the wrong spec and clearance.  <br>*Cause:* FAB sizing/clearance did not follow M3.  <br>*Fix:* Rebuilt the Android FAB to M3 spec with correct clearance.
- [ ] **Barber app shows a wrong photo** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The barber demo app rendered a wrong/mismatched photo.  <br>*Cause:* An ambiguous/mismatched image was used at hero crop.  <br>*Fix:* Rendered mesh-art plus gradient-initial 'MR' avatars instead of the wrong photo.
- [ ] **Composer bar shows giant gradient pills** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The dashboard composer bar rendered giant gradient pills instead of a compact input/mic/button.  <br>*Cause:* It inherited the landing hero styles (a CSS inheritance bug).  <br>*Fix:* Scoped the composer styles to input-first, 40px mic at its side, compact 96px 'New app'.
- [ ] **Desktop/Mac/Web preview renders as a banner and single column** `ui`
  *Symptom:* Desktop, Mac and Web previews showed a lone phone column / banner and one name instead of a full-screen multi-column layout.  <br>*Cause:* One CSS keyword prevented auto-fit multi-column from firing, and a 118px card floor collapsed grids to one column.  <br>*Fix:* Made columns follow measured pane width via auto-fit and lowered the card floor to 70px for genuine multi-column desktop layouts.
- [ ] **Empty-prompt CTA silently does nothing** `ui`
  *Symptom:* Clicking 'New app' or 'Start building' with an empty prompt box did nothing — no builder, error, or feedback.  <br>*Cause:* The `if (!text) return` guard in `start()` bailed silently.  <br>*Fix:* Empty click now focuses the input, shakes the prompt bar, and highlights the hint.
- [ ] **Mobile nav wrap and file-list truncation** `ui`
  *Symptom:* In the narrow panel view the mobile nav wrapped and the file list truncated.  <br>*Cause:* Layout did not handle the narrow width.  <br>*Fix:* Fixed the mobile nav wrap and file-list truncation.
- [ ] **Pitch deck low contrast and overlapping footnotes** `ui`
  *Symptom:* Grey text on slides 7/8/9 was hard to read and footnotes overlapped the content.  <br>*Cause:* Dim text colors and footnotes that overlaid rather than flowed.  <br>*Fix:* Brightened the grey text, made footnotes flow below the legend, and auto-faded the arrow hint after 5s.
- [ ] **Pitch deck nav arrows un-positioned and wrong start slide** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The pitch deck did not start at slide 1 and the nav arrows were not centered at the sides.  <br>*Cause:* An 'all:unset' declared after 'position:fixed' un-positioned the arrows (CSS order matters).  <br>*Fix:* Reordered the CSS so arrows are centered and the deck starts at slide 1.
- [ ] **READY tiles stay dark on the light theme** `ui`
  *Symptom:* Some cards showed as black while others were white (READY tiles dark on the light theme).  <br>*Cause:* The theme token fix missed the dark .card-veil overlay.  <br>*Fix:* Applied the token fix to the .card-veil overlay so tiles match the theme.
- [ ] **Review-phase placeholder shows 'Building…' while awaiting approval** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The review phase displayed a 'Building…' placeholder while it was actually awaiting user approval.  <br>*Cause:* The placeholder text did not distinguish the awaiting-approval state.  <br>*Fix:* Corrected the review-phase placeholder to reflect the awaiting-approval state.
- [ ] **Six preview render/display complaints** `ui`
  *Symptom:* Category leak on a booking app, barber photo reading as a living room, toy-like toggle proportions, tab-bar labels wrapping, and settings labels ellipsizing.  <br>*Cause:* Multiple control-spec and layout issues across preview rendering.  <br>*Fix:* Fixed all six in code to researched control specs and encoded each as a rule in BUILD.md.
- [ ] **Stat numbers spilling over and jumbled** `ui`
  *Symptom:* Numbers in stat chips spilled over and looked jumbled.  <br>*Cause:* Stat chips were not wrap-proof.  <br>*Fix:* Rebuilt the stat chips as wrap-proof primitives and recorded the rule as law.
- [ ] **Wrong hue value on the render wall** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The render wall used a wrong hue.  <br>*Cause:* A hue value of 30 was incorrect.  <br>*Fix:* Set the warm color 0xFFD38C45 so all three render modes and dark mode render correctly.
- [ ] **Dev-server port mismatch in launch.json** `other`
  *Symptom:* The preview tooling couldn't attach to the dev server.  <br>*Cause:* The dev server binds 5197 but launch.json specified 5199.  <br>*Fix:* Corrected the port in launch.json.
- [ ] **Exported Android build fails: missing gradle.properties useAndroidX** `other`
  *Symptom:* The exported Android project failed its first-run compile in the farm.  <br>*Cause:* gradle.properties lacked android.useAndroidX=true.  <br>*Fix:* Added the one-line android.useAndroidX=true to gradle.properties and rebuilt.

## Budget LevelUp  ·  19 fixed

- [ ] **Billable proxy endpoints unthrottled (denial-of-wallet)** `security`
  *Symptom:* Anyone could call /api/translate and /api/vision in a loop and run up billable third-party compute.  <br>*Cause:* The two endpoints proxied to Google Translate and Workers AI but, unlike /api/backup, had no per-IP rate limit — an unbounded-consumption / denial-of-wallet vector.  <br>*Fix:* Added a shared per-IP rate-limit helper and gated both billable endpoints (verified 429 after 60 calls/IP).
- [ ] **No Content-Security-Policy on any page; inline handlers and the Tesseract CDN block a strict policy (flagged, not fixed)** `security`
  *Symptom:* The scanner reports NO-CSP on index, landing and pitch; headers now include nosniff, referrer and frame options only.  <br>*Cause:* The app relies on inline onclick handlers and loads OCR from a CDN, so a strict CSP would break features.  <br>*Fix:* Move handlers to delegated listeners, self-host or pin the OCR assets, then ship a script-src self policy via _headers.
- [ ] **TOTP QR service leaked 2FA secret** `security`
  *Symptom:* Enabling 2FA sent the TOTP secret to a third-party QR service.  <br>*Cause:* The QR code was generated via an external service, exposing the secret.  <br>*Fix:* Removed the external QR call and replaced it with private manual key entry.
- [ ] **Excel month selector wrote to the wrong cell** `data-loss`
  *Symptom:* Changing the month in the Social Tracker workbook did not affect the formulas' results.  <br>*Cause:* The month selector wrote to a cell the formulas never read.  <br>*Fix:* Pointed the selector at the cell the formulas read and re-verified 0 error cells.
- [ ] **note() call destroyed the month selector cell** `data-loss`
  *Symptom:* A merged note silently wiped the month selector cell in the workbook.  <br>*Cause:* A note() call wrote a merged cell over the month selector.  <br>*Fix:* Reworked the merge so the note no longer overwrites the month cell.
- [ ] **Celebrate tab crashes on saved payment card** `crash`
  *Symptom:* The Celebrate tab crashed for a fresh guest and whenever a payment card existed.  <br>*Cause:* S.cards was shared by both the card register and celebration gallery, so Celebrate read .copy.headline off a payment card.  <br>*Fix:* Moved celebration cards to their own S.celebCards key and made the gallery defensive against malformed entries.
- [ ] **Vault C1 brute-force / no per-account retrieval token** `auth`
  *Symptom:* The encrypted vault was brute-forceable and lacked per-account isolation.  <br>*Cause:* No per-account retrieval token; a shared retrieval path was guessable.  <br>*Fix:* Added a per-account retrieval token with a dual-read migration (legacy accounts sign in with zero lockout), first mitigated by a rate limit.
- [ ] **Broken social link handling** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Pasting a social URL produced a dead link, and bad image URLs showed a broken icon.  <br>*Cause:* Links without an https:// prefix and junk text were not normalized, and image errors were unhandled.  <br>*Fix:* Auto-prefix bare URLs to working links, reject junk text, and degrade broken images to a placeholder.
- [ ] **Debt payoff projection ignored APR** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Debt-free date and total-interest figures were wrong — too optimistic.  <br>*Cause:* The old payoff projection computed months by dividing balance by payment with no interest accrual, so it understated both the payoff time and the total interest.  <br>*Fix:* Replaced with an interest-aware payoff engine (snowball + avalanche), giving an accurate debt-free date, total interest, and extra-payment savings.
- [ ] **debtPaid sums payments by debt name, so two debts with the same name merge their paid amounts** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Two debts both called Credit card showed the combined payment history against each.  <br>*Cause:* Payment lookups were switched to debt.id but debtPaid still aggregates by name.  <br>*Fix:* Key payment log entries and debtPaid by debt id and migrate old name-keyed entries.
- [ ] **Invoice button silently added an investment** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Clicking the Business invoice button silently created an investment instead.  <br>*Cause:* The invoice button shared a DOM element id with the Investments add-holding button.  <br>*Fix:* Gave the buttons unique ids and hardened the wiring; verified invoices and investments now act independently.
- [ ] **Merged-cell collision in pillars card** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Merged cells in the pillars card collided, corrupting the layout/values.  <br>*Cause:* Overlapping merged-cell ranges in the pillars card.  <br>*Fix:* Resolved the merged-cell collision and re-verified.
- [ ] **The ?demo=1 link only loaded the demo for first-time visitors, so returning visitors saw their old data** `logic`
  *Symptom:* On the live site a visitor with saved state opened /?demo=1 and saw net worth 319,964 and one month of data instead of the demo.  <br>*Cause:* Boot loaded the example only when !S.onboarded, so any prior visit masked the demo.  <br>*Fix:* Always reload a fresh demo for guest sessions that are empty or already a demo, never touching a signed-in or populated guest session.
- [ ] **toISOString() shifted calendar events a day early** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Calendar events appeared one day earlier than the chosen date.  <br>*Cause:* toISOString() applied a UTC timezone shift to local dates.  <br>*Fix:* Avoided the UTC conversion so dates stay on the intended day.
- [ ] **Untranslated card titles and chart labels** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Card titles and chart row labels stayed in English after switching language.  <br>*Cause:* Those strings were missed by the localization pass.  <br>*Fix:* Translated the remaining titles/labels and redeployed.
- [ ] **Your Data card says no account, no cloud, no tracking while the same app offers accounts, encrypted cloud backup and AI endpoints (flagged, not fixed)** `claims`
  *Symptom:* Settings copy reads as a guarantee that is false for signed-in users and sits beside a footer promise of no bank logins ever.  <br>*Cause:* Guest-mode copy was kept after accounts, KV backup, translate and vision features were added.  <br>*Fix:* Rewrite as a factual description per mode (guest: this device only; signed in: encrypted backup) and remove absolute promises.
- [ ] **Calendar alignment assignment typo** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The calendar rendered with broken alignment.  <br>*Cause:* A typo in the alignment assignment for the calendar.  <br>*Fix:* Corrected the alignment assignment in review.
- [ ] **Jumbled platform-mix card in summary band** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The platform-mix card layout appeared jumbled in the planner summary band.  <br>*Fix:* Reworked the platform-mix card layout in the summary band.
- [ ] **recalc.py crashed on Python 3.9** `other`
  *Symptom:* The recalc verification script threw a TypeError.  <br>*Cause:* ignore_cleanup_errors is unsupported on Python 3.9.  <br>*Fix:* Replaced it with an AppleScript-driven Excel recalc harness.

## Bug Ledger  ·  17 fixed

- [ ] **Harvester copied a live sign-in PIN verbatim into an append-only ledger row and filed it under the wrong app** `security`
  *Symptom:* A harvested row reads "Live: https://joy-day.coconvo.workers.dev · PIN <6 digits> (saved in /.joyday/config.json)" under Hallalu CRM. GET /api/activity and the other read routes need no key, so the PIN is publicly readable, and because the ledger is append-only it cannot be removed or edited afterwards  <br>*Cause:* The transcript harvester lifts assistant sentences as findings with no secret filter; it runs unattended hourly and twice daily from launchd and deploys the public ledger when anything changed, with no human review; and the session had printed the PIN in chat and in a file caption  <br>*Fix:* Open: add a redaction pass to harvest.mjs (PIN or passcode followed by digits, key=, token-like strings, long hex or base64) that drops the row, and allow a tombstone or redact action for secrets only; rotate the exposed PIN
- [ ] **Editing the generated public/bugs.json lost eight appended entries when gen.mjs regenerated it** `data-loss`
  *Symptom:* Entries added by hand to public/bugs.json vanished after the next gen.mjs run.  <br>*Cause:* public/bugs.json is generated from the root bugs.json, so edits to the output are overwritten.  <br>*Fix:* Edit only the source file, mark the generated file read-only with a header comment, and have gen.mjs refuse to run when the output has unsaved differences.
- [ ] **Catalogue cache stored an empty result after one transient fetch miss, so coverage read 0/0 until the isolate recycled** `logic`
  *Symptom:* After a deploy the full-catalogue scan reported 0/0 and /api/checks coverage was wrong; the next requests on the same isolate kept the empty value  <br>*Cause:* catalog() cached whatever the static checklist.json fetch returned, including a failure, in a module-level variable  <br>*Fix:* Cache only a successful, non-empty result and retry on the next request; the test is to fail the first fetch and expect the second request to recover
- [ ] **GET /api/bugs?limit=1000 is silently capped at 200 with no paging, so agents read a truncated ledger** `logic`
  *Symptom:* A request for 1000 returned 200 rows and no next cursor, hiding older entries from dedupe and harvesting.  <br>*Cause:* The list endpoint clamps limit without reporting the cap or offering offset or cursor.  <br>*Fix:* Return total and next in the response and support before=<ts> paging; document the cap in AGENT.md.
- [ ] **Harvested layer is mostly clipped assistant sentences: 73% end in an ellipsis, none carries a file, and 52% are filed to one app** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Of 399 harvested rows in the live ledger 291 have titles cut mid-sentence at about 80 characters, 28 begin with a bullet dash, about 89 begin with narration such as 'One caveat', 'Here' or 'Now', every one has file null, and 206 are attributed to Hallalu CRM; rows from a disk-cleanup session were filed under Hallalu CRM and Wedding Planner  <br>*Cause:* harvest.mjs lifts sentences from assistant messages with a keyword match, truncates the title for display, takes the app from the working-directory guess, and has no step that requires a symptom, a file, or a fix. The first live pass already showed it: all 30 candidates came from one large transcript, titles carried markdown artefacts and planning narration, and the narration filter and per-session cap were bolted on afterwards  <br>*Fix:* Open: require a bug-shaped record (symptom plus a file or command) before filing, store the full sentence in the note and a derived short title, resolve the app from the files touched, and count harvested rows separately from curated ones in the KPIs
- [ ] **Report page dies on a transient fetch failure ('Couldn't load — Failed to fetch')** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Opening a sweep report during a network blip or mid-deploy showed 'Couldn't load — TypeError: Failed to fetch' and never recovered.  <br>*Cause:* The report did Promise.all of three fetches; /api/checks had no .catch, so any one rejecting failed the whole load, with no retry.  <br>*Fix:* Wrapped each fetch in a retrying getJSON() (2 retries + backoff) that returns a fallback instead of rejecting; on empty data show a friendly 'connection dropped' + a Retry link instead of a raw error.
- [ ] **Sweep report attributed ledger additions by a 30-minute time window instead of by session, so a concurrent session's entries appeared as this sweep's finds** `logic`
  *Symptom:* The 'Added to the ledger from this sweep' card listed submissions made within about 30 minutes of the sweep timestamp (first filtered by app, then widened to all apps), with provenance only as a project label  <br>*Cause:* Submissions carry no session id, so the report infers membership from time proximity; with several agents writing at once the window mixes them  <br>*Fix:* Open: stamp every /api/bugs submission with the active worklog session id (or require one) and have the report and timeline group additions by that id
- [ ] **Apps KPI counted non-app buckets such as cross-cutting and Unknown, showing 18 apps instead of 16** `claims`
  *Symptom:* The headline said 18 apps while only 16 real apps existed, and AGENT.md, llms.txt and the checklist totals repeated a hardcoded 18.  <br>*Cause:* gen.mjs used appOrder.length in seven places and the order list includes non-app buckets; the number was typed into docs.  <br>*Fix:* Compute realAppCount excluding cross-cutting and Unknown and generate every published count from it.
- [ ] **Marketing set shows AI-generated people disclosed on only 1 of 7 photo pieces, with a composite confession quote (flagged, not fixed)** `claims`
  *Symptom:* A 2026 study found women rate an ad lower when the model is labelled AI-generated, yet only the Story carried a disclosure; the pain quote was modelled on a real complaint, not a testimonial.  <br>*Cause:* Disclosure was treated as a per-format option and composites were not labelled.  <br>*Fix:* Decide one policy (disclose on every AI-person piece or commission real photography) and label composite quotes as illustrative.
- [ ] **An append-only trigger rejection reached the client as a raw HTTP 500 with the SQL error text** `observability`
  *Symptom:* Re-opening a finished worklog session was correctly refused by the database trigger but the response was a 500 containing the raw error string, so agents could not tell tampering from an outage  <br>*Cause:* The write paths caught every error and returned String(e) with status 500; the worker pre-check missed it because a read replica can lag behind the finish write  <br>*Fix:* Map trigger aborts to a 403 with a clear message through one writeErr helper used by every write path, and keep the trigger as the authority
- [ ] **GET /api/bugs returns entries under 'submitted' capped at 100, so naive parsers read an empty ledger** `observability`
  *Symptom:* Checking that a POST landed, a parser looking for bugs/items/data reported 'total in ledger: 0' although the entry was the newest of 100 returned.  <br>*Cause:* The list response is {ok,count:100,submitted:[...]} with the key 'submitted', each row using 'ts' rather than 'created', and it is silently limited to the latest 100.  <br>*Fix:* Document the shape and add ?id=/?limit=/?q= filters, or alias the key as 'items'. Agents should verify a write with the POST response id or a keyed lookup.
- [ ] **Worklog narration through a zsh variable with stderr sent to /dev/null failed silently for the whole session** `observability`
  *Symptom:* Every narration call failed, the story page showed 6 findings still open, and the finished session (HTTP 409) could not be corrected afterwards.  <br>*Cause:* A variable holding a two-word command is one word in zsh, and 2>/dev/null hid the resulting command-not-found.  <br>*Fix:* Call the CLI directly (node path/worklog.mjs ...), never hide stderr on state-changing calls, and check the first call's output.
- [ ] **Activity timeline re-renders every poll, replaying entrance animations (flicker)** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The /timeline feed rebuilt its whole innerHTML on every 4s poll even when nothing changed, so every card replayed its 'rise' entrance animation and the page visibly flickered.  <br>*Cause:* render() unconditionally set feed.innerHTML each poll; recreated DOM nodes re-run their CSS entrance animation. (Same class as the earlier /live blink, but the timeline was missed.)  <br>*Fix:* Compute a content signature from stable fields (event ids/status/coverage/progress) and skip the re-render when it's unchanged; only rebuild when content actually changes.
- [ ] **Automated git commit used `git add -A`, sweeping a co-editing session's work** `other`
  *Symptom:* The hourly harvester's auto-commit ran mid-work and its `git add -A` staged an in-progress session's unrelated edits, committing them under the wrong message.  <br>*Cause:* harvest.mjs/scan.mjs staged everything (`git add -A`) instead of only their own generated files.  <br>*Fix:* Stage an explicit allow-list of the tool's own outputs (harvested.json / scan-findings.json + their gen artefacts), never `git add -A`. (This is the 'stage only files you touched' optimiser.)
- [ ] **Deepscan bug-phase total includes category:security titles, so a payload without them scored 298/337** `other`
  *Symptom:* The first coverage post returned 298/337 with 39 missed items, all security-category bugs.  <br>*Cause:* The skill computes the bug total over every catalog title but its prose reads as if security items belong only to phase 2.  <br>*Fix:* State in the skill that phase 1 must list every title (security included) in found or notFound, or exclude them from the bug total consistently.
- [ ] **Scanner INVIS-HIDDEN flags legitimate U+00A0 placeholders, a deliberate BOM stripper and thin-space number formatting as smuggled Unicode** `other`
  *Symptom:* finished-app produced INVIS-HIDDEN highs at Notes.tsx:135-140 (U+00A0 in JSX {p.content || ' '}), PinPad.tsx:50, calc.ts:226 (thin space) and kindle.ts:11 (intentional BOM strip); all were false positives after a codepoint-level read.  <br>*Cause:* The detector treats any non-ASCII whitespace as suspicious instead of limiting to zero-width, bidi-control and invisible format characters.  <br>*Fix:* Triaged as false positives with the exact codepoints; detector should flag only U+200B-200F, U+202A-202E, U+2060-2064 and a mid-file U+FEFF, and allow U+00A0/U+202F/U+2009 inside JSX text and string literals.
- [ ] **Sweep commands and reports did not say whether they checked one project or the whole catalogue, so "full" was ambiguous** `other`
  *Symptom:* After a deep security scan the owner asked whether it covered only the security items for the project in hand or every project; the prompts, task labels and final report gave no scope line  <br>*Cause:* The command text described the counter (N of total) but not the scope (app, all, security, optimisers) the server was scoring against  <br>*Fix:* Added a scope note to /securitysweep, /fullscan and /deepscan, task labels that name the scope, and an explicit findings summary at the end of the report

## Evertrue  ·  17 fixed

- [ ] **Etsy OAuth access/refresh tokens and shared secret stored as plaintext columns in D1** `security`
  *Symptom:* The etsy_links table keeps shared_secret, access_token and refresh_token as plain TEXT, so anyone with D1 read access (a dump or an over-broad query) gets live read access to sellers' Etsy shops.  <br>*Cause:* The tokens are kept server-side (good) but not encrypted at rest.  <br>*Fix:* Left open. Encrypt the three columns with AES-GCM using a key derived from a Worker secret before insert, decrypt on use, and add a revoke/delete-on-disconnect check.
- [ ] **renderLanding assumed DOM nodes that do not exist in some states (null deref blanked the landing page)** `crash`
  *Symptom:* Landing view threw on a missing element and rendered nothing for logged-out visitors in one state.  <br>*Cause:* Unconditional getElementById(...).addEventListener / .innerHTML on nodes absent from that template variant.  <br>*Fix:* Null-guard each lookup and render defensively; test every route logged-out and logged-in.
- [ ] **root.App.applyRings called before App existed: blank SPA on cold load** `crash`
  *Symptom:* The whole app rendered blank on first load; console showed 'Cannot read properties of undefined (applyRings)'.  <br>*Cause:* A vendored module called root.App.applyRings at script-evaluation time, before app.js had assigned root.App (script order / IIFE timing).  <br>*Fix:* Call lazily inside render/after DOMContentLoaded and guard with root.App && root.App.applyRings; add a boot smoke test that fails on any uncaught error at load.
- [ ] **Etsy shows connected but syncs nothing: x-api-key was sent as keystring:shared_secret** `auth`
  *Symptom:* The user entered their Etsy keys, OAuth succeeded, and the sync reported 'Synced 0 and 0' with no listings or orders.  <br>*Cause:* etsyGet built the x-api-key header as keystring + ':' + shared_secret whenever a shared secret was saved. Etsy v3 expects the keystring only, so every data call was rejected after OAuth had succeeded. Shop-id extraction from /users/{id}/shops was also brittle.  <br>*Fix:* x-api-key is the keystring only; a pickShop() helper extracts the shop id defensively; the sync returns diagnostics and the client explains a connected-but-empty result instead of printing 'Synced 0 and 0'; the shared-secret field is labelled as not needed.
- [ ] **Sample (demo) owner would schedule sync pushes to the server** `sync`
  *Symptom:* In ?sample=1 mode, edits to the seeded shop would queue /api/sync calls and send demo data to a real account.  <br>*Cause:* The store's schedule() and sync() guards only excluded owner 'guest', not the 'sample' owner.  <br>*Fix:* Excluded owner === 'sample' in schedule(), sync() and the post-put reschedule so sample mode never syncs; caught before first deploy.
- [ ] **Analysis 'Orders · 30 days' delta compared a rolling 30-day count with the previous calendar month** `logic`
  *Symptom:* The orders KPI delta was misleading because the two figures covered different windows.  <br>*Cause:* The delta used months.slice(-2)[0].orders (a calendar month) as the baseline for a rolling 30-day count.  <br>*Fix:* Compute o30p (the 30 days before the current 30) and compare like for like.
- [ ] **Download menu PDF and CSV builders received undefined sheets; zip had no MIME type** `logic`
  *Symptom:* After adding PDF/CSV to the download menu both buttons failed on the live page while the xlsx worked.  <br>*Cause:* workbook() returned only {blob,name,tabs}, so pdfDoc() and exportCsvZip() had no sheets to read. SheetExport.zip() also returned bytes not wrapped in a Blob with type application/zip.  <br>*Fix:* Return {sheets, blob, name, tabs} from workbook() and wrap the zip in new Blob([...],{type:'application/zip'}).
- [ ] **Monthly goal widget ignored revenue and orders goals and edited the wrong month** `logic`
  *Symptom:* After adding a goal the dashboard widget still said 'Set a monthly goal'.  <br>*Cause:* The dashboard widget only checked goal.profit, and editGoal() edited the Money view's filter month instead of the month the dashboard shows (thisMonth()). The word 'goal' also meant four different things (Studio listing challenge, Routine, Dashboard, Money), so users could not tell which one they had set.  <br>*Fix:* The widget honours profit, revenue or orders; editGoal takes the month it was opened for; a Today's goal card now leads the dashboard and a monthly-goal card sits on Money; the Studio copy reads 'New listings to publish in October 2026' with a plain explanation of monthly goal versus daily challenge.
- [ ] **Paste-a-link unfurl returned 403 for Etsy (bot-blocked): feature showed 'failed' with no fallback** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Pasting an Etsy listing link produced an error; the product title/image were never filled.  <br>*Cause:* Server-side fetch of etsy.com is blocked by bot protection (403); the route treated any non-200 as failure.  <br>*Fix:* Treat 403 as 'site blocks automated fetch', fall back to parsing the URL slug for a title and ask the user to paste the details/CSV; never imply the link was verified.
- [ ] **Routine/To-Do seeding wrote sample rows into REAL accounts** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Real sellers opened the Routine/Checklist and found demo tasks they never created.  <br>*Cause:* The seed path keyed off 'no data yet' instead of 'is the sample owner', so any empty real account was seeded.  <br>*Fix:* Gate all demo seeding on the sample owner (?sample=1) only; migration to remove seeded rows from real accounts by id prefix.
- [ ] **Sample shop seeds only when empty, so returning visitors never receive new sample content** `logic`
  *Symptom:* After new sample content shipped (banner, logos, second shop), the same browser kept the old sample data and the new features did not appear until localStorage keys were cleared.  <br>*Cause:* seedSample() runs only when the sample orders collection is empty, and there is no seed version stamp.  <br>*Fix:* Verification workaround was to clear ev_data_sample, ev_meta_sample and ev_shop_sample. The proper fix is to store a seed version and reseed on mismatch.
- [ ] **Sample-inbox seed never re-ran for returning sample users: sample accounts stayed empty after a data fix** `logic`
  *Symptom:* After seeding logic was improved, anyone who had already entered sample mode saw old/empty data; only brand-new browsers saw the fix.  <br>*Cause:* seedSampleInbox() was guarded by a 'seeded once' flag stored with the user, so changes to the seed were never applied to existing sample users.  <br>*Fix:* Version the seed (seedVersion), and re-seed sample owners when the stored version is older; for real accounts never seed at all.
- [ ] **Star Seller sales minimum hard-coded at USD $300 for shops in every currency** `claims`
  *Symptom:* GBP, EUR, CAD and AUD shops would be measured against $300.  <br>*Cause:* defaultStar() used minSales: 300 regardless of shop currency. Etsy's primary Star Seller page lists USD $300, GBP £200, EUR €250, CAD $350, AUD $350 (item sales before shipping/tax, cancelled excluded).  <br>*Fix:* Added a STAR_MIN_SALES map keyed by shop currency (other currencies fall back to $300 USD), read from help.etsy.com on 2026-09-03; the About page and report were updated.
- [ ] **Sample product images were generated on demand at first view (slow, flaky, billed): precompute static-first** `perf`
  *Symptom:* Sample gallery showed blank tiles for seconds and sometimes permanently when generation failed or rate-limited.  <br>*Cause:* Images were baked lazily per request instead of being shipped as static assets.  <br>*Fix:* Bake once, commit the files under public/, reference them by static URL; keep generation out of the user path entirely.
- [ ] **Modal closed on any click inside it because the overlay carried data-close** `ui`
  *Symptom:* Clicking inside the modal body (fields, buttons) dismissed the modal.  <br>*Cause:* The overlay element had data-close and the global handler used e.target.closest('[data-close]'), which matched the overlay for every inner click.  <br>*Fix:* Removed data-close from the overlay and simplified the handler to close only on real [data-close] controls; found pre-deploy.
- [ ] **Paste-a-link fallback through keyless reader proxies cannot get past Etsy's 403 from Cloudflare egress** `other`
  *Symptom:* Etsy listing URLs return 403 to the Worker; the planned fallback (allorigins, codetabs, Jina reader) looked right in code and was assumed to work.  <br>*Cause:* Probed from real Cloudflare egress with wrangler dev --remote: direct 403, allorigins and codetabs time out, Jina 403. Probing from the local network was unrepresentative because it was also blocked. Only a keyed scraper (Firecrawl, as Hallalu uses) works.  <br>*Fix:* Firecrawl first when FIRECRAWL_API_KEY is set, keyless proxies kept as best-effort with short timeouts, and the import copy says Etsy needs the key. Lesson: prove a fallback from the production egress before shipping it.
- [ ] **Timetable empty days were not clickable: nowhere to add a block** `undefined`
  *Symptom:* Clicking an empty day in the timetable did nothing; users could only add blocks from days that already had one.  <br>*Cause:* Click handler was bound to rendered blocks, not to the day cell.  <br>*Fix:* Bind the add-block handler to the day cell (event delegation) and show an affordance on hover/focus.

## Planner Studio  ·  16 fixed

- [ ] **arguments.callee ReferenceError breaks task ticking** `crash`
  *Symptom:* To-do items wouldn't tick off; the first render's refresh callback threw silently.  <br>*Cause:* `arguments.callee` used inside an arrow function throws a ReferenceError in strict mode.  <br>*Fix:* Replaced it with a hoisted `refreshTasks` declaration.
- [ ] **Gradient theme crash from id mismatch** `crash`
  *Symptom:* The gradient theme never applied because its checks never matched.  <br>*Cause:* Theme objects used `id:'grad'` but code checked `t.grad`.  <br>*Fix:* Changed all references to `t.id === 'grad'`.
- [ ] **Voice-search mic threw because voiceSearch() was never defined** `crash`
  *Symptom:* Tapping the voice-search mic did nothing and threw a ReferenceError  <br>*Cause:* The voiceSearch() handler was wired to the button but never actually implemented  <br>*Fix:* Implemented voiceSearch() properly (found during the static + in-browser smoke sweep)
- [ ] **Service worker cached a 404 for a later-added file** `sync`
  *Symptom:* A vendor file added after first cache stayed permanently broken.  <br>*Cause:* The service worker had cached a 404 from before the file existed and kept serving it.  <br>*Fix:* Fixed the worker/caching path so all vendor files serve correctly.
- [ ] **Book-cover auto-fetch hung on a CORS blob fetch** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Fetching a cover from Open Library hung/failed, so covers never appeared after adding a book  <br>*Cause:* Code did a cross-origin blob fetch of the cover image, which CORS blocked  <br>*Fix:* Store the cover URL directly and render it via <img> (cross-origin <img> loads fine and is lighter to sync) instead of fetching a blob
- [ ] **Global search returned zero results after new sections were added** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Search returned 0 hits while the app claimed you could 'find anything you've ever written'  <br>*Cause:* Notes, Books, and Content views were added without extending the search index to include their records  <br>*Fix:* Extended the search index to cover notes, books, content, and journal entries
- [ ] **Monochrome theme hue not updating** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Changing the mono hue had no effect.  <br>*Fix:* Fixed the mono hue to update.
- [ ] **OCR web worker used relative paths that don't resolve in worker scope** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Tesseract 'Magic Fill' pipeline failed to load its worker/core; status stalled and OCR never ran  <br>*Cause:* Worker and LSTM core assets were referenced by relative URL, which do not resolve inside a Web Worker execution context  <br>*Fix:* Switched the worker/core references to absolute URLs (and copied the missing LSTM core variants so all vendor files serve)
- [ ] **Schedule data-model change regression** `logic`
  *Symptom:* After changing schedule data from string to an object, consumers still reading it as a string could break (calm mode, week view, search, celebrate cards).  <br>*Cause:* Schedule model changed from string to `{t,c,done,note}` but readers of `D.sched[h]` weren't updated.  <br>*Fix:* Updated the consumers to the new object model.
- [ ] **'0/0' task counter not updating live** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The task counter stayed at 0/0 instead of reflecting progress.  <br>*Cause:* The counter wasn't refreshed after a toggle.  <br>*Fix:* Made the counter update live.
- [ ] **Bookshelf niche rendered dark in light themes because data-dark=0 still matched [data-dark]** `ui`
  *Symptom:* In pastel/light themes the bookshelf back-panel niche rendered with the dark-mode material  <br>*Cause:* The attribute selector body[data-dark] matches whenever the attribute is present, and data-dark=0 is always present, so light mode matched the dark rule  <br>*Fix:* Target the explicit value with body[data-dark=1] instead of the bare presence selector
- [ ] **Both modal dialogs render on load** `ui`
  *Symptom:* Two modal sheets both showed on first boot.  <br>*Cause:* A `display:grid` CSS rule overrode the `hidden` attribute.  <br>*Fix:* Corrected the CSS so hidden sheets stay hidden and the app boots to Today.
- [ ] **Duplicate calmBtn DOM id** `ui`
  *Symptom:* Two elements shared the `calmBtn` id.  <br>*Cause:* The Today hero button reused an existing element id.  <br>*Fix:* Renamed the Today hero button to `calmStart`.
- [ ] **Kawaii theme buttons unreadable** `ui`
  *Symptom:* Kawaii-theme buttons used white ink that failed contrast.  <br>*Cause:* Light ink on light buttons.  <br>*Fix:* Applied dark ink to kawaii buttons per the contrast research.
- [ ] **Meal grid misaligns below 860px** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The meal grid broke below 860px, silently misaligning days against meals.  <br>*Cause:* Responsive grid layout failed under the 860px breakpoint.  <br>*Fix:* Fixed the grid so days and meals stay aligned at narrow widths.
- [ ] **PDF cover text overlapping** `ui`
  *Symptom:* Cover words overlaid each other on the planner's first page/PDF cover.  <br>*Cause:* Insufficient vertical spacing in the cover layout.  <br>*Fix:* Fixed spacing between title, subtitle and year, and rebuilt all 12 PDFs.

## Coco Modules  ·  14 fixed

- [ ] **Edited the wrong modules.json: only public/modules.json is served, so the live catalog never updated** `sync`
  *Symptom:* After editing the manifest and deploying, the live /modules.json still listed 3 modules and no voice-mic. It happened again after an earlier change that had touched both copies.  <br>*Cause:* The manifest exists twice (repo root and public/); only public/ is served. The root copy was edited and deployed.  <br>*Fix:* Copied root to public/ and redeployed. Needs a single source, a generate-on-deploy step, or a predeploy diff check.
- [ ] **Module change committed by a sibling session but never deployed: live served mixed versions of the same feature** `sync`
  *Symptom:* Live abba-backgrounds.js contained Sunset but live theme-studio.js lacked the new rename control and the catalog tagline still said 16, while git HEAD already had the .js changes.  <br>*Cause:* Commit was treated as done; the sibling session pushed the .js edits without running wrangler deploy for the modules Worker.  <br>*Fix:* Deployed with wrangler@4.28.1 and verified by curl-grepping a unique marker in each live asset (id: 'sunset', data-e, 'The 18 background themes'), instead of trusting git status.
- [ ] **Two copies of modules.json (root and public/) drifted: root was missing the double-tap-speak module** `sync`
  *Symptom:* The served public/modules.json listed one more module than the root manifest (14 vs 13 before the goals add), so the root copy was missing double-tap-speak.  <br>*Cause:* Every module add has to edit both manifests by hand and nothing checks they match; only public/ is served.  <br>*Fix:* Synced root to public/ and kept both identical on each add (both validated as 1-space JSON); better: generate one from the other or add a diff check.
- [ ] **goal-celebrate inferred a filler word as the goal unit ('List 12 new items' -> unit 'new')** `logic`
  *Symptom:* A numeric goal showed the wrong unit label because the first word after the number was used verbatim.  <br>*Cause:* Unit extraction took the first token after the number without skipping filler words (new, more, of, the, a, extra, fresh).  <br>*Fix:* Skip filler words and take the next real noun, so it now yields items / orders / sales / $; verified on 6 phrasings.
- [ ] **pdf-export turned em dashes and euro signs into '?' (text encoded twice)** `logic`
  *Symptom:* A 'a — b · £5 €6' string rendered as 'a ? b · £5 ?6' in Preview.  <br>*Cause:* The WinAnsi encoder ran twice over the same string. Pass 1 mapped the em dash to byte 151 and the euro to 128, and pass 2 only kept bytes 160-255, so 128-159 became '?'.  <br>*Fix:* Made enc() idempotent by keeping 128-255 as already-mapped; verified bytes 151/128/133 survive.
- [ ] **routine-timetable parser ate the leading digit of a typed time and left the time phrase in the title** `logic`
  *Symptom:* '9:00 reply to messages 30m weekdays' parsed to title '00 reply to messages' with no start time, and '7am coffee and inbox 20m' kept '7am' inside the title.  <br>*Cause:* The bullet-stripping regex treated digits as list markers, and the matched time/range phrase was never cut out of the title.  <br>*Fix:* Strip only a real bullet (dash, dot, star, or digits followed by . or )) and remove the matched time/range span from the title; re-ran 13 sentence fixtures.
- [ ] **Theme count was stale in six places after adding Sunset/Sunrise (code 17, README 14, tagline 16, gallery cards 16/14, theme-studio README 16)** `claims`
  *Symptom:* abba-backgrounds.js had 17 themes while its README said 14, modules.json (root and public) and both index.html gallery cards said 16 and the theme-studio README said 16.  <br>*Cause:* Counts are hand-typed in the header comment, README, two modules.json copies and the gallery cards, and drift on every theme added.  <br>*Fix:* Updated all six places (14/16 to 17, later 18) and re-validated both JSON files; the durable fix is to derive the number from AbbaBackgrounds.list().length at publish time instead of typing it.
- [ ] **heart-bell MutationObserver observed its own innerHTML write: infinite microtask loop froze any app on load** `perf`
  *Symptom:* Abba (and any app embedding the shared heart-bell module) hung silently on load when authenticated: main thread pegged, no console error, logged-out view fine.  <br>*Cause:* heart-bell.js observed document.body with {subtree:true}; its paintAll() sets innerHTML on a node inside body, so the observer fired on its own mutation and re-queued paintAll forever (microtask loop, never yields to paint).  <br>*Fix:* disconnect() the observer around every write and re-observe after, plus a requestAnimationFrame debounce; shipped as module v1 to v2 and redeployed modules.coconvo. General rule: never let an observer's callback write inside the subtree it observes without disconnecting first.
- [ ] **Live board cards blinked every poll** `perf`
  *Symptom:* Activity timelines/boards re-appended cards each poll, replaying the CSS entrance animation so cards flickered every ~1.5s.  <br>*Cause:* Re-render/re-append replayed the one-time entrance animation.  <br>*Fix:* One-shot .enter class stripped after it plays; re-render only when content or order changes. Shipped as a reusable module.
- [ ] **activity-timeline keeps light cards under dark themes** `ui`
  *Symptom:* Dropped into a dark app the timeline cards stayed white with dark text.  <br>*Cause:* The module hardcodes light surface colours instead of reading the host theme tokens.  <br>*Fix:* Use CSS variables with light defaults (--atl-card, --atl-ink) and map them from the host theme.
- [ ] **theme-studio swatch chips collapsed because the span had no display:block** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The live-preview chips rendered as thin inline slivers.  <br>*Cause:* Height and width do not apply to an inline span.  <br>*Fix:* Set display:block on .ts-chip (done) and test the gallery once in a host with a reset stylesheet.
- [ ] **to-do-list add box lost focus after each add because the list re-rendered, so rapid Enter-to-add felt broken** `ui`
  *Symptom:* After pressing Enter once the input blurred and further typing went nowhere.  <br>*Cause:* addItem() re-rendered the whole view, destroying the focused input.  <br>*Fix:* Added a keepFocus flag so the new input is re-focused after render.
- [ ] **to-do-list streak card rendered dark in light mode: the dark override sat outside the @media (prefers-color-scheme: dark) block** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The routine streak card was a dark panel on a light page.  <br>*Cause:* The rule '.tdl:not([data-theme="light"]) .tdl-streak' was written as a normal selector, not inside the media query, so it applied in every colour scheme.  <br>*Fix:* Kept only the explicit [data-theme="dark"] selector at top level and moved the :not([data-theme="light"]) variant inside the media query; verified light and dark in the browser.
- [ ] **Registry edited in the wrong copy: root modules.json is not served, public/modules.json is, and the count had drifted** `other`
  *Symptom:* A new module was added and deployed, but the live /modules.json still showed count 26 without it. The plain URL also served a stale cf-cache-status HIT for a while after deploy.  <br>*Cause:* There are two copies. The Worker serves ./public/modules.json, but the root copy was edited first. The hand-maintained 'count' field was already off by one (26 vs 27 entries).  <br>*Fix:* Same insertion applied to public/modules.json, count set to the array length, and the live registry verified with a ?v= cache-bust. Longer term, keep one source and generate count at deploy.

## Fullfill  ·  13 fixed

- [ ] **Auth fail-open and shared static cookie: authed() allowed everyone when ACCESS_CODE was unset; docs/files were unscoped** `security`
  *Symptom:* With no ACCESS_CODE secret configured every request was treated as authenticated; with it set, the session cookie was a static sha256('ff:'+code) shared by all users, every document and file was readable by any holder, and login had no rate limit.  <br>*Cause:* authed() returned true when the secret was missing (fail-open); the cookie was a deterministic hash of the shared code rather than a per-session random token; no owner column on docs/files; no lockout.  <br>*Fix:* Real accounts: PBKDF2 + pepper password hashing, hashed random session tokens, owner column on every doc/file with cross-account access returning 404, lockout/rate limit, recovery code. Fail closed when a secret is missing. Open sign-up remains and needs a decision.
- [ ] **Field font names were interpolated into inline style attributes without sanitising (style injection sink)** `security`
  *Symptom:* A tampered form definition could put arbitrary CSS into the public fill page via the font family/size strings.  <br>*Cause:* fill.js built style strings directly from stored font values.  <br>*Fix:* Sanitise font strings against the known catalogue/characters before injecting them.
- [ ] **Public submit endpoint accepted unbounded PDF uploads (storage and denial-of-wallet abuse)** `security`
  *Symptom:* Anyone with a share link could post an arbitrarily large filled PDF that the Worker stored in R2/D1.  <br>*Cause:* The public submission route had no request/body size limit.  <br>*Fix:* Reject PDFs over about 10 MB with 413 before storing.
- [ ] **Share link filled a stale fillable PDF: recipient saw the old document version** `data-loss`
  *Symptom:* A sent signing/fill link showed an older copy of the form than the one the sender last edited.  <br>*Cause:* The link captured the PDF at creation time while edits were stored separately and never re-bound; the recipient route loaded the first file.  <br>*Fix:* Resolve the envelope's current file/version at open time and bump a revision; show the recipient the latest rendition, and block sending an envelope with unsaved edits.
- [ ] **pdf-lib flatten() left dangling /Annots references that other viewers rejected** `crash`
  *Symptom:* Flattened exports opened in pdf.js but showed errors or blank pages in Preview/Acrobat.  <br>*Cause:* form.flatten() removes widgets from the AcroForm but pages kept /Annots entries pointing at deleted objects.  <br>*Fix:* After flatten, clear or filter each page's /Annots to only live refs, then save; verify the export in more than one viewer.
- [ ] **Uploads froze in a hidden/background tab: requestAnimationFrame-driven pipeline never advanced** `race`
  *Symptom:* Import progress stalled at the first stage whenever the tab was not frontmost (including headless/background-pane tests).  <br>*Cause:* The page-render/upload pipeline awaited rAF callbacks, which browsers pause in hidden tabs.  <br>*Fix:* Use setTimeout/MessageChannel or a promise-based yield for pipeline steps; reserve rAF for visual updates only. Also front the tab when testing in the pane.
- [ ] **Dark fill band (shaded header strip) counted as a line: phantom fields and mis-snapped baselines** `logic`
  *Symptom:* A solid shaded band was detected as many horizontal lines, producing a stack of phantom fields.  <br>*Cause:* Line detector used a global darkness threshold; a filled band satisfies 'dark run of N pixels' for every row.  <br>*Fix:* Add a local-contrast test (the line must differ from rows directly above and below) and cap line thickness; reject filled rectangles.
- [ ] **Export misplaced fields on rotated/cropped PDFs (off by ~330pt)** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Fields filled in the editor landed far from their position in the downloaded PDF for pages with /Rotate or a non-zero CropBox.  <br>*Cause:* Placement used the MediaBox origin and ignored CropBox offset and /Rotate when mapping viewport coordinates to PDF user space.  <br>*Fix:* Map through the page's rotation and CropBox origin (translate then rotate); regression-tested on rotated/cropped pages, error 330pt to under 1pt.
- [ ] **Round buttons, caption-as-title and letter-top phantom lines produced false fields** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Radio/round buttons became text fields, a caption was used as the field title, and the tops of letters were detected as underlines.  <br>*Cause:* Pixel scanner had no shape classification and no minimum line length/aspect test relative to nearby text height.  <br>*Fix:* Classify near-square/round blobs as checkbox/radio, require line length >= several times local glyph height, and prefer the nearest label to the left/above rather than any caption.
- [ ] **Reasoning model timed out on field-labelling: default reasoning effort too high for a Worker request** `perf`
  *Symptom:* AI label pass returned 504/timeouts on busy forms.  <br>*Cause:* Workers AI reasoning model ran at default reasoning effort; latency exceeded the request budget.  <br>*Fix:* Set reasoning_effort low, benchmark candidate models on the synthetic ground-truth form, and pick the fastest model that holds accuracy (non-reasoning vision model for this task).
- [ ] **Small-square (checkbox) detection pass was quadratic over short ink segments and hung field detection** `perf`
  *Symptom:* After adding the checkbox snap pass, ingest showed 0 fields and empty pickers; the page sat on 'Rendering' with no console error.  <br>*Cause:* The new pass compared every short horizontal/vertical ink segment with every other one, and text glyphs produce thousands of short segments.  <br>*Fix:* Rewrote the pass with bounded scans over size-limited candidates; detection returned and 6 fields snapped pixel-perfect.
- [ ] **Editor could not scroll: flex children shrank to viewport height and clipped the page** `ui`
  *Symptom:* On long PDFs the editor canvas was clipped and no scrollbar appeared, so lower pages were unreachable.  <br>*Cause:* Page container was a flex child with default flex-shrink:1 and overflow hidden, so it shrank to the container instead of growing.  <br>*Fix:* flex:none (or min-height:0 on the scroller plus flex-shrink:0 on pages) so the scroll container owns the overflow.
- [ ] **Generated PDF rendered a font-glyph calendar icon as a solid black square and a two-digit step badge wrapped** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The fillable form's calendar affordance was a filled square and step badge '10' wrapped onto two lines in the process report.  <br>*Cause:* The PDF font had no glyph for the icon character so the fallback box was drawn, and the badge box was sized for one digit.  <br>*Fix:* Drew the calendar as vector shapes (rounded box, header bar, rings, dots) and widened the badge; confirmed by rasterising the output.

## Stepapa  ·  12 fixed

- [ ] **Photo AI infers age band, skin tone and facial hair with no age gate** `privacy`
  *Symptom:* A fitness/social app runs an AI pipeline that infers age-band, skin-tone and facial-hair from a user-uploaded photo, and has no age verification or minimum-age gate.  <br>*Cause:* Appearance attributes inferred from images (special-category and children's-data risk under UK GDPR / Children's Code) were built with no lawful basis, DPIA or age assurance.  <br>*Fix:* Open: add an age gate or assurance, make attribute inference opt-in with explicit consent (or drop it), and run a DPIA. Found in the legal inventory, not yet fixed.
- [ ] **Calibration offered to save a useless 0-spm pace when the taps were more than 3 seconds apart (web also auto-saved it)** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Native device testing showed 5 taps spaced over 33 seconds produced 0 spm and 0 confidence, yet the result screen still offered to save the pace; the web flow auto-saved a rhythmless pace.  <br>*Cause:* The result path only warned on implausible step length; a cadence of 0 (every gap rejected as 'you stopped') was never treated as unsaveable.  <br>*Fix:* Show 'Those taps were too far apart' with the accepted gap window instead of a Save action on native (b.pace.cadence <= 0), and apply the same guard on web with no auto-save of a rhythmless pace.
- [ ] **Calibration taps, which are real footfalls, were not added to the day's step total** `logic`
  *Symptom:* After a one-minute pace recording the user's steps for the day stayed unchanged although every tap was a real step.  <br>*Cause:* The calibration save flow stored only the pace record and discarded the tap count.  <br>*Fix:* Add the tap count to today exactly once when calibration finishes (guarded against a double save) in both the web save flow and native PaceLabView.
- [ ] **Native app displayed the phone pedometer count but never persisted it, so the goal ring, coins, badges, widget and sync never saw it** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Today showed the device step count but the day record kept only manually counted steps; goal completion, coins, badges, the widget snapshot and cloud sync all ignored the pedometer. Move also refused to start until a pace had been calibrated.  <br>*Cause:* TodayView took max(store steps, pedometer steps) for display only; CMPedometer readings were never written into the DayRecord, and MoveView disabled Start while the paces list was empty although a height-based step length fallback existed.  <br>*Fix:* Added a persisted deviceSteps field on the day record fed from CMPedometer, dayTotal() used everywhere dayOf().steps was read, a passive web autocount (works with or without a calibrated pace), and removed the calibrate-first gate in web and native Move.
- [ ] **Own counted steps and distance were added to the device count, double counting a day (9,620 steps and 6.80 km instead of 9,120 and 6.43 km)** `logic`
  *Symptom:* With 9,120 device steps and 500 manual steps the ring showed 9,620 and distance 6.80 km; the same walk counted twice.  <br>*Cause:* The day total summed manual and device figures; steps were then switched to max() but the distance path still summed, leaving a sibling double count.  <br>*Fix:* Use the larger of own and device steps (monotonic, never summed) and apply the same max rule to distance, keeping own distance when no device figure exists; 10/10 and 4/4 checks pass with zero calibrated paces.
- [ ] **Pace calibration accepted a physically impossible result (342 cm per step, 22.6 km/h) without warning** `logic`
  *Symptom:* A tap run recovered the right 110 spm cadence but with a bad distance/step input it saved a 3.42 m step length and 22.6 km/h walking speed and showed it as the user's pace.  <br>*Cause:* pace.js never range-checked the derived step length or speed; an impossible distance or step count flowed straight into the saved pace and every later distance estimate.  <br>*Fix:* Added plausibility bounds from gait literature (a sprint stride is about 1.22 m per step, so 1.6 m is a generous ceiling), an implausible-result warning with fallback to the height-based estimate in views.js, 28/28 maths tests still green.
- [ ] **GBP prices shown for virtual-currency purchases that are not wired to any payment processor** `claims`
  *Symptom:* The UI shows GBP prices for buying virtual currency although no payment processor is connected, so users see a purchase offer that cannot complete.  <br>*Cause:* The price UI shipped ahead of the checkout integration.  <br>*Fix:* Open: hide the prices or label them 'coming soon' until real checkout exists. Found in the legal inventory.
- [ ] **Hero ring SVG was a fixed 264px inside a 203px container so the ring overflowed 29px while its text stayed left** `ui`
  *Symptom:* On a narrow phone the steps numeral and plaque sat 29px left of the ring centre and 'STEPS TODAY' collided with the stroke.  <br>*Cause:* The ring SVG had a fixed pixel width while the absolutely positioned inner text box was sized to its container; a cqw-based type scale then measured the whole card (inner 328px against a 264px ring), the --ch digit-count variable never reached the element, and a fit-content wrapper with a 100%-wide SVG collapsed the ring to 0.  <br>*Fix:* Size the ring from one explicit CSS variable on a wrapper that hugs it, drive the numeral size from the digit count and the ring geometry (clear radius 87.6px vs 92.5px corner), verified 8/8 viewport x digit-count combinations with no overflow.
- [ ] **PIN keypad felt sluggish: touch-action auto left the double-tap zoom delay and the keys used only 194px of a 248px card** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The owner reported the keypad hard to press; keys measured 59x54 (above 44pt) but taps registered late.  <br>*Cause:* The keys had touch-action: auto so the browser waited for a double-tap-zoom gesture, and the keypad grid did not fill the card.  <br>*Fix:* touch-action: manipulation on the keys, a wider keypad (86% of the card), taller keys (64x57), pointerdown registering digits; checked at 320, 390 and 768px.
- [ ] **WidgetKit extension installed with GENERATE_INFOPLIST_FILE was rejected by the simulator: no NSExtension dictionary** `other`
  *Symptom:* xcodebuild succeeded and the .appex was embedded (124 KB) but simctl install failed with a placeholder-attributes error; the widget never appeared in the gallery.  <br>*Cause:* The auto-generated Info.plist for the widget target had no NSExtension / NSExtensionPointIdentifier (com.apple.widgetkit-extension) key that WidgetKit requires.  <br>*Fix:* Supplied an explicit Info.plist for the extension only, moved outside the synchronized folder to avoid a duplicate resource copy, then installed and proved the widget in the gallery and on the home screen with real step data.
- [ ] **Xcode build output (ios/build, 4,007 files) was committed and diverged the local and remote histories** `other`
  *Symptom:* git push was rejected with 22 local vs 21 remote commits carrying identical messages but different SHAs; the remote's only unique file was an Xcode build log.  <br>*Cause:* ios/build DerivedData artifacts were never gitignored, so repeated commits tracked thousands of generated files and later rewrites changed the SHAs.  <br>*Fix:* Backed up the remote as backup-remote, untracked ios/build, merged origin/main non-destructively (a force push was blocked) and re-untracked the artifacts the merge brought back; added the ignore.
- [ ] **Xcode scheme resolved to macOS and the build failed on a stale INFOPLIST_FILE reference** `other`
  *Symptom:* First native build tried macOS destinations and then failed on a missing Info-ios.plist even though GENERATE_INFOPLIST_FILE was YES.  <br>*Cause:* Inherited SDKROOT was auto with macosx in SUPPORTED_PLATFORMS so the scheme picked macOS, and the project still pointed INFOPLIST_FILE at a file that does not exist.  <br>*Fix:* Pinned the iOS SDK / simulator destination, removed the stale INFOPLIST_FILE reference (and restored the GENERATE_INFOPLIST_FILE lines a substring filter also removed), then built against the iOS 26.5 SDK for an 18.5 simulator with a 17.0 deployment target.

## Hello Baby  ·  11 fixed

- [ ] **Weak recovery keys and unescaped attribute sinks** `security`
  *Symptom:* Recovery keys were brute-forceable and some attributes were unescaped (XSS risk now that spaces are shared).  <br>*Cause:* Recovery keys used weak `Math.random()`; attribute sinks were unescaped.  <br>*Fix:* Strengthened recovery-key generation and escaped the attribute sinks, in Hello Baby and the same inherited code in Happy Travel.
- [ ] **Countdown hero's corner edit pencil was invisible over the photo and silently wiped the due date with no confirmation** `data-loss`
  *Symptom:* User could not find an edit button on the countdown, and tapping the hidden corner pencil reset the due date instantly.  <br>*Cause:* The only edit affordance sat over the hero photo with low contrast and its handler cleared S.baby.due directly instead of opening an editor or asking first.  <br>*Fix:* Added a visible Edit countdown pill that opens a sheet (name, date, gender) with every reset behind a confirm, plus a confirmed 'close this chapter quietly' path; verified cancel keeps all data.
- [ ] **Entities deleted without confirmation** `data-loss`
  *Symptom:* Five places deleted data without asking.  <br>*Cause:* Missing delete-confirmation guards.  <br>*Fix:* Added delete confirmations to all five places plus broader edit-everything support.
- [ ] **Arrival without due date routed to wrong screen** `logic`
  *Symptom:* A baby with no due date fell into the wrong screen.  <br>*Cause:* Ordering logic mishandled a missing due date.  <br>*Fix:* Route arrival-without-due-date straight to the correct age-based screen (e.g. '3RD BIRTHDAY').
- [ ] **Hero filler image picked from a remembered Unsplash ID showed the wrong subject, and a hand-edited Wikimedia thumbnail width returned no image** `logic`
  *Symptom:* The 'pregnant belly' default loaded baby feet in a blanket, two other remembered IDs failed, and swapping /1920px- for /1400px- in a Wikimedia Commons thumb URL gave naturalWidth 0.  <br>*Cause:* Photo IDs recalled from memory were never rendered and checked; Wikimedia only serves the thumbnail widths it has generated, so a hand-edited width 404s.  <br>*Fix:* Queried the Wikimedia Commons API, auditioned six candidates in an on-page grid, used the exact URL the API returned (verified HTTP 200 and image loads) and noted a licence swap before commercial launch.
- [ ] **App showed Happy Travel nav instead of baby app** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The brand said Hello Baby but the nav showed Happy Travel's 'My Trips / Journey / Passport'.  <br>*Cause:* Leftover Happy Travel app code from the fork.  <br>*Fix:* Restored the correct baby nav (Home / Events / Babymoon & Trips / Milestones).
- [ ] **Baby Tree orbit labels collided: members on adjacent rings stacked on the same spoke and parents sat over the baby's name** `ui`
  *Symptom:* Names such as Mum and Nana, and Dad, Grandpa Joe, Uncle Sam and Tom overlapped on the concentric rings.  <br>*Cause:* Angles were evenly spaced from the top with only ri*0.4 rad per-ring drift, so inner and outer members lined up radially, and long labels had no width cap.  <br>*Fix:* Ring 0 starts at 180 degrees so parents flank the baby left and right, outer rings fan from the top with a per-ring offset of pi/7, avatars shrink on outer rings, labels are single-line with ellipsis on soft backing chips.
- [ ] **Blurry, hard-to-read wording** `ui`
  *Symptom:* Text looked fuzzy and dark numbers collided with the dark silhouette.  <br>*Cause:* A soft glow added for legibility made text fuzzy, and dark-on-dark clashed.  <br>*Fix:* Removed the glow and fixed the number/silhouette contrast.
- [ ] **Budget edits buried and unfindable** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The user couldn't find budget edits because they were buried at the bottom of the Events tab.  <br>*Cause:* Budget was nested inside Events instead of surfaced.  <br>*Fix:* Promoted Budget to its own standalone tab and added a hamburger menu.
- [ ] **Literal \n rendering escaped** `ui`
  *Symptom:* A literal `\n` was showing instead of a line break.  <br>*Cause:* Incorrect newline escaping.  <br>*Fix:* Corrected the escaping.
- [ ] **Photo wallpaper sized with background-size: zoom*100% letterboxed instead of covering the viewport** `ui`
  *Symptom:* A landscape photo behind a portrait viewport left empty bands at the top and bottom, so the wallpaper did not take up 100% of the background.  <br>*Cause:* background-size as a percentage sets width only (height auto), so any image whose aspect differs from the viewport leaves gaps.  <br>*Fix:* Use cover as the baseline and switch to zoom*100% only when zoom is above 1; ported the same rule to Happy Travel, Ever After and Planner Studio and verified bgIsCover in all four.

## Coconvo  ·  10 fixed

- [ ] **Billable Workers AI endpoints had no rate limit (denial-of-wallet)** `security`
  *Symptom:* Anyone holding a room link could POST unlimited audio chunks, note generations and questions to Workers AI, risking a runaway Neurons bill.  <br>*Cause:* The notes/transcription/ask endpoints had no per-room throttling.  <br>*Fix:* Per-room windowed limits in the Room DO: 40 chunks/min, 6 generations/10 min, 30 asks/10 min, 60 uploads/10 min.
- [ ] **Streamed R2 upload needs a known length, and the 25MB size cap trusted Content-Length** `security`
  *Symptom:* Counting upload bytes through a TransformStream before env.MEDIA.put threw on every upload (surfacing as a 413), and a client could declare a small Content-Length and then stream past the 25 MB cap.  <br>*Cause:* R2 put() of a TransformStream-wrapped body requires a declared length, and the cap was enforced from the client-supplied Content-Length rather than the actual bytes.  <br>*Fix:* Pipe the counted stream through FixedLengthStream(declaredLength) and have the counting TransformStream abort the put (and delete the partial object) past 25 MB instead of trusting the header.
- [ ] **/media/* missing from run_worker_first served the SPA shell, so chat images were broken** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Every attached photo rendered as a broken image because /media/<room>/<id> was answered by the static-assets layer with index.html instead of the R2 object.  <br>*Cause:* run_worker_first listed only /api/* and /ws/*, so the Worker (and the R2 streaming route, plus any CSP/cache-control headers it sets) never ran for the new /media/* path.  <br>*Fix:* Set run_worker_first for /media/* (ultimately run_worker_first: true) with a test asserting the Worker answers that path.
- [ ] **max-bundle + a late-added video track triggers a Chrome BUNDLE header-extension collision that kills renegotiation** `logic`
  *Symptom:* With bundlePolicy max-bundle and a recvonly audio transceiver, calling addTrack(video) after the first negotiation made setLocalDescription throw InvalidAccessError about a codec collision for header extension id=1, so the other party silently never received the screen share.  <br>*Cause:* Forcing max-bundle made Chrome reuse header-extension ids across the newly added video m-section, colliding with the existing bundle group.  <br>*Fix:* Use the default (balanced) bundlePolicy and retry a failed offer once; both directions were verified to render after the change.
- [ ] **Pre-creating WebRTC transceivers on both peers doubled every m-section (single-offerer slot trap)** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Each side pre-created mic + share transceivers, so both peers ended with 14 m-sections and remote screen-shares mapped to the wrong slots and never rendered.  <br>*Cause:* JSEP only pairs a remote m-section with a local transceiver created by addTrack, not with ones pre-created on the answerer, so symmetric pre-creation produces mismatched duplicate sections.  <br>*Fix:* Designate a single offerer per connection (the lower peer id) to pre-create the sections; the other side adopts them from the offer, sets direction sendrecv and uses replaceTrack so shares add no new m-sections after the first negotiation.
- [ ] **Presence/chat broadcast echoed to the sender created a ghost self-peer** `logic`
