  *Symptom:* The Room DO broadcast peer-updated/chat/react to every socket including the sender, so a client treated its own peer-updated as a new peer and rendered a duplicate of itself.  <br>*Cause:* The DO broadcast had no sender-exclusion and the client did not filter messages originating from its own id.  <br>*Fix:* broadcast(msg, exceptId) now excludes the sender for peer-updated/chat/react, and the client also drops any message whose from/peer.id equals its own id.
- [ ] **Route prefix /api/me swallowed /api/media so every chat upload returned 401** `logic`
  *Symptom:* The + attachment in chat always failed because uploads to /api/media/* were answered by the auth router with a {signedIn:false} 401.  <br>*Cause:* path.startsWith('/api/me') also matches /api/media/*, routing media requests into the account/auth handler.  <br>*Fix:* Match /api/me exactly (or with a trailing slash) before dispatching; a test then proved a 201 on upload.
- [ ] **SPA HTML cached by the browser meant new deploys kept running the old app.js** `logic`
  *Symptom:* A fixed app.js never reached users and the ?v= bump appeared to do nothing, because the browser heuristically cached the /r/<room> HTML.  <br>*Cause:* Workers Static Assets served index.html with no cache-control, so the HTML was cached and the versioned asset reference was never re-read.  <br>*Fix:* The Worker now sets cache-control: no-cache on text/html responses so HTML revalidates each load while versioned assets stay cached.
- [ ] **Composited recording stalled to ~1 fps when the tab was in the background** `perf`
  *Symptom:* The composited recording dropped to roughly 1 fps whenever the tab was hidden.  <br>*Cause:* The compositor loop was driven by a page setTimeout, which Chrome throttles in hidden tabs.  <br>*Fix:* Tick the compositor from a dedicated (non-throttled) Web Worker, which keeps 15 fps in the background.
- [ ] **Downloaded .webm recording has no duration header (shows infinite length until seeked)** `other`
  *Symptom:* Players show the downloaded recording as having infinite/unknown duration and the scrubber is wrong until the file is seeked or re-muxed.  <br>*Cause:* MediaRecorder writes a live stream with no EBML Duration element, so the container never carries a total length.  <br>*Fix:* OPEN: needs an EBML duration patch (fix-webm-duration) applied to the blob on download, or a server-side remux.

## CD Enterprises  ·  8 fixed

- [ ] **Enquiry/contact form collects personal data with no published privacy policy** `privacy`
  *Symptom:* The site gathers personal-data fields (name, email, phone via the enquiry/contact flow) but ships no privacy policy page.  <br>*Cause:* No policy names what is collected, why, how long it is kept, or how to request deletion — a gap under UK/EU GDPR for a business-facing site.  <br>*Fix:* Publish a short privacy policy covering the enquiry data and link it from the contact form and footer.
- [ ] **Dates built with toISOString() can be off by a day in the user's timezone** `logic`
  *Symptom:* new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10) and recon's fetchedAt record the UTC date, which can land on the wrong calendar day for a Trinidad/UK-local user near midnight.  <br>*Cause:* toISOString() always serialises in UTC, so deriving a local 'today' from it shifts a day across timezone boundaries.  <br>*Fix:* Compute the date in the intended timezone (Intl.DateTimeFormat with an explicit timeZone, or local getFullYear/getMonth/getDate) rather than slicing the UTC ISO string.
- [ ] **stale-while-revalidate on HTML served yesterday's shell and hid a fresh deploy for a whole load** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Pages were sent with cache-control: max-age=0, stale-while-revalidate=86400, so a returning browser showed the previous day's page (older versioned script, five logo looks instead of six) and only revalidated in the background.  <br>*Cause:* The SWR policy was applied to the HTML document itself, which names the versioned asset URLs, so a stale shell pointed visitors at stale scripts.  <br>*Fix:* Pages are now cache-control: no-cache (always revalidate); SWR is kept only for the versioned, immutable assets and never for the HTML that names those versions.
- [ ] **Workers static assets ignore Range requests, so a looping hero video never restarts in Safari** `logic`
  *Symptom:* The muted looping hero film played once in Safari and froze, while Chrome looped because it buffered the whole ~4.6MB file; a bytes=0-99 request got back 200 with the full body and no accept-ranges.  <br>*Cause:* Cloudflare Workers Static Assets (env.ASSETS.fetch) returns the entire file with status 200 and no accept-ranges, and Safari will not seek — therefore will not loop — a source whose server ignores byte ranges.  <br>*Fix:* The Worker now reads .mp4 assets into memory and answers Range with 206 + content-range + accept-ranges: bytes (416 when unsatisfiable); the film was moved to a fresh query-string URL because the edge had cached the old rangeless 200 for a year (immutable).
- [ ] **Logo/home link focus outline removed with outline:none and no visible replacement** `a11y`
  *Symptom:* To hide the browser focus rectangle around the logo after a click, outline:none;box-shadow:none was applied to .brand:focus and :focus-visible, removing the keyboard focus indicator from the logo link entirely.  <br>*Cause:* The look-switch logo is a focusable link; suppressing even :focus-visible leaves keyboard users with no visible focus state on the home/look-switch control.  <br>*Fix:* Restore a visible :focus-visible indicator (a custom ring) rather than outline:none; only the mouse-click rectangle needed suppressing, which :focus-visible already excludes.
- [ ] **Muted hero film freezes after the browser pauses it in a hidden tab** `ui`
  *Symptom:* After a tab was backgrounded, the browser paused the muted autoplay hero film and it sat frozen when the tab returned to the foreground.  <br>*Cause:* Browsers throttle/pause muted autoplay video in hidden tabs and do not always resume it on return, so a decorative looping hero stalls.  <br>*Fix:* Add a visibilitychange handler that resumes playback when the tab becomes visible (unless the visitor explicitly paused), plus an 'ended' -> replay fallback.
- [ ] **Translucent white glass nav over a video hero becomes unreadable on bright frames** `ui`
  *Symptom:* A white glass bar floating over the Circuit film hero lost legibility whenever the clip showed a bright-sky frame.  <br>*Cause:* A light translucent backdrop relies on the content behind it staying dark, which a moving video cannot guarantee frame to frame.  <br>*Fix:* Switch to dark glass at ~30% opacity (blur 18, saturate 1.7, white hairline) so the wordmark stays readable over every frame of the film.
- [ ] **Visually-hidden label class `.vh` used in markup but never defined in CSS** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The stock search's screen-reader-only <label class="vh"> rendered visibly as a grid column and pushed the microphone button onto a second row in every Atelier-based look (Atelier, Night, Glasshouse).  <br>*Cause:* The markup relied on a `.vh` visually-hidden utility class that was never defined in any stylesheet, so the label laid out as normal content.  <br>*Fix:* Define `.vh` with the standard 1px-clip pattern in the base stylesheet, and grep that every utility class used in markup actually exists in CSS.

## Prompt Vault  ·  8 fixed

- [ ] **Beauty-mark descriptor produced a lip piercing** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Subject prompts rendered an unwanted lip piercing.  <br>*Cause:* The phrase 'beauty mark above the left lip' near the mouth was interpreted as a piercing.  <br>*Fix:* Changed subject descriptors to freckles/cheekbones/dimples, never a mark near the lips.
- [ ] **Ebook hyperlinks not rendered inline** `logic`
  *Symptom:* PDF link annotations weren't appearing, undercutting the 'fully hyperlinked' claim.  <br>*Cause:* reportlab requires links to be drawn inline, which the generator wasn't doing.  <br>*Fix:* Drew the links inline so the ebook is fully hyperlinked (27,993 link annotations verified).
- [ ] **Mirror-reversed, garbled on-image timestamp** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Generated mirror-selfie images produced a flipped, broken timestamp.  <br>*Cause:* Prompts let text render inside the mirror, so it came out reversed.  <br>*Fix:* Added a text-overlay clause telling the model to render on-image text as a flat, upright overlay, never inside the mirror.
- [ ] **Prompts produced generic, non-model subjects** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Users got a generic subject instead of a model-grade one.  <br>*Cause:* Only 3 of 1,480 mega prompts demanded a beautiful/model-grade subject.  <br>*Fix:* Added a subject-lock/beauty-floor transform across all mega prompts.
- [ ] **Search returned zero results** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Search returned 0 results despite the 'find anything you've ever written' claim.  <br>*Cause:* Notes, Books and Content were added without extending the search index.  <br>*Fix:* Extended the search index to include the new content types.
- [ ] **Text-fix clause injected inside the --oref placeholder** `logic`
  *Symptom:* The orientation-fix clause landed inside the [your model … --oref …] placeholder, breaking it.  <br>*Cause:* The build transform treated --oref as a trailing parameter when inserting the clause.  <br>*Fix:* Placed the orientation-fix clause at the end so the placeholder stays intact.
- [ ] **Transparent card background over content** `ui`
  *Symptom:* A card used a transparent background causing a visual glitch.  <br>*Cause:* The background was not opaque/theme-aware.  <br>*Fix:* Switched it to an opaque, theme-aware background.
- [ ] **Incorrect flat '696 pages' label** `other`
  *Symptom:* All files were labeled '696 pages' but the 2028 files are actually 697-701 pages.  <br>*Cause:* A hardcoded page count ignored leap-year and week-alignment variation.  <br>*Fix:* Corrected the per-year page-count labels.

## Divorce Rates Atlas  ·  7 fixed

- [ ] **No Content-Security-Policy on served HTML while loading D3/topojson from a CDN** `security`
  *Symptom:* The served index.html carries no CSP, so any injected markup/script would execute unrestricted, and the page already loads D3/topojson from jsdelivr with no source allowlist.  <br>*Cause:* Workers Static Assets serves the HTML with no CSP meta tag or response header.  <br>*Fix:* Add a Content-Security-Policy (meta tag or _headers/response header) that whitelists self plus the jsdelivr CDN to shrink the XSS blast radius.
- [ ] **UK divorce-to-marriage ratio showed an impossible 115% from a corrupted source marriage figure** `data-loss`
  *Symptom:* The UK topped the rankings at 115% divorces-per-100-marriages, an impossible value, when the true ratio is ~41%.  <br>*Cause:* The UN DYB / Wikipedia cross-section row for the UK carried a corrupted marriage rate of 1.3 (should be ~3.4 per 1,000), and that compiled ratio was allowed to override the authoritative Eurostat series.  <br>*Fix:* Only let a compiled cross-section ratio override an authoritative Eurostat/OECD series when it is plausible (<=100%), dropping the corrupted point; UK now shows Eurostat's 41.2%.
- [ ] **Country-name .find() result dereferenced without a null guard in the data merge** `crash`
  *Symptom:* A build-time merge dereferences the result of iso.find(...) directly; an unmatched country name yields undefined and throws 'cannot read property of undefined', aborting data generation.  <br>*Cause:* const hit = iso.find(r => ...) is used without checking that a match was found before accessing its properties.  <br>*Fix:* Guard the result (if (!hit) continue / skip) before dereferencing it.
- [ ] **"Latest" value picked a series' last year instead of the genuinely most recent value across sources** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Russia showed 4.7 (2013) and China 1.8 (2012) as 'latest' even though more recent cross-section values existed (Russia 3.9/2020, China 2.5/2024).  <br>*Cause:* The merge took the last year of a single agency series as 'latest' rather than comparing years across all merged sources.  <br>*Fix:* Select the most recent value across Eurostat/OWID/UN cross-section sources by year, so 'latest' means genuinely latest.
- [ ] **Six search/slider inputs have no label and the focus ring is removed with no :focus-visible replacement** `a11y`
  *Symptom:* Screen-reader users hit unlabeled fields (#search, #yrSlider, #yrSlider2, #rankFilter, #cmpSearch, #expSearch) and keyboard users get no visible focus indicator.  <br>*Cause:* The inputs rely on placeholder text instead of a <label for> or aria-label, and an outline:none reset at line 53 has no matching :focus-visible style (WCAG 2.2 fail).  <br>*Fix:* Add an id + <label for> or aria-label to each input, and pair the outline reset with a high-contrast :focus-visible rule.
- [ ] **D3 map zoom hijacked the page's mouse-wheel scroll (choropleth scroll trap)** `ui`
  *Symptom:* Scrolling the wheel while the cursor was over the choropleth map zoomed the map instead of scrolling the page, so the user got stuck and could not scroll past the map.  <br>*Cause:* d3.zoom() binds a wheel.zoom listener to the SVG by default, capturing every wheel event over the map.  <br>*Fix:* Detach the wheel handler with svg.call(zoom).on('wheel.zoom', null) so the map pans by drag and the +/- buttons only, leaving page scroll intact.
- [ ] **Duplicate DOM id "expChart" in markup wires handlers to the wrong element** `ui`
  *Symptom:* id="expChart" appears twice (lines 309 and 721), so getElementById resolves to the first match and the country-explorer chart can render to or bind the wrong node.  <br>*Cause:* Two elements were given the same id, which is invalid HTML and silently ambiguous for getElementById/querySelector.  <br>*Fix:* Make the ids unique (e.g. rename the second to expChart2) and point each handler at the correct node.

## Hopefil World  ·  7 fixed

- [ ] **Unauthenticated /api/start fetches a user-supplied URL server-side and writes to D1 with no visible rate limit** `security`
  *Symptom:* Any visitor can make the Worker fetch an arbitrary URL and create rows, so the endpoint can be used to probe internal or third-party targets or to fill the database.  <br>*Cause:* The public front door was built for zero friction and only an SSRF guard was added during the session.  <br>*Fix:* Keep the SSRF allowlist and add per-IP rate limiting, a response size cap, a body timeout and a daily write cap.
- [ ] **Primary CTA white on #6B7BFF is 3.56:1 and the site had no skip link, skipped headings and two sub-4.5:1 text tiers** `a11y`
  *Symptom:* The deep a11y pass failed the primary button (3.56:1), found no skip link on 7 of 7 routes, heading level skips, a code comment at 4.41:1 and the /start https:// prefix at 4.25:1.  <br>*Cause:* Accent colour was chosen for the dark surface only and body-tier colours were never measured against every elevation surface.  <br>*Fix:* Darken the CTA surface to 4.5:1, add a skip link to the shared layout, fix heading order, and lift the two text tiers.
- [ ] **curl POST with the response discarded masked a missing endpoint and left the board at 0/38** `observability`
  *Symptom:* A batch of POSTs to a non-existent route all looked successful because output was thrown away, so the live board never advanced.  <br>*Cause:* The script used curl -s -o /dev/null with no status check.  <br>*Fix:* Print the HTTP status for every call (-w with the code) and stop on the first non-2xx.
- [ ] **a11y.mjs and verify.mjs read the same routes.json with incompatible schemas** `testing`
  *Symptom:* One harness silently audited an empty or wrong route list while the other worked, so a clean run proved nothing.  <br>*Cause:* Two scripts assumed different shapes for one shared config file and neither validated it.  <br>*Fix:* Validate the shared routes file on load (fail loudly on an unexpected shape) or give each harness its own file.
- [ ] **Measure script read font-size from one element and line-height from another, reporting a phantom body line-height gap** `testing`
  *Symptom:* The audit reported a body line-height ratio of 2.21 that did not exist on the page.  <br>*Cause:* measure.mjs sampled different elements for the two values and divided one by the other.  <br>*Fix:* Read both computed values from the same node and print the element used with each number.
- [ ] **Grid item with min-width:auto holding a long code line pushed the page 608px wider than a 390px phone** `ui`
  *Symptom:* At 390px the page scrolled horizontally by about 608px.  <br>*Cause:* A grid child containing an unbreakable code line kept the default min-width:auto and refused to shrink below its content.  <br>*Fix:* Set min-width:0 on the grid item and overflow-x:auto on the code block instead of hiding overflow on the page.
- [ ] **Sticky side rail collapsed inside a grid with align-items:start, and a ul-only list reset left ordered-list numbers missing** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The sticky rail had no height to stick within, and numbered steps rendered without their markers.  <br>*Cause:* align-items:start gave the rail only its content height, so sticky had nothing to travel through; the list reset targeted ul and ol markers were left with list-style:none from a broader rule.  <br>*Fix:* Use align-self:start on the rail with the grid stretching, and reset list styles per list type.

## Joy  ·  7 fixed

- [ ] **Mail session cookie is an unsalted SHA-256 of the 6-digit PIN: identical on every login, 30 days, no logout, and recoverable offline from the cookie value** `security`
  *Symptom:* POST /api/login set cm_auth=<sha256('joy:'+code)> and returned the same 64-hex value on every login, with and without 'remember me'; the value was printed in the session transcript, the PIN is compared with !==, and there is no logout route (the HttpOnly cookie could not even be cleared to test the lock screen)  <br>*Cause:* The cookie is derived from the secret instead of being a random per-session token. The login code was also downgraded from a random 12-hex-digit code (about 48 bits) to a 6-digit PIN (about 20 bits) to fit the PIN pad, so the 1,000,000 possible hashes can be tried offline in under a second and rotating the PIN is the only revocation  <br>*Fix:* Open: issue a random session id stored server-side (or HMAC of an expiry with a separate JOY_SECRET), add /api/logout that clears it, compare the PIN in constant time, and keep the login throttle
- [ ] **Outbound SEND binding is deployed unrestricted and the client chooses the From address** `security`
  *Symptom:* wrangler shows env.SEND (unrestricted) and /api/send takes {from,to,subject,body} from the request, so any signed-in or script-injected session can send DKIM-signed mail as any address on the domain, and as any other verified sender  <br>*Cause:* The send_email binding was added with no allowed_sender_addresses or destination limit, and the Worker does not check that from is a pinned address  <br>*Fix:* Open: set allowed_sender_addresses in wrangler.jsonc to the pinned addresses, validate from against the addresses table, and rate-limit /api/send
- [ ] **Joy Cloud puts personal data in a URL query string** `privacy`
  *Symptom:* Personal data is passed in a URL query string, where it leaks via browser history, Referer headers and server/access logs.  <br>*Cause:* PII placed as a query param instead of a POST body / opaque id.  <br>*Fix:* Move PII out of the URL into a POST body or an opaque token; never put personal data in query strings.
- [ ] **File-request links rejected any file over 95 MB because the whole upload went through one Worker request** `logic`
  *Symptom:* A sender dropping a 100 MB video on a request link failed; the sender page promised 'up to 95 MB each' while the drive itself accepted multi-GB files.  <br>*Cause:* The share Worker forwarded the file to the drive in a single PUT, so Workers' request-body limit capped it; the drive's multipart path was not reachable from the share Worker.  <br>*Fix:* Added /api/request-mpu/* routes on the drive Worker reached with a ticket scoped to that one request job; the sender page splits the file into pieces with per-piece retry and progress; a 100 MB file landed in four pieces on the live site, folder chosen server-side.
- [ ] **Owner previews of their own share link were counted as recipient views and downloads (and could trigger a receipt)** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Opening a Cloud share link as the owner incremented views/downloads and wrote a link-activity row, so the activity list and the first-download receipt could not be trusted.  <br>*Cause:* The share Worker treated every GET of /s/:token and /f/:id as a recipient hit; there was no way to mark an owner preview.  <br>*Fix:* Owner preview requests carry preview=1 (propagated through file, download and zip URLs in share.js); the share Worker counts nothing and records no event for them; verified views/downloads/cloud_link_events unchanged after a preview.
- [ ] **Completion message called Joy a complete $0 email platform while the research had found Email Sending is a beta that is transactional-only by policy** `claims`
  *Symptom:* The research lane stated Cloudflare Email Sending is public beta, transactional-only by its FAQ, on shared IPs with an unpublished adaptive daily quota, so customer-composed mailbox traffic should go through SES; the final message, the Handbook and the Dossier then described person-to-person sending from any address as live and finished, without that caveat  <br>*Cause:* The blocker was recorded in the research and in the agent's own notes ('gray area') but never carried into the user-facing status, the launch guide or the cost line  <br>*Fix:* Open: add the policy and quota caveat to the Handbook send section and the status copy, state the real monthly cost including the Workers plan, and note the SES fallback if sending is throttled or the beta changes
- [ ] **Shared links rows clipped their status pills on medium-width windows** `ui`
  *Symptom:* On medium viewports the Shared links list cut off the Last opened / password / expiry pills at the right edge.  <br>*Cause:* The row used a fixed single-line flex layout with nowrap pills that did not wrap or shrink.  <br>*Fix:* Rows now wrap their pill group on medium widths (commit 'shared-links rows wrap on medium widths') and the layout was re-checked live.

## Ever After  ·  6 fixed

- [ ] **Service worker pins returning users to old build** `sync`
  *Symptom:* Returning visitors saw stale builds — old splash rings, white space under content, and a mis-sized rainbow ring.  <br>*Cause:* The root `/` navigation was served cache-first, pinning users to a stale index.html.  <br>*Fix:* Made navigations network-first and bumped the cache version to purge stale caches; the identical bug was also fixed in Planner Studio.
- [ ] **Join boot race gives joiners the wrong cloud key** `race`
  *Symptom:* Users joining a shared space were given their own cloud key and shown the wrong 'your key' popup.  <br>*Cause:* Two boot-time races in the share/join flow.  <br>*Fix:* Fixed the boot races so joiners use the correct shared key and popup.
- [ ] **Wedding voice dictation dumped every spoken item into Guests** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Speaking a mixed rundown (budget, vendor, song, gift, to-do) routed everything into the Guests list  <br>*Cause:* The wedding dictation parser greedily consumed the whole transcript instead of splitting on command/section boundaries like the planner's parser  <br>*Fix:* Rewrote it to split on command boundaries so items route to guests/budget/vendors/tasks/songs/gifts correctly
- [ ] **Choose-a-photo box shows literal ${imgIconSVG(34)}** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The photo-picker box displayed the literal text `${imgIconSVG(34)}` (plus a fallback clock glyph) instead of an image icon.  <br>*Cause:* `imgIconSVG(34)` was inside a single-quoted string (wed-data.js:15), so it wasn't evaluated.  <br>*Fix:* Broke out of the single-quoted string to call imgIconSVG so a real image icon renders.
- [ ] **tvPhoto icon printed as literal text** `ui`
  *Symptom:* `${IMG_ICON(32)}` showed as literal text in tvPhoto instead of the icon.  <br>*Cause:* The placeholder sat inside a single-quoted string, so it wasn't interpolated.  <br>*Fix:* Built the string with concatenation so IMG_ICON is actually called.
- [ ] **Corrupted emoji entry** `other`
  *Symptom:* One corrupted emoji entry had slipped into the data.  <br>*Cause:* Malformed emoji entry.  <br>*Fix:* Corrected the emoji entry.

## Hallalu Bookings  ·  6 fixed

- [ ] **Public unthrottled billable /api/chat with client-supplied context (denial-of-wallet and prompt injection surface)** `security`
  *Symptom:* Anyone can POST to the booking page chat endpoint without auth or limits; each call runs a Workers AI 70B model billed to the owner, and the page context is accepted from the client.  <br>*Cause:* No per-IP/per-page rate limit, no body size cap, and the grounding context is taken from the request body instead of being rebuilt server-side from the booking page record.  <br>*Fix:* Rate limit (per IP and per page, with a daily ceiling), cap message/context size, rebuild grounding context server-side from the page id, and return the FAQ fallback when over budget.
- [ ] **Mixing ?? with && without parentheses is a SyntaxError in esbuild/Workers** `crash`
  *Symptom:* wrangler deploy failed to bundle the Worker with a cryptic syntax error on a chat-context line.  <br>*Cause:* JavaScript forbids mixing ?? with || or && without parentheses; the expression a ?? b && c was written unparenthesised.  <br>*Fix:* Parenthesise ((a ?? b) && c). Run the bundler locally before deploy.
- [ ] **Concurrent bookings claim the same slot (double-booking race)** `race`
  *Symptom:* Two visitors open the same booking page, both see a slot as free, and both submit within seconds — the app accepts both and the owner is double-booked.  <br>*Cause:* Availability is read once into the client and the final insert does not re-check for a conflicting booking inside the same transaction. No unique constraint on (resource, start_time) and no server-side conflict guard, so simultaneous writes both succeed.  <br>*Fix:* Make the slot the source of truth: add a DB unique constraint / conditional insert on (owner_or_resource, start, end) so the second write fails, re-validate availability server-side at submit time, and on conflict return a friendly 'just taken — pick another time'. Optionally hold a short soft-lock while the visitor fills the form.
- [ ] **Availability rendered in owner's timezone, not the visitor's** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Slots show the business's local hours to every visitor regardless of where they are, so an out-of-area client books '2pm' meaning their own 2pm and shows up at the wrong hour.  <br>*Cause:* Times are formatted from stored local/naive datetimes without converting to the visitor's detected timezone, and the page never states which timezone the times are in.  <br>*Fix:* Store all slots in UTC, detect the visitor's timezone (Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone), render every time converted to it, always print an explicit timezone label plus a picker to override, and spell out both parties' timezones in the confirmation.
- [ ] **Index-keyed edit persistence misaligned after reorder/delete: edits saved onto the wrong service/slot** `logic`
  *Symptom:* After removing or reordering an item, inline edits were saved against the wrong row.  <br>*Cause:* Edit handlers used the array index captured at render time rather than a stable id.  <br>*Fix:* Key every editable row by id and look it up at save time.
- [ ] **Orphaned dead whitespace in FAQ + Policies section** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The FAQ and Policies cards sat only in the right column while the left column ended at the testimonials card, leaving a dead bottom-left quadrant.  <br>*Cause:* The section used a 5fr/7fr split where the short left column (About + Testimonials) ended early while the long right column (Gallery + FAQ + Policies) ran on, orphaning the bottom-left quadrant.  <br>*Fix:* Restructured the section into a balanced editorial layout — a full-width gallery strip then a 2x2 equal-height card grid (About / Testimonials / FAQ / Policies) so rows align and nothing is orphaned.

## Wedding Planner  ·  6 fixed

- [ ] **openpyxl StyleProxy crash on font copy** `crash`
  *Symptom:* xlsx generation failed when copying fonts.  <br>*Cause:* Assigning `.font = ws[cell].font` uses an unhashable StyleProxy.  <br>*Fix:* Apply named fonts conditionally instead of copying the StyleProxy.
- [ ] **Analysis donut rendered incorrectly** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The analysis donut chart displayed wrong.  <br>*Fix:* Fixed the donut rendering.
- [ ] **Duplicated block in wedding-party view** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The wedding party showed a duplicated half-block.  <br>*Cause:* Duplicated code lines in wed.js (caught by node --check).  <br>*Fix:* Removed the duplicate lines and converted the cramped table to readable cards + editor.
- [ ] **Filler images not loading (naturalWidth 0)** `ui`
  *Symptom:* Filler/Unsplash images failed to load (naturalWidth 0).  <br>*Cause:* Image URL handling prevented the images from loading.  <br>*Fix:* Store the image URL directly and let the `<img>` element load it.
- [ ] **Seating flow dead-ends after adding a table** `ui`
  *Symptom:* Adding a table left an empty table with no way forward.  <br>*Cause:* There was no way to seat guests into a newly added table.  <br>*Fix:* Added capacity meters, per-table '+ Seat a guest here' dropdowns, meal tallies, auto-seat and a 'still to seat' pool.
- [ ] **Nested git repo leaked into monorepo commit** `other`
  *Symptom:* A wedding-planner gitlink was committed into the BUDGETLEVELUP monorepo.  <br>*Cause:* wedding-planner has its own repo and was added as a gitlink.  <br>*Fix:* Removed the leaked gitlink from the monorepo commit.

## ABS  ·  5 fixed

- [ ] **Admin API shipped in 'open bootstrap' mode: unauthenticated until an admin user exists, including product CRUD and billable AI assist** `security`
  *Symptom:* On production, GET /api/admin/products and the AI assist endpoint answered without any credentials while no admin account existed.  <br>*Cause:* The admin guard computed openMode = no admin user exists and skipped the role check when true, so a fresh deployment is fully open to anyone who finds the path (including delete and Workers AI spend).  <br>*Fix:* Not fixed in the session. Fix: require a deploy-time secret or one-time claim token to create the first admin, never branch the auth check on 'no admin yet', and throttle the AI assist endpoint.
- [ ] **A research 'block for v1' recommendation was hard-coded as a legal block, excluding cosmetics/personal care** `logic`
  *Symptom:* The catalogue refused the biggest TikTok Shop category and the user's explicit 'personal hygiene' scope although selling them only needs a gate.  <br>*Cause:* compliance.js treated a lane's risk-appetite suggestion as a regulatory prohibition and, separately, the same session had acted on a critique (Lane G's CAC figure) without verifying the critic; verified data showed a UK CPSR starts around 55 pounds, most lines finish under 500 and a seller can be their own Responsible Person.  <br>*Fix:* Moved cosmetics and installed batteries from blocked to gated (named Responsible Person, CPSR reference, SCPN/CPNP, ingredient list; UN38.3 for batteries) while keeping genuinely licensed categories blocked; gate re-tested at 47 rules (19 clean, 13 gated, 15 blocked).
- [ ] **Voice intent parser matched substrings: 'home' in 'trending home decor' went to the homepage and 'sell' fired inside 'best selling'** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Spoken commands navigated to the wrong place; 'add this to my basket' only opened the basket.  <br>*Cause:* Intent keywords were checked as plain substrings in a fixed order, and navigation words were tested before search/filter phrases.  <br>*Fix:* Word-boundary matching, search/filter phrases checked before navigation words, and an add-to-basket intent that acts; testing before wiring found five defects and the parser reached 17/17 intents ('show me trending home decor under $50' now yields category, price cap and sort).
- [ ] **Denied microphone permission closed the voice panel, hiding both the error and the command list** `ui`
  *Symptom:* Pressing V with the mic blocked flashed a toast and closed the panel, leaving the user with no explanation or help.  <br>*Cause:* The recogniser onError handler always removed the panel's 'on' class.  <br>*Fix:* For not-allowed/service-not-allowed/audio-capture errors the panel stays open with a 'Voice needs microphone access' message above the command list.
- [ ] **Keyword-matched filler photos (loremflickr) ignored the lock param, so unrelated products shared one fallback image** `ui`
  *Symptom:* A vase, a throw and a washi-tape set all showed the same cat-statue photo even though every image URL was unique.  <br>*Cause:* loremflickr returns one generic fallback for obscure keyword combinations and did not honour ?lock=, so distinct URLs still resolved to the same picture.  <br>*Fix:* Switched to per-product deterministic picsum seeds derived from the product id; verified 63 products have 63 distinct first images (duplicates: 0), accepting 'distinct but not subject-matched' over duplicated.

## Listing Lab Pro  ·  5 fixed

- [ ] **SSRF via /api/open and /api/fetch** `security`
  *Symptom:* Server-side fetch endpoints could be pointed at arbitrary internal hosts.  <br>*Cause:* The browser-open/fetch paths had no host allowlist.  <br>*Fix:* Added a host allowlist to /api/open and /api/fetch in worker.js.
- [ ] **XSS in Listing Lab render path** `security`
  *Symptom:* Untrusted content could execute script in Listing Lab.  <br>*Cause:* Unsanitized input rendered into the page.  <br>*Fix:* Applied output sanitization to close the XSS vector.
- [ ] **AI misattributed quotes between listings** `logic`
  *Symptom:* The analyzer attributed one listing's quote to a different listing.  <br>*Cause:* The prompt allowed cross-listing bleed when run on many listings.  <br>*Fix:* Tightened the prompt and re-ran 10 listings to confirm correct attribution.
- [ ] **Digit confusion when reading listing images** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Digits were misread from images.  <br>*Cause:* No image preprocessing before digit recognition.  <br>*Fix:* Added proper image preprocessing and generalized the digit-confusion fix.
- [ ] **Scraper pulled related-item ads and foreign shops** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Analyses were polluted by related-item ads and other shops' listings, which would have produced wrong results.  <br>*Cause:* The scraper didn't filter related-item ads or restrict to the listing's own shop.  <br>*Fix:* Filter out related-item ads and keep only the dominant shop, detected via the shop ID embedded in image URLs.

## Analysis  ·  4 fixed

- [ ] **Bulk edit missed 16 of 35 analyses: the ANALYSES array mixes single-quoted JS and double-quoted JSON entry styles** `logic`
  *Symptom:* A first pass baked and wired hero images into 19 analyses and the grep count looked complete (19/19), but the live DOM showed 35 cards with 16 still lacking a thumbnail.  <br>*Cause:* worker.js ANALYSES holds two object styles: hand-written JS entries (slug: 'x') and JSON-style entries appended by publish scripts ("slug": "x"). The discovery regex and the grep -c anchored on one style only, so the inventory was silently partial.  <br>*Fix:* Counted both styles (grep -c '"slug":'), baked and wired the second set anchored after each "slug": line with matching quoting, node --check, then verified against rendered output (querySelectorAll count: 35/35 thumbs, 0 broken) instead of trusting the grep count.
- [ ] **Interactive cost-compare artifact: the cost-per-prompt slider was mis-scaled 10x against its model defaults** `logic`
  *Symptom:* In the first Max-vs-API-credits calculator the per-prompt slider value disagreed with each model's default cost by a factor of ten, so the 'credits last N months' and crossover figures were wrong.  <br>*Cause:* The slider stored dollars while the defaults were in cents (cpp/cps tables), and the readout divided by a different scale; no test replayed the real slider scaling.  <br>*Fix:* Rewrote the calculator cents-based with a full model roster and by-hand vs at-volume modes, then replayed the actual slider scaling in Node for both modes (e.g. 10 prompts/day Sonnet = $13/mo; 1,000 sites on Haiku $500 to Fable $9,000) and republished.
- [ ] **Archived Workers AI cost report says Cloudflare now offers four video models, though Cloudflare's own pricing page lists none** `claims`
  *Symptom:* The cloudflare-workers-ai-image-video-costs report and its companion artifact state that Workers AI does text and image to video (LTX-2.5 Fast, Hailuo-2.3, Vidu Q3 Pro, Pruna avatar) with market rates of about $0.08 to $0.30 per second; two later fetches of the primary pricing page found no video rows, and the Joy Day audit removed the same figures as invented  <br>*Cause:* Availability and per-second prices came from search snippets and the video vendors' own sites, not Cloudflare's docs, and were labelled estimates only on the cost, not on the availability claim  <br>*Fix:* Open: append a correction report (the archive is append-only) stating availability is unconfirmed and no Workers AI video price is published; add a rule that any 'platform X offers Y' claim needs the platform's own page
- [ ] **Downloaded report HTML kept root-relative /img/ paths so images broke when opened off the origin** `ui`
  *Symptom:* A saved report opened from disk or email showed broken images.  <br>*Cause:* The download branch served the same HTML as the web page, with same-origin relative image URLs.  <br>*Fix:* Rewrite /img/ to absolute https URLs in the ?download=1 response (done).

## BirthdayTreat  ·  4 fixed

- [ ] **Text-wish 'note' tab posted kind 'note' but the API only accepts 'text': silent 400, wish never saved** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Friends typing a written wish saw it appear to submit, but nothing was stored.  <br>*Cause:* Client note tab sent kind:'note' while the Worker validates kind against an allowlist that has 'text'; the 400 was not surfaced in the UI.  <br>*Fix:* Map tab to kind 'text' on the client, share the kind enum between client and server tests, and always surface non-2xx responses to the user.
- [ ] **An empty/failed wish card broke the CSS multi-column masonry layout** `ui`
  *Symptom:* One card with no media collapsed or spanned columns, pushing the rest of the wall out of alignment.  <br>*Cause:* Cards without content had zero height and the column-span/break rules did not apply; missing break-inside:avoid on the card.  <br>*Fix:* Skip rendering empty cards, set break-inside:avoid, and give every card a minimum height.
- [ ] **Flagship 'add your wish' action was below the fold on mobile** `undefined`
  *Symptom:* First-time visitors to a birthday link did not see how to post a wish without scrolling.  <br>*Cause:* Countdown/header hero consumed the full viewport.  <br>*Fix:* Pin the primary CTA in the first viewport (sticky bar); measure at 375x667.
- [ ] **Recorded video/voice MIME with ';codecs=' parameter mapped to '.bin' extension** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Recorded wishes were stored in R2 as .bin and would not play inline in Safari/Chrome.  <br>*Cause:* extFor() matched the raw Content-Type including ';codecs=opus' against an exact table, so nothing matched and the default was used.  <br>*Fix:* Strip parameters (split(';')[0].trim().toLowerCase()) before lookup and also set the response Content-Type explicitly on read.

## Claude Code Browser pane  ·  4 fixed

- [ ] **javascript_exec with a top-level return fails with an unhelpful SyntaxError: Illegal return statement** `other`
  *Symptom:* Snippets that end with return value failed immediately; the tool reported only the SyntaxError.  <br>*Cause:* The tool evaluates the text as an expression with REPL semantics, not as a function body.  <br>*Fix:* Write the value as the last expression, or wrap the code in an IIFE; keep this in the Browser verification checklist.
- [ ] **Pane viewport reports 0 width and stale computed styles, so layout measurements are wrong** `other`
  *Symptom:* getComputedStyle and bounding boxes returned zero-width or stale values while the rendered pixels were correct.  <br>*Cause:* The pane page was hidden or not yet laid out when the script ran, so layout reads came from a collapsed viewport.  <br>*Fix:* Front the tab, wait a frame, and trust a screenshot or a re-measured value over a single early reading; resize explicitly before measuring.
- [ ] **preview_start refused a configured port held by another chat's server, and the verification then ran against that other session's process** `other`
  *Symptom:* preview_start name postplan failed with 'Port 8823 is in use by another chat's dev server'; the run carried on by fetching and rendering http://localhost:8823 directly, so the screenshots came from a server it did not start and whose working tree a second session was editing  <br>*Cause:* The launch config pins one port for every chat; the refusal text suggests autoPort or freeing the port, but a quick curl to the same port looks like a success  <br>*Fix:* Set autoPort in launch.json (and drop hard-coded --port flags) for shared projects, or start a private server on a free port and assert its pid before treating a 200 as proof
- [ ] **Screenshots go blank on very tall pages and computer actions time out when the pane is not displayed** `other`
  *Symptom:* Full-page captures of tall pages and captures after scrolling far down returned a blank slice; with the pane hidden, screenshot timed out after 5s and scroll after 30s.  <br>*Cause:* The pane does not composite frames for off-screen or very tall content, and a hidden pane produces no frames at all.  <br>*Fix:* Call tabs_select to front the pane, capture one viewport at a time (or hoist the element to the top / scroll_to a ref), and fall back to DOM reads for proof.

## Joy Day  ·  4 fixed

- [ ] **6-digit PIN gate shipped with no attempt throttle in code or tests** `security`
  *Symptom:* The dashboard holds copies of every generated image behind a 6-digit PIN; the auth assertions in the harness (wrong PIN, tampered cookie, expiry, locked routes) never cover repeated wrong guesses and no limiter appears in the session's code, so the 1,000,000-value space can be walked through POST /auth  <br>*Cause:* The gate was wired to the UI-only auth-gate module with a server session, but the lockout the other apps use (counted failures, 429 after five) was not carried over  <br>*Fix:* Open: count failures per IP and per day in the append-only store, return 429 with a lock window after five, and add a test that the sixth wrong PIN is refused even when the seventh is right
- [ ] **Daily and monthly figures called composed carousel slides generations** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Today read '8 generations' when only 1 image had cost any Neurons; seven composed slides at 0 N were counted as generations in the day, month and lifetime totals  <br>*Cause:* The totals used COUNT(*) over every synced image instead of separating rows that cost Neurons from rows that were only filed  <br>*Fix:* Count generations as neurons > 0 and images as all rows, in the day, month and lifetime queries and in the page copy ('1 generation, of 8 images today'), with tests for both
- [ ] **'Joy Day' collides with Happy Money's JOY mark for personal-finance software** `claims`
  *Symptom:* Joy Day is a personal expenditure/budget dashboard. Happy Money holds UK 'JOY' UK00003310491 in classes 9/36/42, which covers personal-finance, budget and expense-tracking software, live to 2028.  <br>*Cause:* The name was not cleared against classes 9/36/42, and the product sits squarely inside Happy Money's goods.  <br>*Fix:* Open: rename (rated HIGH risk in the clearance lane).
- [ ] **Usage history depends on a manual sync script, and locking the dashboard broke the embeddable badge** `observability`
  *Symptom:* The count is only true as of the last manual run of sync-pixelbake.mjs, nothing schedules it, and /usage.svg moved behind the PIN gate so any pasted badge now shows a 401  <br>*Cause:* Worker-to-Worker fetch on the same account fails with error 1042, so history is copied by a laptop script; the gate was applied to every route except /rates  <br>*Fix:* Open: add a scheduled pull from a route Pixelbake pushes to, or a cron on the machine, and decide per route which data is safe to expose (a percent-only badge token)

## PatentCake  ·  4 fixed

- [ ] **Unauthenticated GET /api/inventions exposes all 24 invention disclosures** `security`
  *Symptom:* curl returned HTTP 200 with count 24 and no auth. Every disclosure, including the 'candidate' inventions and drafted claim language describing the other apps' internals, is publicly readable.  <br>*Cause:* The read API was left public while only the write API is protected by PATENT_KEY. Public disclosure destroys novelty in the UK/EU (Patents Act s.2(2), no general grace period); only the US 12-month grace (35 USC 102(b)(1)(A)) is still running.  <br>*Fix:* Open: put the read routes behind the owner auth or PATENT_KEY now, and treat anything already served as published for prior-art purposes.
- [ ] **Auto-derived slug truncated at 80 characters mid-word on an append-only registry, so it can never be fixed** `logic`
  *Symptom:* Filing the patentscan disclosure created the permanent slug authorship-time-invention-harvester-the-coding-agent-triages-and-files-its-own-i, cut off mid-word. The registry triggers reject UPDATE and DELETE so it cannot be renamed; fixing it means filing a duplicate entry.  <br>*Cause:* The worker builds the slug from the title and slices it to a fixed length with no word-boundary logic, and the CLI neither previews the slug nor requires an explicit one for long titles before an irreversible write.  <br>*Fix:* Truncate at a word boundary and cap the title length used for slugs; make pc.mjs print the final slug and require --slug (or a confirmation) when the derived one is cut. The patentscan skill now passes a short explicit slug.
- [ ] **Sub-agent false negative shipped as 'does not exist': zsh unquoted --include glob and truncated output** `claims`
  *Symptom:* A miner reported a capability absent from the codebase, and the registry marked a disclosure 'not present'; it was in fact in the repo.  <br>*Cause:* grep --include=*.js was unquoted so zsh expanded or failed the glob and searched nothing; the agent also read a truncated listing, then concluded absence.  <br>*Fix:* Quote globs ('--include=*.js') or use ripgrep; treat 'not found' as 'not verified' until a second method confirms; verify the critic, not just the claim. Restored the vision-to-watchlist disclosure.
- [ ] **Legend colours used a CSS custom property that was not defined in that scope** `ui`
  *Symptom:* Status legend dots rendered transparent/black instead of their category colours.  <br>*Cause:* Legend referenced var(--x) names that the stylesheet defined under different names (candidate/defensive/known).  <br>*Fix:* Use the same token names as the stylesheet and a var() fallback; add a check that every var(--*) used is defined.

## Social LevelUp  ·  4 fixed

- [ ] **XSS via paste into contenteditable notes editor** `security`
  *Symptom:* Pasting HTML into the notes editor could inject script.  <br>*Cause:* Contenteditable paste was unsanitized (same class as Trix CVE-2024-53847).  <br>*Fix:* Sanitized pasted content in the notes editor and applied the same fix to the budget app's notes editor.
- [ ] **cur0 is not defined crash on post save** `crash`
  *Symptom:* Saving a post with income entered crashed the app.  <br>*Cause:* An undeclared identifier (cur0) was referenced during post-save.  <br>*Fix:* Guarded with a typeof money0 check so income posts save cleanly.
- [ ] **Older profiles crash on missing state fields** `crash`
  *Symptom:* Profiles created before new features crashed on load.  <br>*Cause:* normalize() did not backfill newly added state fields.  <br>*Fix:* Backfilled the new state fields in normalize() so old profiles load.
- [ ] **To-do done button crash** `crash`
  *Symptom:* Clicking a to-do's done button threw undefined.split.  <br>*Cause:* The attribute was written as data-tododone but read as dataset.todoDone (case mismatch).  <br>*Fix:* Aligned the dataset key naming so the done button reads correctly.

## Capital Signal  ·  3 fixed

- [ ] **LazyVideo removed the video element when autoplay was blocked, and a duplicate HEAD-probe handler aborted the request** `logic`
  *Symptom:* On browsers that refuse autoplay the hero video vanished entirely; the console showed ERR_ABORTED from a second HEAD probe.  <br>*Cause:* play().catch(() => v.remove()) treated a normal autoplay refusal as a load failure, and an older HEAD-probe handler was left in app.js alongside the new one.  <br>*Fix:* Keep the element on play() rejection (show the poster and a play control); verify the source server-side and mark it data-verified; lazy-start with IntersectionObserver; delete the duplicate probe.
- [ ] **Two hero variants in the DOM at once gave the page two h1 elements** `a11y`
  *Symptom:* The V1 and V2 heroes were both rendered with one hidden by CSS, so the a11y audit and assistive tech saw two h1s and duplicate landmarks.  <br>*Cause:* Variant switching only toggled display or visibility; the inactive variant stayed in the accessibility tree.  <br>*Fix:* Mark the inactive variant inert and aria-hidden (or do not render it) and assert exactly one h1 in the accessibility tree.
- [ ] **First git add -A swept 254 evidence files into the repo and inflated .git to 163MB** `other`
  *Symptom:* The initial commit of a new project carried screenshots and competitor captures, making the repository 163MB before anything shipped.  <br>*Cause:* Evidence and scratch folders lived inside the project tree and no .gitignore existed when git add -A ran.  <br>*Fix:* Untrack screenshots and competitors, rewrite the unpushed history, add them to .gitignore; keep evidence in Aprizely shots, not in git.

## GitBagel  ·  3 fixed

- [ ] **esc() HTML helper does not escape quotes → attribute-context XSS** `security`
  *Symptom:* User-controlled data interpolated into an HTML attribute can break out of the attribute and inject markup/script.  <br>*Cause:* The escape helper in public/js/hl.js escapes <>& but not single/double quotes, so it is unsafe in attribute context.  <br>*Fix:* Also escape ' and " (&#39; / &quot;) in esc(), or set values via setAttribute/textContent instead of string-building attributes.
- [ ] **Sessions created before the accounts cookie existed were not folded into the account switcher** `auth`
  *Symptom:* After deploying multi-account switching, signing in again showed only the new account and lost the earlier one.  <br>*Cause:* signin and signup merged the stored accounts list with the new token but ignored the current gb_session cookie.  <br>*Fix:* Fold the current valid session token into the accounts list on every signin and signup (done).
- [ ] **Name violates the Git trademark policy on 'Git' as a syllable or portmanteau** `claims`
  *Symptom:* The register search found no hit, but the Git project's trademark policy bars 'Git' as a syllable or portmanteau in third-party product names (GitHub and GitLab are grandfathered).  <br>*Cause:* Register clearance alone missed a policy-based restriction on the name.  <br>*Fix:* Open: rename, or obtain written permission from the Software Freedom Conservancy.

## Payrails  ·  2 fixed

- [ ] **Rank by value sorts figures that use different measurement bases as if they were comparable** `claims`
  *Symptom:* Re-ranking by value orders Pix (about $6.5T), UPI ($3.5T), MTN MoMo ($500B), M-Pesa ($450B) and Telebirr ($20B) on one scale, although the research itself found MTN MoMo quoted anywhere from roughly $342B to $1.1T depending on basis, and entries mix annual and cumulative values and registered versus active users.  <br>*Cause:* One numeric field per system was chosen from conflicting sources to make the sort work; the basis and the disagreement stay in the notes instead of the data model.  <br>*Fix:* Store value as low, high, basis and year per system, sort on the midpoint with a visible basis badge, and caveat or disable the cross-basis sort.
- [ ] **Undated forward-looking statements (IPO looming, IPO in prep) on a ranking page will go stale** `claims`
  *Symptom:* The ranked board says GCash has a ~$8B IPO looming and Airtel Money has an IPO in prep, with no as-of date, source date or hedge, on a site whose data is dated 2025 to 2026.  <br>*Cause:* News-style future events were copied into evergreen ranking copy; the page has no per-claim as-of date and nothing flags statements that describe the future.  <br>*Fix:* Date-stamp every forward-looking line (as of the month, with the source) or remove it, and add a lint step to the deploy script that flags words such as looming, upcoming, soon, plans to and expected in static copy.

## Remembrance  ·  2 fixed

- [ ] **Inline FALLBACK dataset in index.html drifted from data.json, so a failed fetch showed an older, incomplete record** `sync`
  *Symptom:* The page loads public/data.json at runtime and falls back to a copy of the dataset inlined in index.html. After data.json was rewritten with new faiths, the race and ethnicity lenses, countries and perpetrators, the inline copy still held the earlier faith-only data. Whenever the fetch failed, for example in the local file preview the editor pane opens after each edit, the page silently showed only the original faiths and the lens buttons had no data to switch to. The user reported the lenses as not working.  <br>*Cause:* Two hand-maintained copies of the same data with no build step tying them together; edits were applied to data.json and only sometimes to the FALLBACK constant. The first diagnosis blamed browser cache, which delayed finding this.  <br>*Fix:* Make data.json the single source: inline it into index.html at deploy time with a tiny build script, or drop the fallback and show a visible load-error state. The shipped page now also retries the live /data.json with a CORS header; add a deploy check that asserts FALLBACK equals data.json.
- [ ] **Headline lives-recorded total sums a single reporting-year count with one-time historical event tolls** `claims`
  *Symptom:* The All-history total (about 8.1 million, and about 12 million under the Race lens once the enslavement toll was added) adds the Open Doors latest-year figure (4,849 for the year to Sep 2025) to multi-decade one-time event tolls from sources with very different methods and ranges, and the per-day rate is derived from that same annual figure. The basis of each number (annual rate, one-time estimate, contested range) is visible only per entry.  <br>*Cause:* All records share one events array and the total is a plain sum of the representative value, with no field that marks an entry as an annual rate versus a one-time toll and no range shown on the headline.  <br>*Fix:* Add a basis field (annual, one-time, cumulative) and either exclude annual-rate rows from cumulative totals or show them separately; show the low-to-high range beside the headline total and keep the contested flag visible on the card, not only in About.

## Breadcrumb Admin  ·  1 fixed

- [ ] **Admin surface kept off the user-facing app** `security`
  *Symptom:* Putting admin oversight (which reads the app D1) inside the main app would widen its attack surface.  <br>*Cause:* Admin routes co-located with the user app.  <br>*Fix:* Admin runs as a separate passcode-gated Worker that reads the app D1; nothing admin ships in the user bundle.

## Breadcrumb Plan  ·  1 fixed

- [ ] **Business plan and /deck must not leak on any direct path** `security`
  *Symptom:* A passcode-gated business plan plus /deck could leak via direct asset paths (plan.html.txt, src, deck).  <br>*Cause:* Static assets can be served before the auth gate runs.  <br>*Fix:* Plan and deck live on a separate PLAN_PASS-gated Worker; verified every direct path returns the lock screen.

## Bug Ledger Pitch  ·  1 fixed

- [ ] **Deck chip truncated the name to M...** `ui`
  *Symptom:* A slide chip clipped the label (e.g. M...) instead of showing the full name.  <br>*Cause:* The chip had flex-shrink:0, starving the name of width.  <br>*Fix:* Chip yields width first and titles wrap to two lines; also added reading-progress and tick-off.

## Claude Code  ·  1 fixed

- [ ] **SendUserFile upload of a 2.1MiB screenshot returned 400 to Remote Control viewers** `other`
  *Symptom:* A full-size screenshot could not be delivered to a Remote Control viewer; the upload failed with a 400.  <br>*Cause:* The relay rejects larger attachments and the tool gave no size guidance.  <br>*Fix:* Downscale or JPEG-encode images below the limit before SendUserFile and report the size in the failure.

## Currency Picker  ·  1 fixed

- [ ] **Currency picker logic duplicated per app** `other`
  *Symptom:* The 153-currency parse-search picker was re-implemented in each app, duplicating a whole class of currency bugs.  <br>*Cause:* No shared module for the picker.  <br>*Fix:* Extracted to a standalone dependency-free module (CurrencyPicker.search/open/button) that ships its own sheet and CSS.

## Happy Travel  ·  1 fixed

- [ ] **Recovery keys generated with Math.random (brute-forceable)** `security`
  *Symptom:* Cloud-data recovery keys came from Math.random (~15.5M combos); the key IS the password to a users synced blob, so the space is scannable.  <br>*Cause:* Math.random is not cryptographically secure.  <br>*Fix:* Regenerated recovery keys with crypto.getRandomValues (~39 quadrillion combos). Same fix applied to Hello Baby.

## Kairos  ·  1 fixed

- [ ] **Unsupportable 47% more productive landing claim** `logic`
  *Symptom:* The landing asserted 47% more productive and other stats with no citable source.  <br>*Cause:* Marketing copy used an unverified stat (traces to a non-peer-reviewed internal note).  <br>*Fix:* Cut to verified-only claims; validated the manual-first default against real, still-true competitor gaps.

## Kindly  ·  1 fixed

- [ ] **Opaque gate never left, so Take-me-in did nothing** `ui`
  *Symptom:* The sign-in gate stayed opaque over the booted app and the Take-me-in button appeared dead.  <br>*Cause:* Author .gate{display:grid} beat the UA [hidden]{display:none} rule (author styles win), so setting hidden had no effect.  <br>*Fix:* Global [hidden]{display:none!important} near the CSS reset; pad rebuilt to exactly 5 digits with phone letters.

## PostPlan  ·  1 fixed

- [ ] **Starter posts added after a user's first visit never appear: a single boolean seeded flag gates seeding** `logic`
  *Symptom:* New sample carousels (navy edition, the Bug Ledger, Pixelbake and Aprizely starter sets, versions 4 and 5) did not show for anyone who had already opened PostPlan.  <br>*Cause:* Boot state is { projects: [], calendar: [], seeded: false }; once seeded is true the SAMPLES list is never consulted again.  <br>*Fix:* Seed by id: each starter registers under its id and boot adds any SAMPLES id that is missing from state (commit 'seed starters by id', then versions 4 and 5 registered).

## Sell skill  ·  1 fixed

- [ ] **Playbook cites a 53.1% early-branding figure that could not be found in any source** `claims`
  *Symptom:* When the evidence was refreshed the 53.1% System1 early-branding number in sell-playbook.md could not be located anywhere; agents running /sell would repeat it in paid-ad strategy  <br>*Cause:* A figure was copied into the playbook from a summarising tool's output without opening the primary page and recording a quote and date  <br>*Fix:* Open: remove or replace the number, and give every playbook figure a source URL, read date and a quoted line; mark graded SIGNAL until re-opened

## SkillSea  ·  1 fixed

- [ ] **Searching a hyphenated skill name with a space ('deep standard') returned zero results** `ui`
  *Symptom:* After deep-standard was catalogued, typing 'deep standard' showed 0 cards while 'deep-standard' showed 1; the session dismissed it as expected because skill names have no spaces  <br>*Cause:* The client search matches the query as a literal substring of name, purpose, tag and trigger, with no normalisation of hyphens, underscores and spaces  <br>*Fix:* Open: normalise both the haystack and the query (lowercase, replace - and _ with space, collapse spaces) and match every query word, not the whole string

## Unknown  ·  1 fixed

- [ ] **Screenshot box completely unstyled** `ui`
  *Symptom:* A box looked cheap because it had no styling at all.  <br>*Cause:* The element rendered completely unstyled.  <br>*Fix:* Applied proper styling to the box.
